# Ferva — Agent navigation + verification map

Canonical map for coding, QA, and product agents. **Not** generic product docs.

Use this file to answer, without rediscovering the app:

- Where does the feature live (Swedish UI name + route)?
- How does a user get there?
- Which states, tables, and components matter?
- How do I reproduce and verify a fix?

**Product:** Swedish business app for a one-person trade/service company (carpenter, painter, electrician, plumber, excavation contractor). Live: https://driva-alpha.vercel.app/

**Core chain:** Kund → Uppdrag → Offert (kunden godkänner på länken) → Faktura → Betalning → Bokföring.

**Status vocabulary:** one source — `src/lib/status-labels.ts`. Never show raw enums (`skickad`, `POSTED`, `pending`) as primary UI. The accept method is **not** a status — and the customer accept is **never** called BankID, e-legitimation or avancerad underskrift (see `offer.accept_simple`).

**Unknowns** are marked `UNKNOWN`. Facts below are from code + the live demo on 2026-09-01 unless noted. Shared address autocomplete ([PR #79](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/79), hardened in [PR #94](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/94): loader timeout, street-only types, Enter-while-searching) verified against main code 2026-09-04. Receipt file storage ([PR #77](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/77)) verified against main code and the live demo 2026-09-04. Receipt upload hardening for **Lägg till kvitto** ([PR #90](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/90): `FormData` transport, store-before-mutate, demo never touches the bucket, bucket-reject inline fallback) verified against main code 2026-09-06; [PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120) (merge `3633c21`) moved the two remaining callers - the standalone Inbox upload (at the time **Ladda upp kvitto** without a purchase / Inbox **Lägg till dokument**) and the manual verifikat underlag - onto the same `FormData` path, removed the data-URL exports and made demo tenants skip the `inbox_attachments` bucket, verified against main code 2026-09-07. [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) (merge `98fff2a`) replaced the document file pickers (receipts, Inbox, verifikat underlag, SIE, import center, prisfil, support attachment) with the shared `FileDropzone` (`src/components/file-dropzone.tsx`: drag/drop, click, Enter/Space, paste; variants `landing` / `inline` / `compact`; the logo / site `ImageDropzone` only got the same look, same compression) and main commit `90d43e3` put the `ReceiptUpload` queue (`src/components/receipt-upload.tsx`: several files at once, one row per file with thumbnail + status, *Fota kvitto* on touch screens, page-wide Ctrl/Cmd+V) on top of it for `/inbox` and Ekonomi → Utgifter - the button names **Lägg till dokument**, **Ladda upp kvitto** (Utgifter), **Välj fil** / **Byt fil** and **Välj SIE-fil** are gone (drop-zone titles below; the command-bar label **Ladda upp kvitto** remains), while the `FormData` transport, client caps, store-before-mutate order, demo bucket rules and Swedish errors from #90 / #120 are unchanged; verified against main code 2026-09-08, code-verified only. Customer e-mail subjects for quotes/invoices ([PR #101](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/101)) verified against main code 2026-09-04. Komplett bokföring för aktiebolag ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109), merge `7dcb653`: kontoregister, manuella verifikat, omvänd byggmoms, momsperiodicitet, skattekonto, lön/AGI, periodstängning, SIE-import, bokslutsbilagor, avstämning, K2-årsredovisning, INK2, inlämningslager, arkiv) verified against main code and the live demo 2026-09-05 - the new owner routes (`/bokforing/skattekonto`, `/lon`, `/verifikationer/nytt`, `/periodstangning`, `/ingaende-balans`, `/bokslut/bilagor`, `/bokslut/avstamning`, `/bokslut/ink2/<id>`) plus the eSKD, AGI and arkiv APIs answered with the demo cookie; `/bokforing/lon/[runId]` and the årsredovisning pages need data the seed does not have (no payroll run, no closed year) and the consultant routes (`/redovisning/k/...`) are code-verified only. Grossistbeställningar ([PR #111](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/111), merge `d65b830`: optional feature `wholesalers`, migration `38_wholesalers`, Grossister tab, material search + cart + order e-mail, order confirmations in the Inbox) and onboarding + **Flytta dina uppgifter** import ([PR #112](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/112)) verified against main code **and** the live demo 2026-09-06, after [PR #119](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/119) (merge `f7e669a`, CI green, Vercel production deployed): with the demo cookie `/installningar?flik=funktioner` lists Grossistbeställningar next to Hemsida + Samarbeta (`data-feature="wholesalers"`), **Aktivera** seeds *Demo-grossisten* (40 articles) and the material search answers on `job-kok`, `/kom-igang/importera` answers 200 with *Flytta dina uppgifter* + `data-import-*`, and `?flik=grossister` redirects to Funktioner while the feature is off. The Grossist cart → send → confirmation steps below are asserted by `src/lib/wholesalers.test.ts` and `scripts/verify-wholesalers-browser.ts` (JSON dev), not clicked through on driva-alpha for this map. **Weekly full audit 2026-09-07** against main `35b54aa` (after [PR #123](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/123): Bokföring overview without footer, *Behöver lösas* capped at 8) with a fresh demo cookie on driva-alpha: landing, Hem, all four Ekonomi tabs, Inbox, Bokföring overview / Verifikationer / Moms / Skattekonto / Lön / Periodstängning / Bokslut / Avstämning, Uppdrag, Kunder, Funktioner, Kom igång, `/kom-igang/importera`, Offert #116, `/offert/demo-bertil-fasad` (+ 404 for `demo-eva-bokhylla`), and the redirects for `/samarbeta`, `?flik=grossister`, `/kunder?flik=uppdrag`, `/bokforing/detaljer`, `/jobb/*`, `/pengar`, `/assistent` all match the sections below; the consultant routes, Lön with an employee, årsredovisning and the Grossist cart flow remain code-verified only. Owner live refresh after the customer's public accept/decline ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127), merge `9e3f760`: `LiveRefresh` in both app layouts, `announceLiveRefresh` from **Godkänn offert** / **Avböj offerten**) verified against main code 2026-09-07 - see [Layout](#layout) and [Offerter](#offerter-quotes); code-verified only, not clicked through on driva-alpha for this map. Company räkenskapsår as the default period for the bookkeeping views ([PR #124](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/124), merge `f371808`: Inställningar → Företag **Räkenskapsår** card, `resolveViewFiscalYear(?ar)` in Huvudbok / Rapporter / Moms / Bokslut and the consultant Moms / Rapporter / Bokslut twins, `FiscalYearPicker` chips once a company has more than one year) verified against main code **and** the live demo 2026-09-08 - with the demo cookie the card (`#installningar-rakenskapsar-start` / `-slut`, **Spara räkenskapsår**, **Skapa nästa år**) renders on `/installningar?flik=foretag`, Huvudbok / Resultat / Saldobalans read *2026-01-01 till 2026-12-31*, Bokslut *Räkenskapsåret 2026 pågår ännu*, and `?ar=1999` on the export API answers 400 *Okänt räkenskapsår*; the write flow (save a broken year → Huvudbok follows → **Skapa nästa år** → chips on every view) was clicked through on the local JSON dev server, not on driva-alpha - see [Inställningar](#inställningar) and [Bokföring](#bokföring). **Audit Tier 0 + Wave 1–3** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), merge `a8f6284`, migrations `42_wholesaler_shop` … `47_wave3_catalog`: Ny utgift, momsen i tre steg, Skatteverket-kalender + ICS, Bokföring enkelt/avancerat, Hem money strip, räkenskapsår, materialbutik, artikelregister, radrabatt/rubrikrader, utskick med förhandsgranskning/SMS/kopiera offert, automatiska påminnelser, uppdrag med förfrågningar/timer/foton/täckning, ROT-regler, delkredit, gemensam kvittouppladdning, svenska felsidor) verified against main code **and** driva-alpha (Vercel production deploy of `a8f6284`) with a fresh demo cookie 2026-09-08 - see the sections below. Two live findings from that pass - every `/bokforing/*` route rendered the Swedish error boundary in a browser, and `GET /api/cron/reminders` was bounced to `/login` by the proxy - are **fixed by [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134)** (merge `d82238d`; it also added the pending-schema twins for migrations 44–45, code-verified only), verified on driva-alpha 2026-09-10 - see [Bokföring](#bokföring) and [Fakturor](#fakturor). **Bokföring för snickaren** ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), merge `8b135bb`, 2026-09-12): the **Inbox** nav item is gone - its pages live on as **Bokföring → Underlag** (`/bokforing/underlag`, `/inbox*` redirects there), the **Bank** tab moved from Ekonomi to **Bokföring → Bank** (`/bokforing/bank`, `/ekonomi?flik=bank` redirects), the enkelt tab row is **Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Skatt** with the toggle **Visa redovisningsvy** / **Visa enkel vy** (cookie + per-user `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser`), new **Skatt** (`/bokforing/skatt`) and **Kontoregister** (`/bokforing/konton`) pages, one **8 MB** upload cap for kvitton / underlag / verifikat-bilagor, F-skatt auto-booked on page load, overdue moms / AGI / bokslut rows that cannot be snoozed, *Skicka till redovisningskonsult*, verifikationssök, manual-verifikat templates and a 2026 skattetabell - verified against main code **and** driva-alpha (Vercel production serving `8b135bb`) with a fresh demo cookie 2026-09-12 by fetching the pages (status codes, `<title>`, HTML/RSC text), not by clicking through; see [Primary nav](#primary-nav-swedish-labels-as-shown), [Legacy path rewrites](#legacy-path-rewrites-srclibnavts), [Underlag](#underlag-the-former-inbox), [Bokföring](#bokföring) and [Bank](#bank-bokforingbank--open-banking-ais-via-tink). The two FEATURE_MAP paragraphs #137 itself committed described an intermediate state (Översikt · Moms · Skattekonto row, *Visa avancerat*, "no server action") and are corrected below. **Brand strings:** every user-visible surface says **Ferva** (UI, `<title>`, manifest, e-mails, PDF attachments, downloaded file names `ferva-betalningar-…xml` / `ferva-arkiv-…zip`, ICS UIDs `@ferva`, inbound domain `in.ferva.se`). The old name survives only as technical identifiers (env prefix `DRIVA_*`, cookies `driva_*`, DB role `driva_app`, `/tmp` file names, log prefixes, the Vercel project name) — `src/lib/brand-scan.test.ts` fails on anything else, so never add a visible "Driva" and never rename the technical ones without a migration. **Materialkedjan** ([PR #151](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/151), merge `48cb555`, migration `58_document_lines`): job material chooser, document lines, purchase ref and invoice readiness - code-verified against main 2026-09-13; [PR #153](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/153) (merge `902e035`) moved `purchaseRef` assignment off RSC into `ensureJobPurchaseRefAction`; not a full live click-through on driva-alpha for this map. See [Materialkedjan](#materialkedjan-kvitto--faktura--beställning--uppdrag--faktura).

**Quality gate (spec §11):** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs typecheck · lint · `npm test` · `npm run test:db` · `npm run test:adapter` · `npm run build` on every PR and push to `main`, installs `libxml2-utils` first, and with `CI=true` a missing `xmllint` is a test failure (`src/lib/__fixtures__/xmllint.ts`): HUS files validate against the pinned Skatteverket XSD (`docs/skatteverket/hus/SCHEMAS.sha256`, `src/lib/hus-schema-pin.test.ts`), AGI / eSKD / iXBRL are checked as well-formed XML with `--nonet`. Go-live steps that cannot be verified from the repo (Stripe live, bootstrap, TOTP, PITR, Resend DNS, filing provider, expert review, app stores) are listed in `GO_LIVE_CHECKLIST.md`.

## How to use this map (example)

Bug: *“the quote delete button doesn't work.”*

1. **Where:** Offerter live under **Ekonomi → Offerter**, not a top-level nav item.
2. **Go there:** `/demo` → sidebar/bottom **Ekonomi** → tab **Offerter** (`/ekonomi?flik=offerter`).
3. **States that matter:** only **Utkast** can be discarded. Sent quotes use **Inte aktuell** (→ `avbojd`), not delete.
4. **Demo fixture:** Offert **#116** `quote-bokhylla` (Eva Holmgren) is the only seeded draft.
5. **Code/data:** `DiscardDraftButton` → `discardQuoteAction` → `discardQuote()`; tables `quotes`, `quote_versions`.
6. **Verify:** see [Offerter](#offerter-quotes) → *How an agent verifies*.

---

## App shell & navigation

### Layout

- App chrome: `src/app/(app)/layout.tsx` — `Sidebar` + `BottomNav` (`src/components/nav.tsx`) + `main` (`lg:pl-60`, bottom padding for mobile nav).
- Root wrapper: `data-driva-demo="1"` when JSON demo **or** public demo session. Client gates read it: the shared address autocomplete (`src/components/address-input.tsx`) stays on the local Swedish example list and never calls Google when `[data-driva-demo]` is present — see [Address autocomplete](#address-autocomplete-shared).
- Back/origin: `tillbaka` + `tillbakaNamn` query params (`src/lib/nav.ts`). `AppLink` stamps origin; `SmartBack` reads it. Browser back is real history.
- Completing a **hard** send-blocker (company settings, ROT personnummer; invoice customer email) must stamp the **document you left**, not that document's parent. Offert #6 → *Komplettera företagsuppgifter* / *Lägg till personnummer* → Back is **Offert #6**. Missing quote e-post/telefon are **not** this path ([PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165), [PR #167](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/167)): they are filled in the Skicka dialog on the quote (same ids `#offert-skicka-epost-{documentId}` / `#offert-skicka-telefon-{documentId}`) and never leave the quote for soft contact. Invoice `#invoice-send-blockers` still links *Lägg till e-post* → `/kunder/{id}#kund-epost` and that Back must stay the invoice. Helpers: `pageOrigin`, `hrefFromOrigin`. Inställningar accepts `tillbaka` even without a default back (`acceptsReturnTo`).
- Breadcrumbs = structure, never history (`structuralCrumbs`).
- Support impersonation: `SupportModeBanner` when a Ferva Admin support session is active.
- Error pages ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)), Swedish, inside the chrome: `src/app/(app)/error.tsx` (client boundary for the whole `(app)` segment) renders a card *Sidan kunde inte visas* / *Något gick fel när innehållet hämtades. Ingenting har gått förlorat – försök igen, eller gå vidare till en annan del av appen.* + the error `digest` in mono + **Försök igen** (`retry()`) + **Till Hem**; sidebar and bottom nav stay. `src/app/(app)/not-found.tsx` catches `notFound()` from any `(app)` page (deleted / foreign-tenant uppdrag, faktura, kund …): `EmptyState` *Sidan finns inte* / *Det du letar efter kan ha tagits bort, eller så är länken fel. Kolla adressen eller gå tillbaka till översikten.* + **Till Hem** / **Uppdrag** / **Ekonomi**, chrome kept - note the HTTP status is **200** on driva-alpha (`/uppdrag/finns-inte`; the app layout has already started streaming). URLs that match no route at all (`/bokforing/finns-inte-alls`) and `notFound()` outside `(app)` (unknown offert/faktura token) get the root `src/app/not-found.tsx`: **404**, no app shell, *404 · Sidan finns inte · Länken kan vara fel eller ha slutat gälla. Har du fått den i ett mejl – be avsändaren skicka en ny.* + **Till startsidan**. No `data-*` / testid on any of them - match on the title text.
- Toasts (shared): `ToastProvider` wraps the whole `(app)/layout.tsx` shell; client components call `useToast().toast({ title, text?, tone?: "neutral"|"ok"|"danger", action?: { label, onClick | href }, duration?, id? })` (`src/components/toast.tsx`). Viewport `[data-toast-viewport]`, each toast `[data-toast][data-toast-tone]`, `role="status"` + `aria-live="polite"`, max 3 visible, auto-dismiss 5 s (8 s with an action, `0` = sticky), hover/focus pauses the timer, same `id` replaces instead of stacking. Sits above the mobile `BottomNav` (`--bottom-nav-h` + safe area) and centered over the content next to the desktop sidebar; `z-[100]` (above modals). Outside the provider (public pages) the hook is a silent no-op. **URL flash notices:** `FlashToast` (mounted once in the shell, inside `Suspense`) reads one-shot params via `flashFromSearch` (`src/lib/flash-notices.ts`, tests `flash-notices.test.ts`) — `?kastat=offert|faktura` (draft discarded) and `?bank=kopplad|avbrutet|fel[&meddelande=…]` (Tink callback) — shows the toast and strips the params with `router.replace`. Only catalogued keys render; free text never becomes a notice (the bank error message is the single exception and only with `bank=fel`). Users: line editor *Raden borttagen · Ångra* (`lines-editor.tsx`), `CopyLinkButton`/`ResendInvoiceButton`/`SimulatePaymentButton` in the `ActionMenu` (menu closes, toast confirms), `BankRowActions` (row leaves the list after refresh — toast stays), `PaySupplierButton`, `NewCustomerModal`. Inline feedback stays where it belongs: form errors (`role="alert"`), autosave *Sparat*, send-success cards on the document pages.
- Empty states: `EmptyState` (`src/components/ui.tsx`) always gets an `action` on list pages — filtered → *Rensa sök och filter* (`[data-clear-filters]`, resets q + status/filters + page), truly empty → the first natural step (Uppdrag: *Ny offert* + *Skapa uppdrag*; Kunder: *Ny kund*; Offerter/Fakturor: *Ny offert*/*Ny faktura*; Utgifter: *Koppla företagskontot* when no bank is connected (→ `/bokforing/bank` since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)); Underlag `/bokforing/underlag` (the former Inbox): *Visa allt som kommit in*; Verifikationer: *Visa alla verifikationer*; Skattekonto: *Till momsdeklarationen*). On the job page **Arbete och material** is one list; empty copy is *Inget registrerat än.* Add via `[data-job-add-entry]` **Lägg till** (no per-list empty-action links).
- Live refresh ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127)): `LiveRefresh` (`src/components/live-refresh.tsx`) is rendered in **both** `(app)/layout.tsx` and `(redovisning)/layout.tsx`; not on the public `/offert/*` / `/faktura/*` pages (outside the route groups). It is invisible - returns `null`, no DOM, no selector. It calls `router.refresh()` (server re-fetch; client state and scroll kept) when the tab becomes visible or the window regains focus after **≥ 1000 ms** away (`LIVE_REFRESH_MIN_AWAY_MS` - a shorter blur is focus flicker and is ignored, as is the initial focus), on `popstate` (browser Back/Forward - the App Router otherwise restores those from its client cache with zero server fetch), on bfcache `pageshow` (`persisted`) and on a `BroadcastChannel` message on `driva-live-refresh` (`LIVE_REFRESH_CHANNEL`; `{ kind: "data-changed", source: "quote-accepted" | "quote-declined", at }`). At most one refresh per **1500 ms** (`LIVE_REFRESH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`). **Skipped** while `[data-editor-shell]` or `[data-site-editor-shell]` is in the DOM - the open form is the truth until saved. Pure, DOM-free helpers in `src/lib/live-refresh.ts` (`createLiveRefreshGate`, `announceLiveRefresh`, `isLiveRefreshMessage`; tests `src/lib/live-refresh.test.ts`). Why: `revalidatePath` in a server action only re-renders the tab that ran it, and an owner tab left open does not re-read on its own - see [Offerter](#offerter-quotes).

### Address autocomplete (shared)

One Places integration for every editable physical address — `AddressAutocomplete` (single input) + `AddressFields` (gata / postnummer / ort) in `src/components/address-input.tsx`; pure helpers in `src/lib/address-autocomplete.ts` (+ `address-autocomplete.test.ts`). **Do not fork another.**

- **Source:** Google Places API (New) when `NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` is set **and** the surface is not demo. Key is read via `googleMapsApiKey()` (trimmed; empty / whitespace = not configured). `[data-driva-demo]` → local Swedish example list only, no Google HTTP. Missing key / Google failure → manual typing still works; no raw Google errors, no dead dropdown. Demo suggestions carry a **Demo** tag.
- **Behaviour:** **Default** is Sweden-only + street-only (`ADDRESS_PRIMARY_TYPES` = `premise`, `subpremise`, `street_address`, `route`; `includedRegionCodes: ["se"]` / `ADDRESS_REGION_CODES`), language `sv-SE`; no map/Street View. Search from **3 meaningful characters** after trim (`"va"` does not fire), debounce 250 ms (`ADDRESS_SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS`). One session token per typing session; `fetchFields(["addressComponents"])` only after the user picks a suggestion. Maps loader (`referrerPolicy="origin"`) gives up after **8 s** (`ADDRESS_PLACES_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS`); invalid key / referer block / script failure clears the searching state — **no hung spinner**, field falls back to manual typing. Opening an edit form with a saved address does **not** call Places. Picking writes street + postal + city (or one composed line); name / e-mail / phone / personnummer untouched. `composeSelected` accepts `"street"` (default), `"line"` (`gata, postnummer ort`) for single-field forms, or `"trip"` for a Traktamente resmål. **Trip override** (Ny utgift → Traktamente): `composeSelected="trip"` + `TRIP_PRIMARY_TYPES` (`geocode`) + empty `TRIP_REGION_CODES` (any country; city or street — empty list omits `includedRegionCodes` on the Places request) + `formatTripDestination` / `demoTripSuggestions`. Live key path (real Göteborg results etc.) is **not** verified in this environment — code only.
- **Keyboard:** Arrow moves highlight; Enter picks the highlighted row when the list is open; Enter never submits the parent form while the list is **open or a search is in flight** (`searching`), so Ny kund cannot submit early; Escape closes; Tab moves on.
- **Selectors:** input `role="combobox"` `aria-expanded` `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-busy` while searching; menu is portaled + viewport-flipped with `data-address-suggestions`, `z-index` 400 (`ADDRESS_MENU_Z_INDEX`, above the Ny kund modal at z=50); options `data-address-option={i}`. No `data-testid`.

| Surface | File | Mode |
|---------|------|------|
| Ny kund (privat + företag) | `new-customer-modal.tsx` | `AddressFields` |
| Redigera kund | `customer-details-form.tsx` | `AddressFields` |
| ROT-bostad / fastighet | `work-location-form.tsx` (kundkort ROT/RUT + quote/invoice **+ Ny fastighet**) | `AddressFields` |
| Onboarding företagsadress | `onboarding-form.tsx` | `AddressFields`; ids `ob-address`, `ob-postal`, `ob-city` |
| Inställningar företagsadress | `settings-form.tsx` | `AddressFields`; ids `installningar-address`, `installningar-postalCode`, `installningar-city`; label **Gatuadress** |
| Faktura/offert blockers → komplettera adress | `settings-billing-readiness.tsx` | `AddressFields`; ids `komplettera-address`, `komplettera-postalCode`, `komplettera-city` |
| Nytt uppdrag, ny adress | `uppdrag-form.tsx` | `AddressFields`; names `newAddress`, `newPostalCode`, `newCity` |
| Redigera uppdrag (one line) | `uppdrag-form.tsx` | `AddressAutocomplete composeSelected="line"` |
| ROT saknad arbetsadress | `tax-reduction-application.tsx` | `AddressAutocomplete composeSelected="line"` |
| Ny utgift → Traktamente resmål (`#traktamente-resmal`) | `manual-expense-form.tsx` | `AddressAutocomplete composeSelected="trip"` (+ `TRIP_PRIMARY_TYPES` / `TRIP_REGION_CODES`) |

**Not autocomplete (do not expect it):** invoice/quote documents and public `/faktura/*` `/offert/*` (read-only); website contact/footer (company address from settings); inbox inbound e-mail address; domain registrant; fastighetsbeteckning; Land/säte fields; search boxes; supplier/kvitto extraction.

**Verify (demo):** `/demo` → Kunder → **Ny kund** → type `Väd` in **Adress** (default label; Inställningar says **Gatuadress**) → list `[data-address-suggestions]` with **Demo** tag (e.g. *Vädursvägen 13*) → pick → adress + postnummer + ort filled, name field untouched. Puppeteer: assert no request to `maps.googleapis.com`.

### Primary nav (Swedish labels as shown)

Config: `NAV_ITEMS` in `src/lib/nav.ts` (each item has `group: "primary" | "more"`; helpers `primaryNavItems` / `moreNavItems` apply the optional-feature filter). Icons in `src/components/nav.tsx` (`NAV_ICONS`, Uppdrag = `Hammer`).

**Main navigation = Hem · Uppdrag · Kunder · Ekonomi · Mer** — identical on desktop sidebar and mobile bottom bar. Uppdrag is the craftsman's workspace and therefore primary; Kunder is only the customer register.

| UI label | Route | Section | Group | Badge |
|----------|-------|---------|-------|-------|
| Hem | `/` | `hem` | primary | never |
| Uppdrag | `/uppdrag` | `uppdrag` | primary | never |
| Kunder | `/kunder` | `kunder` | primary | never |
| Ekonomi | `/ekonomi` | `ekonomi` | primary | never |
| Bokföring | `/bokforing` | `bokforing` | Mer | open underlag **+** bookkeeping questions (`countInboxBadge() + countBookkeepingBadge()` in `nav-counts.ts`) |
| Samarbeta | `/samarbeta` | `samarbeta` | Mer | never; **optional** |
| Hemsida | `/hemsida` | `hemsida` | Mer | never; **optional** |

**Inbox is not a nav item any more** ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)): `NavSection` has no `inbox`, `NAV_ITEMS` has no `/inbox` row (`nav.test.ts` asserts it), and the pages moved to **Bokföring → Underlag** (`/bokforing/underlag`, see [Underlag](#underlag-the-former-inbox)); `/inbox*` redirects. Its badge count was folded into the Bokföring number: `getNavAttentionCounts()` returns `{ inbox: 0, bokforing: countInboxBadge() + countBookkeepingBadge() }` (the `inboxCount` prop is still passed to `Sidebar` / `BottomNav` but ignored).

**Mer** also holds (not sections): **Inställningar** `/installningar`, **Hjälp & support** `/support?fran=<path>`. Bokföring keeps its count badge inside Mer; on mobile the **Mer** tab shows the same number (`aria-label="Mer, {n} att lösa"`, `moreCount` = the Mer items' counts, which today is Bokföring alone).

Active section: `sectionForPath` → `isSectionActive`. `/uppdrag` **and** `/uppdrag/[id]` (plus legacy `/jobb/[id]`, `/kunder/forfragningar/[id]`) light up **Uppdrag**; `/kunder` and `/kunder/[id]` light up **Kunder**; every `/bokforing/*` route incl. `/bokforing/underlag/[id]`, `/bokforing/bank`, `/bokforing/skatt`, `/bokforing/konton` lights up **Bokföring**. Active links carry `aria-current="page"`.

**Optional features** (`src/lib/features.ts`, `src/lib/optional-features.ts`; `OPTIONAL_FEATURE_IDS`): `website`, `collaboration`, `wholesalers` (Grossistbeställningar). Hidden from nav when off; a direct URL (`/hemsida`, `/samarbeta`, `/installningar?flik=grossister`) redirects to `/installningar?flik=funktioner`. `wholesalers` **never** has a nav item - when on it lives in the job's material UI and under Inställningar → Grossister (`NavFeatures` only knows `website` / `collaboration`; activation lands on `OPTIONAL_FEATURE_HREF`). Existing usage without a stored flag counts as on (backfill: website/domains, invitations, `hasWholesalerUsage` = a connection or an order). Explicit `false` wins. Deactivate does **not** delete content.

**Demo (JSON mode):** sidebar shows Hem, Uppdrag, Kunder, Ekonomi, then the **Mer** group: Bokföring (badge), Hemsida, Inställningar, Hjälp & support. **Samarbeta is absent** (collaboration not activated). Company footer: **Södermalms Snickeri AB** + **Demo** badge.

Badge aria: `Bokföring, {n} att lösa` (`Bokföring, 1 att lösa` for one) and `Mer, {n} att lösa` on the mobile Mer button - the old `Inbox, {n} öppna` / `Bokföring, {n} bokföringsfrågor att lösa` strings are gone ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)). Counts from `src/lib/services/nav-counts.ts`. Live demo 2026-09-12: **7** (`Bokföring, 7 att lösa` / `Mer, 7 att lösa`) = the open Byggmax underlag + the bookkeeping rows; the individual rows were not enumerated for this note.

### Desktop vs mobile

- **Desktop (`lg+`):** fixed 240px sidebar. Primary four rows, then a muted **Mer** heading (`#sidebar-mer-rubrik`) over the always-expanded group `[data-nav-group="mer"]` (Bokföring, optional Samarbeta/Hemsida, Inställningar, Hjälp & support - no Inbox row since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)). Footer = company name / demo menu / workspace switcher / logout only — in a public `/demo` session the logout row is replaced by **Skapa ditt eget konto** (`CreateAccountRow`, `aria-label="Skapa ditt eget konto"`, also at the bottom of the Mer sheet).
- **Mobile:** bottom bar `nav[aria-label="Huvudnavigation"]` = **Hem · Uppdrag · Kunder · Ekonomi · Mer** (five equal tabs, icon + label, ≥44px). Mer sheet (`role="dialog"` `aria-label="Mer"`) holds Bokföring, optional Samarbeta/Hemsida, Inställningar, support, demo/logout.
- Editor pages widen via `data-editor-shell` / `data-site-editor-shell`.

### Auth gates

Proxy (Next 16 middleware): `src/proxy.ts`. Real auth is always server-side (`ensurePageBusiness` / `withBusiness`).

**Public prefixes** (no login): `/login`, `/signup`, `/verifiera-epost`, `/glomt-losenord`, `/auth/bekrafta`, `/demo`, `/valkommen`, `/villkor`, `/integritet`, `/bitradesavtal`, `/underbitraden`, `/offert`, `/faktura`, `/sajt`, `/integritetspolicy`, `/inbjudan`, `/api/health`, `/admin/inbjudan`, `/api/auth/send-email`, `/api/inbox`, `/api/dev`, `/api/cron` (since [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134): Vercel Cron has no session - the route itself still requires `CRON_SECRET`, see [Fakturor](#fakturor)). (`/api/bankid/*` is **removed** — the customer accept is a server action from `/offert/[token]`.)

`/api/kvitto/[receiptId]` is **not** public — it requires a session or demo cookie (`withBusinessRead`); without one the proxy redirects to `/login?next=/api/kvitto/…`. Same for `/api/verifikat/[id]/bilaga`, `/api/bokforing/export`, `/api/bokforing/deklaration`, `/api/bokforing/kalender` (ICS, [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)), `/api/inbox/bilaga/...`, `/api/betalfil/[id]` and `POST /api/grossist/prisfil` (session + same-origin check + `manage_wholesalers`). **`/api/cron/reminders` is allowlisted** through the `/api/cron` prefix ([PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134)) — the proxy lets the session-less request through and the handler's own `CRON_SECRET` check is the gate (401 `{"ok":false,"error":"Obehörig."}` without it, verified on driva-alpha 2026-09-10). Before #134 the prefix was missing and the proxy bounced the cookie-less request to `/login?next=%2Fapi%2Fcron%2Freminders` (307, seen 2026-09-08), so the Vercel cron in `vercel.json` never reached the handler - see [Fakturor](#fakturor).

**Read gate vs write gate** ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109), `src/lib/collaboration/permissions.ts`): `withBusinessRead` → `assertReadAccess` = capability `read_accounting`, which **every** role has (owner, admin, member, `accounting_consultant`, `auditor`) - so SIE export, kvitton, inbox attachments, payment files and filing downloads work from the redovisning workspace. `withBusiness` (write) → `assertWriteAccess`: with a `capability` the role matrix decides; **without** one the action is owner-surface only (*Den här åtgärden är inte tillgänglig från redovisningsytan.*). Before #109 the read path went through the write gate and denied accounting roles.

**Logged-out `/`:** rewrite to `/valkommen` (URL stays `/`). **`/valkommen`:** redirect to `/`.

**Protected + no session + no demo cookie:** redirect `/login?next=<path>`.

**Logged-in on `/login` or `/signup`:** redirect `/`. Demo sessions are **allowed** on signup (create-account from demo).

**JSON mode** (no Supabase env): proxy is passive; local `.data/db.json` is already the demo company. Production without Supabase env **hard-errors** (no silent demo).

**Page gate:** `ensurePageBusiness()` — demo loads per-session JSON; Supabase loads tenant snapshot; no memberships → `/onboarding`; accounting-only role → `/redovisning`.

### Org / company context

- Tenant = `businesses` row. Active company via request slot / `driva_business` cookie.
- Roles: `owner`, `admin`, `member`, `accounting_consultant`, `auditor` (`src/lib/collaboration/permissions.ts`).
- Workspace cookie `driva_workspace`: `owner` | `redovisning`.
- Demo actor cookie `driva_demo_actor=accountant` switches to accountant UI.
- `WorkspaceSwitcher` appears for real users who also have accounting memberships.

### Legacy path rewrites (`src/lib/nav.ts`)

| Old | Now |
|-----|-----|
| `/pengar`, `/pengar/*` | `/ekonomi`, `/ekonomi/*` |
| `/jobb`, `/jobb/:id` | `/uppdrag`, `/uppdrag/:id` |
| `/kunder?flik=uppdrag` (old Kunder tab) | `/uppdrag` (q, visning, ekonomi, sortering, sida, tillbaka, tillbakaNamn preserved) |
| `/kunder?flik=forfragningar` | `/uppdrag` |
| `/kunder?flik=kunder` | `/kunder` |
| `/assistent` | `/` |
| `/kunder/forfragningar/:id` | `/uppdrag/:id` |
| `/inbox`, `/inbox/:id`, `/inbox/:id/kontrollera` ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)) | `/bokforing/underlag`, `/bokforing/underlag/:id`, `/bokforing/underlag/:id/kontrollera` (list query `q`, `visning`, `sida` preserved) |
| `/ekonomi?flik=bank` ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)) | `/bokforing/bank` (`q`, `status`, `sida`, `atgard`, `sort`, `direction` preserved, `flik` dropped) |

Server-side: `next.config.ts` `redirects()` - `/jobb*`, `/pengar*`, `/assistent` are `permanent: true` (308), the three `/inbox*` rules are **`permanent: false`** (307, *kvar som redirect i minst två releaser*) - plus page-level `redirect()` in `kunder/page.tsx` (`?flik=uppdrag|forfragningar` → `/uppdrag`, drops `flik`, keeps the list/back params), `jobb/page.tsx`, `jobb/[id]/page.tsx`, `kunder/forfragningar/[id]/page.tsx`, the three `inbox/**/page.tsx` files (same targets, the list page re-appends every string query param) and `ekonomi/page.tsx` (`?flik=bank` → `/bokforing/bank`). The proxy runs before a page `redirect()`, so cookie-less `/ekonomi?flik=bank` is still `307 /login?next=%2Fekonomi%3Fflik%3Dbank`, while cookie-less `/inbox` is `307 /bokforing/underlag` (the `next.config` rule fires first) and only then `/login?next=%2Fbokforing%2Funderlag` - both seen on driva-alpha 2026-09-12. With the demo cookie `/ekonomi?flik=bank` answers 200 with Next's meta-refresh shell to `/bokforing/bank`. `/bokforing/detaljer` (`bokforing/detaljer/page.tsx`, title *Bokföringsdetaljer*) still redirects to `BOKFORING_FLIK_HREF[flik]` or `/bokforing/verifikationer` (200 + meta refresh with the demo cookie 2026-09-12); its `ROUTES` pattern was removed in #137, `bokforingDetailTabForPath` still maps it to the Verifikationer tab. Client-side: `rewriteLegacyHref` / `sanitizeReturnTo` in `src/lib/nav.ts` normalise hrefs and `tillbaka=` chains the same way (`rewriteInboxPath`, and `rewriteLegacyLocation` turns `/ekonomi?flik=bank` into `/bokforing/bank`); `kunderInboxHref()` returns `/bokforing/underlag`, `labelForHref("/inbox…")` reads *Underlag*.

---

## Data model overview

Postgres in `supabase/migrations/`. App aggregate `DB` in `src/lib/types.ts` (JSON mode = one snapshot). Amounts: **öre-free kronor as `bigint`**. Entity ids: **text** (uuids or seed ids like `cust-anna`).

`public.requests` existed in migration 03 and was **dropped** in `15_migrate_requests_to_jobs.sql`. Incoming website forms create **jobs**, not inbox items.

```
auth.users
  └── business_memberships (role) → businesses
        ├── business_settings (1:1; company_form ab|enskild, vat_periodicity helar|kvartal|manad, f_skatt_per_month, tax_account_ocr (mig 45), notices JSONB (mig 43))
        ├── business_sequences (quote / invoice / verification = serie A; verification_series JSONB = next number per serie A|M)
        ├── customers (reverse_charge_construction - företag only, DB check; tax_reduction_used JSONB = legacy hos-andra blob, mig 47; UI no longer writes it — `usedTaxReductionThisYear` ignores it) → work_locations
        ├── jobs (photos JSONB, mig 47; billing_deferrals / closeout / customer_share JSONB + share_token, mig 48; purchase_ref + material_price_rule, mig 58) → job_work_entries (source manual|quote|ai|import|wholesaler; wholesaler_provenance JSONB; expense_id = the receipt/expense a material row came from, mig 47; change_id = registered on an approved job change, billed via the change, mig 48; document_line_id + document_line_allocation_id, mig 58)
        │     ├── job_changes (Ändringar och tillägg: number + version, status utkast|vantar_pa_kunden|godkand|avbojd|ersatt, lines JSONB, token, approval JSONB, content_hash - mig 48)
        │     └── billing_allocations (source_type + source_id → invoice_id + invoice_line_id; status draft|invoiced|released; ONE live allocation per (business_id, source_type, source_id) - the double-billing guard, mig 48)
        ├── document_lines (mig 58: article lines from receipt / supplier invoice / order confirmation; unique (business_id, source, source_document_id, source_index); billed only via JobWorkEntry → work_entry)
        ├── quotes → quote_versions (lines JSONB carry discountPercent / isHeading without a schema change); signatures (1:1 = the customer's acceptance record); bankid_orders (legacy, unused by UI)
        ├── invoices (reverse_charge, frozen at issue) → invoice_line_items (discount_percent 0–100, mig 46; is_heading, mig 47); invoice_issued_snapshots; payments
        ├── expenses (paid_by foretagskonto|privat, kind kop|milersattning|traktamente|representation, details JSONB - mig 44; NULL = köp from the company account) → receipts (file: storage_path in bucket `receipts` XOR content_base64)
        ├── supplier_invoices → supplier_payments → payment_files
        ├── bank_accounts → bank_transactions; bank_connections (Tink tokens, server-only RLS)
        ├── inbox_items (attachments JSONB; file in bucket `inbox_attachments` via storage_path, or inline ≤ 1,5 MB; document_type incl. orderbekraftelse + purchase_order_id / purchase_order_confirmation_id / purchase_order_candidates)
        ├── wholesaler_connections (favorite_articles JSONB, mig 42) → wholesaler_price_imports → wholesaler_products (the article catalog - outside the tenant aggregate: SQL in Supabase, file store in JSON/demo; brand, image_url, category_key + index, mig 42)
        ├── purchase_orders (job + connection; unique (business_id, reference); once sent: snapshot/reference/links immutable, no delete, never back to draft - trigger purchase_orders_guard) → purchase_order_lines; purchase_order_confirmations (history, no DELETE grant)
        ├── reminders; attention_states
        ├── chart_accounts (only the company's deviations from the coded BAS standard plan: custom / renamed / archived)
        ├── verifications (series A|M, transaction_date, attachment_* = underlag on the voucher) → accounting_entries
        ├── fiscal_years (label unique per business - fiscal_years_business_label_uq; start_date/end_date = calendar year by default, broken years allowed; opening_balances, opening_source, reopenings JSONB); vat_reports; assets; accruals
        ├── year_end_schedules (semesterloneskuld | kundfordringar_nedskrivning | periodiseringsfond, one per kind + year)
        ├── employees → payroll_runs (one per employee + month); employer_declarations (AGI, one per month)
        ├── annual_reports (superseded_at / superseded_reason after a reopen)
        ├── filing_submissions (moms | agi | ink2 | arsredovisning → skatteverket | bolagsverket; files = name + SHA-256 only)
        ├── websites; domains
        ├── collaboration_invitations; client_information_requests
        ├── business_onboarding (1:1 – onboarding status + Kom igång profile/task choices)
        ├── data_imports (audit of file imports, hash-unique per kind); suppliers
        └── assistant_messages; pending_actions; audit_log
```

Migrations for the accounting product ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109)): `30_chart_of_accounts`, `31_manual_verifications` (series counters, `transaction_date`, attachment columns, `app.post_verification` CAS per serie), `32_reverse_charge_construction` (+ `app.issue_invoice` carries the flag), `33_vat_periodicity`, `34_payroll`, `35_year_end_schedules`, `36_reopen_fiscal_year`, `37_filing_submissions`. All mirrored in `apply-pending-schema.ts` + `mappers.ts`; JSON files without the new arrays are read with `?? []`. After #109: `38_wholesalers` ([PR #111](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/111) - the six wholesaler / purchase-order tables plus the `job_work_entries` and `inbox_items` columns above; twin `ensureWholesalerSchema`), `39_onboarding_imports` ([PR #112](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/112)), `40_demo_sessions` ([PR #89](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/89)), `41_sie_import_series_counter` ([PR #119](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/119) - `app.import_verification` now advances **both** `business_sequences.verification` and the `verification_series` jsonb for serie A, and other imported series in jsonb only; before it only the column moved, so the next `app.post_verification` - which reads the jsonb series first - reused or CAS-rejected a number after a SIE import; twin in `ensureOnboardingSchema`). **[PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)** (all additive `add column if not exists`): `42_wholesaler_shop` (`wholesaler_products.brand`, `image_url`, `category_key` + partial index `wholesaler_products_import_category_idx`; `wholesaler_connections.favorite_articles` jsonb keyed on article number; guarded by `to_regclass` so it is a no-op where 38 has not run - twin in `ensureWholesalerSchema`), `43_owner_notices` (`business_settings.notices` jsonb, see [Inställningar](#inställningar)), `44_manual_expenses` (`expenses.paid_by` check `foretagskonto|privat`, `kind` check `kop|milersattning|traktamente|representation`, `details` jsonb), `45_tax_account_ocr` (`business_settings.tax_account_ocr` text, check `^[0-9]{10,25}$`), `46_line_discount` (`invoice_line_items.discount_percent` numeric 0–100; quote lines live in `quote_versions.lines` jsonb and take the field without DDL), `47_wave3_catalog` (`invoice_line_items.is_heading` boolean not null default false, `customers.tax_reduction_used` jsonb, `jobs.photos` jsonb, `job_work_entries.expense_id` text). Mirrored in `mappers.ts` (`paidBy`/`kind`/`details`, `taxAccountOcr`, `discountPercent`/`isHeading`, `taxReductionUsed`, `photos`, `expenseId`, `favoriteArticleNumbers`, `brand`/`imageUrl`/`categoryKey`). **Pending-schema twins exist for 42–47** (`ensureWholesalerSchema` for 42, `ensureColumn` calls in `applyPendingPageLoadSchema` - run by `ensurePendingSchema` - for 43 / 44 / 45 / 46 / 47, all in `apply-pending-schema.ts`). 44 and 45 got theirs in [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134) (merge `d82238d`), next to the 43 `notices` twin: `business_settings.tax_account_ocr` (text, check null or `^[0-9]{10,25}$`), `expenses.paid_by` (null or `foretagskonto|privat`), `expenses.kind` (null or `kop|milersattning|traktamente|representation`) and `expenses.details` (jsonb) - the same checks as the migrations, one `ensureColumn` per column so a partly migrated table is completed too. Before #134 the expense insert / settings upsert errored on a Supabase database where `db push` had not applied 44/45 (the mappers always name those columns). Code-verified only - not exercised by dropping the columns on production. **`48_closeout`** (closeout layer, branch `cursor/ferva-closeout-61d8`; twin `ensureCloseoutSchema` in `apply-pending-schema.ts`, run from `ensurePendingSchema`): `billing_allocations` + `job_changes` tables (RLS `app.is_member`, `driva_app` grants), `jobs.billing_deferrals` / `closeout` / `customer_share` jsonb + `share_token` (partial unique index), `job_work_entries.change_id`, `invoice_line_items.source_kind` check extended with `CHANGE_LINE`, `app.resolve_public_token` learns `job_change` (`job_changes.token`) and `job_share` (`jobs.share_token`), `app.reset_demo_business` clears both new tables. Mirrored in `mappers.ts` (`billingAllocations`, `jobChanges`, `billingDeferrals` / `closeout` / `customerShare` / `shareToken`, `changeId`) and `commit.ts`. See [Avsluta uppdrag](#avsluta-uppdrag-ändringar-betalplan-tidslinje-kundvy-closeout). The article register (`meta.articles`), the bookkeeping mode (`meta.bookkeepingMode` per company, and since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser[userId]`), the F-skatt auto-booking opt-out (`meta.autoBookFSkatt`, #137), the onboarding trade profile (`meta.tradeProfile`, #137), the last pasted skattekontoutdrag (`meta.lastTaxAccountStatement`, #137) and the learned bank counterpart rules (`meta.bankCounterpartRules`) live in `DB.meta` = the **`businesses.meta`** jsonb column (migration 02; `commit.ts` writes the whole object as `meta = $6::jsonb`, `metaFromBusinessRow` reads it) - no migration, and **not** `business_settings` (#137 also writes `settings.autoBookFSkatt` / `settings.tradeProfile`, but those two have no column or mapper - the `meta` twins are what survives a Supabase round trip; `isAutoBookFSkattEnabled()` reads both).

Platform (not tenant): `platform_admins`, `platform_admin_invitations`, `support_tickets`, `support_sessions`, `admin_audit_log`, `email_events`.

**Relationships agents hit most:**

| From | To | Notes |
|------|----|-------|
| Quote / invoice | Customer | Required; same customer if linked to a job |
| Quote / invoice | Job | Optional; same-customer invariant (`22_document_job_same_customer`) |
| Invoice | Quote | Optional chain |
| Invoice | Payments | One bank tx → one invoice (unique) |
| Inbox item | Expense or supplier invoice | Pipeline after review |
| Inbox item (`orderbekraftelse`) | Purchase order | Matched confirmation (`purchase_order_id`) or candidates for the user - never expense / supplier invoice |
| Purchase order | Job + wholesaler connection | Both required; one draft cart per (job, connection); a sent order makes the job archive-only |
| Job work entry (`source = wholesaler`) | Purchase order line | One per confirmed line with a customer price (`purchase_order_lines.job_work_entry_id`, unique) |
| Reminder | customer / quote / invoice / job | Optional related entity |
| Attention state | `action_id` | Presentation only — does not change domain status |

**Businesses extras:** `is_demo` (frozen), `trial_started_at`, `trial_ends_at`, `subscription_status` (trial is DB state; **no paywall UI found**), `meta` jsonb = `DB.meta` (`features`, `purchaseOrderSequence`, `wholesalerDemoSeeded`, `websitePausedAt`, `bookkeepingMode`, `bookkeepingModeByUser`, `autoBookFSkatt`, `tradeProfile`, `lastTaxAccountStatement`, `articles`, `bankCounterpartRules`, `ignoredLineDescriptions`).

---

## Demo mode

### What it is

Two layers (`src/lib/demo.ts`):

1. **`isDemoMode()`** — env: `DRIVA_DEMO=1` force on; `=0` force off; production default off; local JSON/dev default on. Gates **fake money** via `assertDemoMode` in `services/banking.ts` (**Simulera inbetalning** on a customer invoice, the simulated ROT/RUT payout, supplier **Simulera betald** without a bank); `isDemoMode() || isDemoBusiness()` also selects the legacy mock-BankID provider, the mock bank / filing providers and suppresses accept / credit notice mail. There is no “exempelkvitto” feature.
2. **`isDemoBusiness()`** — `db().meta.demo === true` from `businesses.is_demo`. Public seeded demo company in Supabase. Public `/demo` session clones also set `meta.demo = true` (`src/lib/storage/demo-session-store.ts`).

Public **Se demo** never touches tenant data (no `businesses` / `auth.users` / RLS rows). `GET /demo` (`src/app/demo/route.ts`) sets httpOnly cookie `driva_demo` (`<expiresMs>.<sessionId>`) and clones `buildSeed()` (`src/lib/seed.ts`) into the session's own store (`src/lib/storage/demo-session-store.ts`): local JSON mode → `.data/demo-sessions/<id>.json`; Supabase mode (Vercel) → one jsonb row in `public.demo_sessions` (migration 40, `state_version`-checked per-instance cache) so every serverless instance sees the same session — a file in `/tmp` was per-instance and made the Ekonomi register show stale status after a public accept ([PR #89](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/89), server side); the remaining client-side staleness - an owner tab left open or restored from the App Router cache - is handled by `LiveRefresh` ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127), see [Layout](#layout)). Incognito = new clone.

Logged-in real user hitting `/demo` → `/`. Prefetch → 204. Rate limit fail → `/login?demo=upptagen`. Cookie TTL default 24h (1–72). Expired cookie → landing.

### Enter / exit

| Action | How |
|--------|-----|
| Enter | Landing **Se demo** or login **Se demo** → `/demo` |
| Stay | Same cookie reuses the file |
| Reset | Sidebar company row → **Återställ demo** (confirm **Återställa demon?**) or Inställningar demo section. `resetDemoAction` → `resetDemoSessionState(sessionId)` for a public `/demo` session; local JSON without a demo cookie → `resetDemoData()` (`store.ts`) |
| End | **Avsluta demo** (public session only) → `endDemoAction` |
| Convert | **Skapa ditt eget konto** → `endDemoToSignupAction` → `/signup` |
| Accountant view | **Visa redovisningsvyn** (with the trailing *n*, in the company menu) → `enterLocalAccountantDemoAction` (cookie `driva_demo_actor=accountant`). Not the same thing as the Bokföring tab-row toggle **Visa redovisningsvy** (`button[data-bokforing-mode]`, [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)), which only switches the owner's enkelt/avancerat row - see [Bokföring](#bokföring) |

Demo menu: `src/components/demo-menu.tsx`. Trigger = company name + **Demo** badge. Copy: *Du använder demon* / *Utforska Ferva med exempeldatan för {title}.*

Local JSON (`npm run dev`, no Supabase): already seeded; **Avsluta demo** hidden (`canEndDemo` false). Reset still works. JSON-only persona shortcuts (route handlers, each `redirect("/")` in Supabase mode): `GET /dev/som-konsult` → cookie switch to the seeded consultant (`LOCAL_JSON_ACCOUNTANT_ID`, workspace `redovisning`) → `/redovisning`; `GET /dev/som-admin` → seeded platform admin → `/admin`; `/dev/onboarding?steg=1|2` previews the onboarding steps.

### Seed company

**Södermalms Snickeri AB**, org `559123-4567`, address Renstiernas gata 12, 116 28 Stockholm, bankgiro `5678-1234`, inbound `demo@in.ferva.se`, site slug `sodermalms-snickeri`. Demo user: `demo@driva.local` / name **Du**. Accountant persona: **Anna Svensson**.

| `quote-fasad` | 115 | Fasadarbete och nya fönsterfoder | Bertil Lindqvist | Väntar på godkännande (`viewedAt` set → list *Öppnad · väntar på godkännande*) | `demo-bertil-fasad` |

Seeded acceptances (`signatures`): `sig-nord1`, `sig-kok`, `sig-altan` — all `method: simple_accept` with statement, contentHash, e-mail, ip, userAgent.

**Uppdrag (stable):** `job-kok` (Pågår), `job-altan` (Pågår — future `startDate` in data only), `job-fonster` / `job-nord1` / `job-kokso` / `job-racke` (Klart — hidden under Aktiva), plus other open jobs for Sara, Karin, garderob, nord2, fasad.

**Fakturor:** paid `#1033`–`#1036`, `#1039`–`#1041`, `#1045` (there is **no** #1037 / #1038); `#1042` slutfaktura Brf Eken **Förfallen 6 dagar** (relative due date); `#1047` delbetalning Johan **Skickad**; `inv-1048` **Utkast** Brf Eken (list title is the first line “Lagning av portparti, entré Åsögatan 114”; status chip Utkast, detail heading *Faktura (utkast)*). Two 0 kr **Utkast** for Eli (`inv-eli-luckor` “Luckor i ek”, `inv-eli-bankskiva` “Bänkskiva i ask”). The seed gives these drafts `number` 1048 / 1049 / 1050, but `invoiceHasIssuedListNumber` ignores the number while `status === "utkast"`, so no draft ever shows `#n`. Tokens e.g. `demo-f1048`.

**Underlag / inbox items:** `inbox-mail-beijer` (Beijer, redo att betala), `inbox-mail-byggmax` (Byggmax, kontrollera belopp — the one item `countInboxBadge()` counts, which since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) is part of the **Bokföring** badge, not a nav item of its own), `inbox-mail-bauhaus`, `inbox-mail-okq8`. Listed on `/bokforing/underlag`; `/inbox/inbox-mail-byggmax` 307s to `/bokforing/underlag/inbox-mail-byggmax`.

**Påminnelser:** `rem-karin` (Ring Karin…), `rem-slutgenomgang`, `rem-kapsag` (utan datum).

Also: bank (SEB …4512), expenses, supplier invoices, verifications (all serie **A**), published website, payment files. **Not seeded:** `employees`, `payrollRuns`, `employerDeclarations`, `yearEndSchedules`, `filingSubmissions`, `chartAccounts` are empty; no customer has **Omvänd byggmoms**; `vatPeriodicity` unset (= kvartal); `fSkattPerMonth` = 12 400 kr. Since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) the seed itself books the F-skatt charges: one `ver-fskatt-{YYYY-MM}` (*F-skatt {YYYY-MM}*, 2518 D / 1630 K, `source: { type: "skattekonto", id: "fskatt-{YYYY-MM}" }`, dated the 12th - 17th in January and August) for every month of the current calendar year whose charge date has passed (`passedFskattMonths`; nine on 2026-09-12: 2026-01 … 2026-09), and the two seeded Skatteverket bank payments `ver-skatt-jul` / `ver-skatt-aug` are *Inbetalning till skattekontot* (1630 D / 1930 K, `entriesTaxAccountDeposit`) instead of the old *Preliminärskatt* 2510/1930 rows - so the page-load auto-booking (`ensureAutoFSkattBookings`, see [Skattekonto](#skattekonto-bokforingskattekonto)) has nothing to add, there are **no** *Bokför F-skatt {YYYY-MM}* cards on the demo, and the old *Skattekontot är inte à jour · F-skatt för N månader* row is gone. Live `/bokforing/verifikationer` 2026-09-12 lists *F-skatt 2026-01* … *F-skatt 2026-09*. `wholesalerConnections`, `wholesalerPriceImports`, `purchaseOrders`, `purchaseOrderLines`, `purchaseOrderConfirmations` are empty and `meta.features.wholesalers` is unset, so **Grossistbeställningar is off** for Södermalms Snickeri until you activate it (then *Demo-grossisten* + a 40-article CSV price list are seeded through the real importer, once per session - `meta.wholesalerDemoSeeded`). **Räkenskapsår:** the seed carries none (`fiscalYears: []`); `migrateAccounting` (store load, `yearsToCoverDate`) creates the calendar year(s) covering today and every verification date, so the demo runs on **2026** (1 Jan–31 Dec) as one year → no `FiscalYearPicker` chips anywhere until you press **Skapa nästa år**. Fiscal year ids are per session clone (e.g. `fy-2026-qzhkremw`, random suffix) - read them from links, never hard-code. The Supabase seed import (`importStateIntoBusiness` in `src/lib/storage/import-state.ts`, used by `scripts/db-seed.ts`, `migrate-local-to-supabase.ts` and `demo-reset.ts`) normalizes the source first (which backfills its own `2026` with a different id) and then drops the placeholder calendar year that `createBusinessWithOwner` inserted (`dropPlaceholderFiscalYears`, `2bfa9c3`) so the two do not collide on the unique label. `meta.bookkeepingMode` and `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser` are unset (→ **enkelt** tab row **Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Skatt** until the browser carries a `driva_bokforing_lage=avancerat` cookie), `meta.articles` is empty (*Inga artiklar ännu.*), `settings.taxAccountOcr` is unset (Moms step 3 links to the OCR e-service), no `expenses.kind` / `paid_by` rows exist, `customers.tax_reduction_used` is empty (legacy jsonb; kundkort has no year-cap widget / no *Ferva i {år}* / no *Kvar att lova*). Jobs carry `source` + `originalMessage` (`job-kok` web form → *Kundens förfrågan* card; Karin Ek *Via telefon* and Sara Nilsson *Via webbformulär* are open **Ny förfrågan** rows; garderob / nord2 arrived by e-mail, fasad by phone). The bank seed includes the unbooked *SEB · Månadsavgift Företagspaket −79 kr* (→ *Bokför SEB 79 kr som bankavgift?*).

### Demo vs real accounts

| | Demo / JSON | Real Supabase |
|--|-------------|---------------|
| Auth | Cookie / no login | Email + password |
| Data | Isolated JSON clone | RLS tenant |
| Outbound mail | Simulated | Resend if configured |
| Quote accept | Simple accept (name + **Godkänn offert**), no e-mail to the carpenter | Same simple accept; carpenter notified via Resend if configured |
| Receipt file (**Lägg till kvitto**) | Inline `content_base64` (≤ 1,5 MB) — **always**, the bucket is never touched by a demo session | Private bucket `receipts` with `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`; inline fallback without it, **and** when the bucket rejects the upload (≤ 1,5 MB) |
| Inbox upload (drop zones **Släpp kvitton här** on Ekonomi → Utgifter / **Släpp kvitton och fakturor här** on `/bokforing/underlag` and the `/bokforing` overview, all `ReceiptUpload` without a purchase) | Inline base64 on the item (≤ 1,5 MB, larger = metadata only); `inbox_attachments` is never called (`inboxBucketAvailable`, [PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120)) | Private bucket `inbox_attachments` with `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (uploads capped at **8 MB** client + server since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), `UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES` via `validateReceiptFile`; the store's own `MAX_INBOX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES` for inbound **mail** attachments stays 10 MB); inline ≤ 1,5 MB without it |
| Bank / payments | **MockBankProvider** (SEB ···· 4512, synthetic tx, zero HTTP to Tink); **Simulera inbetalning** (customer invoice) / **Simulera betald** (supplier invoice) | **LiveTinkProvider** if all `TINK_*` set, else honest *Bankkoppling är inte konfigurerad* — never fake success |
| Places (address autocomplete) | Local Swedish examples with **Demo** tag — **zero Google HTTP** even if a key is set | Google Places API (New, Sweden + street types only) if `NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` set; no key / Google failure / 8 s load timeout → manual typing (local examples only when no key). Shared component — see [Address autocomplete](#address-autocomplete-shared) |
| AI | Optional; honest fallback if no key | Same |
| Trial | null | 14 days `trialing` |
| Fake money APIs | Allowed | `DemoModeError` |

## Public landing page

- **User-facing name:** (no product nav label — logged-out `/`)
- **Purpose:** Convert to trial or demo.
- **Routes:** `/` (rewrite to `/valkommen` when logged out). File: `src/app/valkommen/page.tsx`. Direct `/valkommen` → `/`.
- **How to get there:** Open live URL while logged out / no demo cookie. Header **Logga in**, **Testa gratis**.
- **Main actions:** **Testa gratis i 14 dagar** → `/signup`. **Se demo** → `/demo`. Header **Logga in** → `/login`.
- **Copy (live):** H1 *Ferva ditt företag. Inte administrationen.* Steps: *Få jobbet / Gör jobbet / Få betalt / Slipp administrationen.* Features: ROT/RUT, Digitalt godkännande, Kvitton & bokföring, Egen hemsida. Price: *199 kr/mån efter provperioden · Inget kort krävs · Säg upp när du vill* (footer CTA repeats *14 dagar gratis · Inget kreditkort · 199 kr/mån därefter · Säg upp när du vill*).
- **Related:** `/villkor` (v2.0), `/integritet` (v2.0), `/bitradesavtal` (DPA + säkerhetsbilaga), `/underbitraden` (derived from `src/lib/legal/providers.ts` — only active providers). Customer-site policy is `/integritetspolicy`. All Ferva legal pages render from `src/lib/legal/documents.ts`; the counterparty comes from `LEGAL_*` env (`src/lib/legal/entity.ts`) and is shown as *[avtalspart ej konfigurerad]* when missing — never invented. Pages carry a draft banner until legal review.
- **Terms acceptance:** signup requires the `acceptTerms` checkbox (`data-signup-terms`) and stores `terms_version`/`terms_accepted_at` in user_metadata; `terms_acceptances` (migration 53, append-only) holds the audit. A **major** version bump gates `/`, `/redovisning`, `/onboarding` behind `/godkann-villkor` (`data-terms-gate-form`) and `withBusiness` writes throw until re-accepted (`ensureTermsAccepted` / `termsGateStatus` in `src/lib/legal/acceptance.ts`).
- **Data subject:** `GET /api/konto/export` (own account JSON) from Inställningar → Konto → *Dina uppgifter och avtal* (`data-konto-export`); admin user page has *Registrerades begäran (GDPR)*: rättelse (logged) / radering / **anonymisering** (`anonymizeUserAccount`, super_admin, audited). Checkout is blocked (`LEGAL_ENTITY_MISSING_MESSAGE`) and `/api/health` is 503 in production while `LEGAL_*` is incomplete.
- **Components:** `src/components/home-preview.tsx` (static preview of demo company — does **not** start a demo session).
- **DB:** none.
- **Invariants:** No prefetch of the app from landing (`<a>`, not `<Link>`). Demo cookie is created only on `/demo`.
- **Desktop/mobile:** stacked CTAs on small screens.
- **Verify:**
  1. Incognito → `https://driva-alpha.vercel.app/` → landing (not Hem). Title: *Ferva – mindre administration, mer tid till jobbet*.
  2. Link *Se demo* → `/demo` → Hem titled *Hem · Ferva*, `data-driva-demo="1"`, company *Södermalms Snickeri AB*.
  3. Protected URL without cookie (e.g. `/ekonomi`) → `/login?next=/ekonomi`.
  4. Script: `scripts/verify-logged-out-demo.ts`.

## Signup / login / email verification

- **User-facing names:** Logga in · Skapa konto · Verifiera din e-post · Glömt lösenord
- **Purpose:** Create/authenticate a real Supabase user. Demo does not use this.
- **Routes:**
  - `/login` — `src/app/(auth)/login/page.tsx` + `login-form.tsx`
  - `/signup` — `signup/page.tsx` + `signup-form.tsx`
  - `/verifiera-epost?email=` — post-signup “check your mail”
  - `/auth/bekrafta` — Supabase OTP/PKCE
  - `/glomt-losenord`, `/uppdatera-losenord`
  - `POST /api/auth/send-email` — Supabase Send Email-hook → Resend (Ferva-mall)
- **How to get there:** Landing CTAs; login *Har du inget konto? Skapa konto*; signup *Har du redan ett konto? Logga in*; invite `/inbjudan/[token]` may send here with `?next=`.
- **Main actions:** Logga in / Skapa konto / Skicka igen / Skicka återställningslänk. Login also **Se demo**.
- **Fields / ids:** `auth-email`, `auth-password`, `signup-email`, `signup-phone`, `signup-password`, `reset-email`, `new-password`, `confirm-password`. Errors: `*-fel`.
- **Subflows:** Signup without session → `/verifiera-epost`. Confirm → `/` → `requireBusiness` → `/onboarding` if no membership **or** the owned company's onboarding is not complete (step 2 resumes). Reset → `/auth/bekrafta?next=/uppdatera-losenord`. Notices: `?bekraftelse=utgangen|ogiltig`, `?demo=upptagen`. Auth mail: GoTrue calls the hook; `token_hash` + `type` land on `/auth/bekrafta`. Without the dashboard hook, default “Supabase Auth” mail is still sent.
- **Actions:** `src/app/auth-actions.ts`. Hook: `src/lib/auth/send-email-hook.ts`, templates `src/lib/email/auth-templates.ts`.
- **Related:** Onboarding, invitations, demo convert-to-account.
- **DB:** `auth.users`; memberships created at onboarding.
- **Invariants:** Password never in URL. `safeAuthNext` blocks open redirects. JSON mode: login/signup return Swedish “requires Supabase” errors. Auth hook requires `SEND_EMAIL_HOOK_SECRET` + live Resend (no mock “sent”).
- **Desktop/mobile:** centered card, same form.
- **Live:** login and signup are reachable without auth. `/ekonomi` without session = login with `next`. `GET /api/health` on driva-alpha (2026-09-07) reports `RESEND_API_KEY` + `RESEND_FROM_EMAIL` **true** but `SEND_EMAIL_HOOK_SECRET` **false** — the Resend auth-mail hook is not armed there, so signup/reset mail on the live deploy is Supabase's default template (customer document mail via Resend is unaffected).
- **Verify:** fill `#auth-email` / `#auth-password`; submit *Logga in*. Signup: `#signup-email`, `#signup-phone`, `#signup-password`. Full signup→verify→onboarding needs real Supabase + email (`verify-logged-out-demo.ts` explicitly skips it). Tests: `src/lib/signup-flow.test.ts`, `src/lib/auth-email.test.ts`.

## Onboarding

- **User-facing name:** Berätta om företaget · Anpassa Ferva efter företaget (the H1 is the title alone; the step indicator *1 av 2* / *2 av 2* is a separate element in `onboarding-shell.tsx`)
- **Purpose:** Create the first company in a few minutes, asking only what is needed right away; personalize suggestions without locking anything.
- **Route:** `/onboarding` — `src/app/onboarding/page.tsx` (+ `onboarding-form.tsx` step 1, `personalize-form.tsx` step 2, `onboarding-shell.tsx`). Actions: `src/app/onboarding-actions.ts`. Local preview of both steps in JSON mode: `/dev/onboarding?steg=1|2` (Supabase mode → `/`).
- **How to get there:** After login, `requireBusiness()` redirects owners whose company has **no membership** (step 1) or whose `business_onboarding.status ≠ complete` (step 2) — `ownerNeedsOnboarding()` in `src/lib/setup/onboarding-state.ts`. `/onboarding` never calls `requireBusiness` → no loop. Consultant-only users and demo/JSON → `/`.
- **Step 1 fields / ids:** `ob-company-form` (Aktiebolag · Enskild firma · Annan företagsform → *Ferva stödjer just nu aktiebolag och enskild firma. Andra företagsformer kan inte skapas ännu.*, Fortsätt disabled), `ob-orgnr` (label *Organisationsnummer*, becomes *Personnummer (enskild firma)* for enskild firma), `ob-name`, `ob-vat` (**read-only, derived** `SE + orgnr + 01`, “Uträknat automatiskt”; hidden input `vatNumber`), `ob-address`/`ob-postal`/`ob-city` (shared `AddressFields`), `ob-email`, `ob-phone`, `ob-payment-timing` (*Lägg till nu* / *Gör det senare*), `ob-payment-method` + `ob-bankgiro|ob-plusgiro|ob-bankkonto` only when “now”. Summary error `#ob-sammanfattning`. Button **Fortsätt** (`data-onboarding-continue`).
- **Step 1 scope question (spec §10):** `ob-scope` — *Stämmer något av det här in på företaget?* Five checkboxes `input[name="scope"]` (values `foreign` · `inventory` · `k3_group` · `complex_payroll` · `reverse_charge`, `SCOPE_QUESTIONS` in `src/lib/support/eligibility.ts`) and a live verdict `[data-eligibility="supported|consultant|unsupported"]` (*Ferva passar företaget* / *Ferva passar – tillsammans med din redovisningskonsult* / *Ferva stöder inte företaget ännu*) computed by `assessEligibility` against the **support matrix** (`src/lib/support/matrix.ts`). `unsupported` disables Fortsätt; the server repeats the assessment in `createCompanyAction` (`assertEligibleToCreate` → `ScopeBlockedError` → field error on `ob-scope`) and `validateOnboardingFields` sets `fieldErrors.scope`. Answers are stored as `business_settings.scope` jsonb (migration 57, `BusinessScope { matrixVersion, assessedAt, flags, approvals }`) via `createBusinessWithOwner({ scope })`. Enskild firma and omvänd byggmoms are **consultant cases** (company can be created, the case is gated until the consultant approves); utland/valuta, lager, K3/koncern and complex payroll are **unsupported** (creation blocked server-side).
- **Step 2:** `ob-industries` (multi: El · VVS · Bygg och snickeri · Måleri · Mark och anläggning · Annat → `ob-otherIndustry`; help copy since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137): *Välj allt som stämmer. Det styr vilka leverantörer Ferva känner igen från start, till exempel Ahlsell, Flügger och Beckers. Dina egna regler vinner alltid.* - `applyPersonalization` derives `tradeProfileFromIndustries` (måleri → `malare`, el → `elektriker`, vvs → `vvs`, bygg → `snickare`, else `ovrigt`; first match wins in that order) and `applyTradeProfile` stores it as `settings.tradeProfile` + `meta.tradeProfile`; only `malare` seeds anything today - `meta.merchantCategoryRules` for *flügger* / *flugger* / *beckers* → `material` unless a rule already exists. The wider `KNOWN_SUPPLIERS` list in `src/lib/bas.ts` (Ahlsell, Dahl, Solar, Onninen, Optimera, Woody, …) applies to every company regardless of profile), `ob-payroll` (Nej, inte idag · Ja, till mig som ägare · Ja, till anställda · Jag tar det senare), `ob-bookkeeping` (Företaget har bokföring som ska flyttas hit · Företaget är nystartat · Min redovisningskonsult sköter bokföringen · Jag tar det senare). Button **Öppna Ferva** (`data-onboarding-open`) → `/`. No system names asked here.
- **State:** `OnboardingState` (`DB.onboarding`, table `business_onboarding`): `status` not_started | company_done | complete, `currentStep`, `startedAt`, `companyCompletedAt`, `personalizationCompletedAt`, `completedAt`, `industries`, `otherIndustry`, `payroll`, `bookkeeping`, `taskOverrides`. `membershipsForUser` joins `business_onboarding.status` into `MembershipInfo.onboardingStatus` (missing row = complete).
- **DB:** step 1 → `createBusinessWithOwner({ companyForm, onboardingStatus: "company_done" })` writes `businesses`, `business_settings.company_form`, `business_sequences`, `business_memberships`, `business_onboarding` **and one open calendar-year `fiscal_years` row for the current year** (`calendarFiscalYear`, [PR #124](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/124)) - there is no fiscal-year step in the wizard; a broken year is set afterwards under Inställningar → Företag → **Räkenskapsår** (see [Inställningar](#inställningar)). Step 2 → `applyPersonalization` in tenant context. Migration `39_onboarding_imports.sql` **backfills every existing business as complete**; `ensureOnboardingSchema` (pending schema) does the same where `db push` has not run.
- **Invariants:** Company form saved explicitly (no hidden AB default for new companies). VAT never typed — derived from org.nr; a mismatching value is rejected server-side. Payment details optional at create; the **same** `settingsBillingReadiness` blocks invoice sending, drives Kom igång *Lägg till betalningsuppgifter* and the settings banner. Existing companies are never sent back. Resume: an interrupted user lands on the remaining step at next login.
- **Verify:** JSON: `scripts/verify-onboarding-browser.ts` (forms on 390/320 px, derived VAT, payment later, honest company-form message). Supabase path: `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` (“steg 1 … company_done … steg 2 gör det klart”). Tests: `src/lib/onboarding.test.ts`, `src/lib/setup/setup.test.ts`.

## Kom igång (setup center)

- **User-facing name:** Gör Ferva redo (Hem card) · Kom igång (Inställningar tab)
- **Purpose:** Persistent, resumable list of what remains — derived from real data, never a blocking wizard.
- **Routes:** Hem card `src/components/setup/setup-home-card.tsx` (`data-setup-home-card`, max 3 open tasks, link *Alla steg*); `/installningar?flik=kom-igang` → `setup-center.tsx` (`data-setup-center`): profile (`setup-profile-form.tsx`, `data-setup-profile-edit|save`), *Att göra*, *Fler saker du kan göra*, *Gör senare*, *Behövs inte*, *Klart*, *Genomförda importer* (`data-setup-imports`), button **Ladda upp filer** → `/kom-igang/importera`.
- **Tasks (`src/lib/setup/tasks.ts`):** `move_bookkeeping` (done when a `bokforing` import is imported; hidden for *nystartat*), `invite_consultant` (done via `hasCollaborationUsage`), `first_customer`, `first_job`, `payment_details` (done when readiness has no payment blocker), `connect_bank` (done when `bankConnectionView().status === "connected"`), `articles_prices` (done when an active wholesaler price import exists; recommended for El/VVS; when the feature is off the CTA activates it via `activateOptionalFeatureAction` and lands on Grossister), `payroll` (**Ställ in lön** - *Lägg in dig själv (och eventuella anställda) så räknar Ferva skatt och arbetsgivaravgifter och bokför lönen varje månad.*; href `/bokforing/lon`, CTA **Lägg till anställd** / **Visa**; done when an `anstalld` employee exists; `recommended` when the profile says *Ja, till mig som ägare* / *Ja, till anställda*, `hidden` for *Nej, inte idag* without employees, else optional - [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) retargeted it to the real Lön tab; the old *Lön finns inte i Ferva ännu* / *Lönehantering finns inte …* copy is gone and the profile summary now reads *Lön hanteras under Bokföring → Lön: lägg in anställda så räknas skatt och avgifter automatiskt.*), `f_skatt` (**Ange preliminärskatten** - *Beloppet i Skatteverkets beslut om debiterad preliminärskatt. …*; href `/bokforing/skattekonto`, CTA **Fyll i belopp** / **Visa**, done when `settings.fSkattPerMonth > 0`, optional when a consultant does the books), `company_claims` (**Bekräfta F-skatt och försäkring** - *Raden ”Godkänd för F-skatt” och texten om ansvarsförsäkring skrivs på offerter, fakturor och hemsidan bara när du bekräftat uppgifterna här.*; done when `fSkattVerified(settings)` (F-skatt claim); doneDetail *F-skatt och ansvarsförsäkring* if `insuranceVerified`, else *F-skatt*; `recommended` when quotes/invoices exist (`hasInvoicing`), else optional; href `SETTINGS_HREF.foretag` (Företag tab); CTA **Bekräfta** / **Visa**; `canDismiss: true`). Priority scores: customer 20 · job 25 · move_bookkeeping 40 · consultant 45 · payment 50 (5 once quotes/invoices exist) · articles 55 · bank 60 · payroll 65 · f_skatt 70 · company_claims 75 (30 once quotes/invoices exist).
- **Status:** todo | in_progress | done | later | not_needed. Only `later` / `not_needed` are stored (`taskOverrides`); `done` always wins. Row selectors: `[data-setup-task=<id>][data-setup-status=…]`, actions *Senare*, *Behövs inte* (not for bank/payment/customer), *Ta upp igen* (`data-setup-reactivate`), *Visa*.
- **Priority:** bookkeeping=existing → *Flytta in bokföringen* first; consultant → *Bjud in din redovisningskonsult* first; new → customer/job; payment details jump first once quotes/invoices exist. Optional (non-recommended) tasks never show on Hem. Card disappears when no recommended task is open, and is never shown to backfilled existing companies without a profile that already have customers/jobs; center stays reachable.
- **Actions:** `setSetupTaskAction`, `updateSetupProfileAction` (`src/app/onboarding-actions.ts`).
- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-onboarding-browser.ts` (profile save, Senare/Ta upp igen, Hem card order). Tests: `src/lib/setup/setup.test.ts`.

## Flytta dina uppgifter till Ferva (import)

- **User-facing name:** Flytta dina uppgifter till Ferva
- **Purpose:** Upload what you have (SIE bookkeeping, customer/supplier registers, wholesaler price lists); Ferva identifies each file, previews exactly what will be created/omitted and imports only after an explicit confirmation.
- **Route:** `/kom-igang/importera` — `src/app/(app)/kom-igang/importera/page.tsx` + `src/components/imports/import-center.tsx`. API: `POST /api/kom-igang/import` (multipart, `mode=analyze|import`, `options` JSON; capability `import_data` = owner/admin; Origin check; max 25 MB; file never stored).
- **Flow:** dropzone (since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) the shared `FileDropzone` landing variant with `multiple`, title *Dra filer hit eller välj från enheten*; still `data-import-dropzone` on the zone and `data-import-file-input` on the hidden `input[type=file]`, *Vilka filer fungerar?* toggle inside the zone; the zone has no `role="button"` here because it carries children) → per-file card (`data-import-card[data-import-state=reading|checking|ready|importing|done|failed]`, `data-import-kind`) with real progress *Läser filen → Kontrollerar innehållet → Redo att granskas* → choices (SIE years `data-import-year`, register mapping “Vi tror att dessa kolumner hör ihop” `data-import-field=<field>`, article connection `data-import-connection`, unknown → `data-import-kind-select`) → **Importera** (`data-import-confirm-open`) → confirmation box (`data-import-confirm`, `data-import-run`) → done card (`data-import-state=done`, `data-import-summary`, link to next view). *Kontrollera detaljer* (`data-import-details`) lists unbalanced verifications, duplicates, review rows, unused columns.
- **Detection (`src/lib/services/data-imports.ts`):** SIE by content (`looksLikeSieBytes`/`parseSie`); PDF → unsupported (points to Inbox); tables via the wholesaler readers (`parsePriceFile`: CSV/TXT/XLSX/XML/ZIP) then `classifyRegisterTable` (kunder / leverantorer / artiklar / unknown). AI (`src/lib/imports/classify-ai.ts`) only **suggests** kind + mapping when deterministic detection fails and a key exists; result is badged *AI-förslag* and confirmed by the user. Without AI everything works manually.
- **Imports:** bokföring → `applySieImport` (see docs/onboarding-import.md); kunder → `Customer` rows (+ work locations for fastighetsbeteckning), suppliers → `suppliers` table (listed under Ekonomi → Utgifter, `data-supplier-register`); artiklar → **existing** `importPriceFile` of Grossistbeställningar (needs active feature + connection; no second catalog). Audit row `data_imports` per import (user, hash, counts, warnings, choices, summary); unique `(business, kind, hash)` for imported files → *redan inflyttad*.
- **Invariants:** nothing is written before confirmation; the confirmation upload must hash-match the analysis; one import per file+kind; SIE never imports an unbalanced verification; year conflicts default to skip; no file content is logged or stored.
- **Series counters after a SIE import** ([PR #119](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/119), migration `41_sie_import_series_counter`): imported verifications keep the file's serie + number (unnumbered ones and balances-only years get serie `SIE`, `source.type = "sie_import"`). Afterwards **both** the legacy `sequences.verification` column **and** `sequences.verificationSeries.A` must be ≥ max(imported A) + 1: domain `applySieImport` sets both; on Supabase the commit sends `sie_import` verifications through SQL `app.import_verification` (no CAS, runs **before** the app's own `app.post_verification` calls) which updates the column and the `verification_series` jsonb (serie A in both, other series in jsonb only); `loadTenantState` reads A as `Math.max(column, jsonb)` so a jsonb left behind by an older import can never pull A below the column. Why it matters: `postVerification` (`allocateNumberInSeries` in `accounting/series.ts`) and `app.post_verification` read the jsonb series **first**, so a stale jsonb A after an import handed the next own verification an already-used number (or `sequence_conflict` in the CAS). Not to be confused with the `/bokforing/ingaende-balans` SIE panel, which imports opening balances only and never touches the counters.
- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-onboarding-browser.ts` (CSV upload → mapping → import → done → duplicate refused → Kunder list → Genomförda importer). Tests: `src/lib/imports/sie.test.ts` (incl. `sequences.verification` and `verificationSeries.A` after import, next own verification does not collide), `src/lib/imports/registers.test.ts`, `scripts/db-validate.ts` (import_verification advances `verification` **and** `verification_series ->> 'A'`, data_imports index, RLS), `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` (SIE through commit).
