## Hem

- **User-facing name:** Hem
- **Purpose:** Command bar + prioritized work. Not a document register.
- **Route:** `/` (authenticated or demo). File: `src/app/(app)/page.tsx`. Title *Hem*.
- **How to get there:** Logo, nav **Hem**, `/assistent` redirect, post-login default.
- **Layout (live demo, 2026-09-08):** greeting (*God förmiddag* etc. + weekday/date) → command bar → **Gör Ferva redo** (only while recommended Kom igång tasks remain — see [Kom igång](#kom-igång-setup-center)) → **money strip** (below) → **Behöver din uppmärksamhet** (first 5, *Visa N till*) → **På gång** → **Påminnelser**.
- **Money strip** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130); `HomeMoneyStrip` in `src/components/home-money-strip.tsx`, numbers from `financeOverview()` in `src/lib/services/finance.ts`): `<section data-home-money>` with three tiles - **På kontot** (ledger account **1930**, not the bank API balance; → `/bokforing/bank`), **Reserverat** (*Moms, F-skatt och löneskatt*: current + declared-unpaid moms, 2 × F-skatt, payroll reserve, net of skattekontot; → `/bokforing`), **Ungefär ledigt** (= kontot − reserverat − unpaid supplier invoices, hint *{kr} i leverantörsfakturor*; → `/bokforing/bank`) - plus the row **Utestående kundfordringar** (*· {kr} förfallet*; → `/ekonomi?flik=fakturor`) from `businessStats()`. The two bank tiles pointed at `/ekonomi?flik=bank` until [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) moved the bank workspace to Bokföring (`home-money-strip.tsx`); Reserverat and Utestående kundfordringar are unchanged. Always rendered (0 kr without a bank). No selector on the individual tiles. Live demo: *På kontot 288 678 kr · Reserverat 60 864 kr · Ungefär ledigt 196 814 kr (31 000 kr i leverantörsfakturor) · Utestående kundfordringar 48 500 kr · 23 000 kr förfallet*.
- **Main actions:** type in command bar; click attention CTAs (Skicka påminnelse, Skapa bankfil, Öppna bokföring, **Lägg till kvitto** = a compact drop zone (shared `FileDropzone`, `role="button"`, `aria-label` = the CTA label, formats text `UPLOAD_MAX_FORMATS` = *PDF, JPG, PNG, HEIC · max 8 MB* since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) - was *PDF, bild · max 5 MB*; code-verified in `attention-list.tsx`, the live Hem HTML of 2026-09-12 did not render the row, so not seen live: drop, click, paste or *Fota kvitto* on touch screens - no plain file-picker button since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125)) that stores the file — see [Utgifter & kvitton](#utgifter--kvitton-flikutgifter), one-click **Bokför som bankavgift** / **Använd tidigare uppgifter**, …); reminder Klar / Snooza / Redigera / Ta bort.
- **Related:** same action engine as Bokföring (`src/lib/services/actions.ts`, `action-views.ts`). Hem shows a **projection** (`projectHomeAttention`), not a second queue.
- **Components:** `command-bar.tsx`, `attention-list.tsx`, `watching-list.tsx`, `home-reminders.tsx`.
- **DB:** derived from invoices, quotes, inbox, expenses, VAT, plus `reminders`, `attention_states`.
- **Invariants:** Hem has **no** nav badge. Attention ids match Bokföring. Snooze/dismiss writes `attention_states` only — overdue invoices stay overdue.
- **Desktop/mobile:** command bar full width; lists stack.
- **Live demo attention (2026-09-08, *Behöver din uppmärksamhet · 10*):** *Momsen för april–juni 2026 skulle ha deklarerats 17 aug.* (→ **Öppna momsöversikten**); *Faktura #1042 är 6 dagar sen*; *Bokför SEB 79 kr som bankavgift?* (one-click **Bokför som bankavgift**, see [Bank](#bank-bokforingbank--open-banking-ais-via-tink); since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) its deep link is `/bokforing/bank?atgard=bank-<txId>` and the moms rows link to `/bokforing/moms?fokus=<period>`); Fastighets AB Söderport *betalningsuppgifter saknas på fakturan* (**Använd tidigare uppgifter**); *Trygg-Hansa är redo att betalas* (**Skapa bankfil**); then *Visa 5 till*. **På gång** lists Offert #115 / #113 / #114 and Faktura #1047. New leads surface as *Nytt uppdrag: {title}* with a secondary **Skapa offert** (see [Uppdrag](#uppdrag)).
- **Verify:**
  1. `/demo` → Hem. Heading is a greeting, not “Hem”.
  2. Section title *Behöver din uppmärksamhet*.
  3. Click *Faktura #1042* row → `/ekonomi/fakturor/inv-1042` (or current id).
  4. Overflow *Fler alternativ* (`aria-label` starts with *Fler alternativ för*).
  5. Scripts: `scripts/verify-attention-browser.ts`, `scripts/verify-reminders-browser.ts`. Tests: `actions.test.ts`, `action-views.test.ts`.

## AI / command bar

- **User-facing name:** (no heading — field placeholder **Vad vill du göra?**; ⌘K hint)
- **Purpose:** Deterministic commands first; LLM only if configured and rules miss. **Not a chatbot** — no history.
- **Routes:** rendered on Hem (`variant="hem"`). `/assistent` → `/`. Accountant variant on `/redovisning` (placeholder *Fråga om bokföringen…*, prefetched as *Fråga om dina klienter eller be Ferva hantera bokföring…* / *Fråga om {klientens} bokföring…*).
- **Voice input:** mic button inside the field (`VoiceInputButton`, `src/components/voice-input-button.tsx`, loaded dynamically; Web Speech via `src/lib/speech/*`): `button[aria-label="Använd röst"]` → while listening the same button is `"Stoppa inspelning"` and the field shows *Lyssnar…* with interim text → silence auto-stops and the final transcript goes through the same parse path as Enter; `"Avbryt röstinmatning"` (× / Esc) discards without running anything. Hidden when the browser has no speech recognition; no audio is stored. Script: `scripts/verify-voice-browser.ts`.
- **How to get there:** Hem. Focus field or ⌘K.
- **Owner commands (labels):** Skapa faktura, Skapa offert, Skapa uppdrag, Skapa påminnelse, Ny kund, Hitta kund, Visa obetalda fakturor, Visa sena fakturor, Visa öppna offerter, Vad behöver jag göra idag?, Vad är på gång?, Visa fakturor, Ladda upp kvitto, Skapa en hemsida, Bjud in redovisningskonsult, Skicka betalningspåminnelse, Granska inför moms.
- **Accountant extras:** Vilka klienter behöver min hjälp?, Moms denna vecka, Saknade underlag, Bankavvikelser, Stäm av banken, …
- **Subflows:** slot-fill (customer → invoice target → …) then server `executeTool`. Confirm cards for `CONFIRM_REQUIRED` (send invoice, remind late, purchase domain, mark VAT declared). Reminders parse the **full sentence** — autocomplete never drops the rest.
- **Related:** docs/ai.md; tools in `src/lib/ai/tools.ts`; domain services under `src/lib/services/`.
- **Components:** `src/components/command-bar.tsx`, `src/lib/command-bar.ts`, `src/lib/services/command-bar.ts`, `src/lib/ai/loop.ts`.
- **DB:** `reminders` on create; `assistant_messages` / `pending_actions` for audit. Writes go through the same store as UI.
- **Invariants:** Risk classes enforced server-side. `FORBIDDEN_FOR_AI` not exposed. Personnummer never sent to LLM. No key → *Jag kan ännu inte tolka helt fri text. Välj en åtgärd nedan.*
- **Selectors:** `role="listbox"` `aria-label="Förslag"`; `aria-label="Påminnelsetext"`; `aria-label="Stäng resultatet"`. **No data-testid on the input.**
- **Verify:** type `Skapa offert` → suggestion list → pick customer. Or `Påminn mig att ringa Karin imorgon` → reminder on Hem. Scripts: `scripts/verify-command-bar.ts`, `npm run test:assistant`. Tests: `command-bar.test.ts`, `ai-loop.test.ts`.

## Attention items / reminders

Two different systems:

### Action-engine attention (*Behöver din uppmärksamhet*)

- Derived, not a table of todos. Snooze/dismiss: `attention_states` (`business_id`, `action_id`, `user_id`).
- Surfaces: Hem, Bokföring *Behöver lösas* (`#behover-losas`, `?visa=olosta`), accountant queue.
- Overflow can **Inte aktuell** a sent quote (→ `markQuoteNotRelevant`).
- **Lagkrav cannot be silenced** ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137); `controlsForAction` in `services/action-issue.ts`, `legal-deadline.ts` / `legal-deadline-id.ts`, tests `attention-state.test.ts`): a moms (`vat-*`), AGI (`agi-*`) or bokslut / årsredovisning (`year-end-*`) row whose `dueDate` has passed has `canSnooze: false` - no **Snooza** in the menu, and `snoozeAttention` throws *Den här raden kan inte snoozas – den ska aldrig tystas.* A snooze on an upcoming one is clamped to **08:00 the day before the due date** (`legalDeadlineSnoozeCap`). Everything else keeps the old snooze model (`attention_states` only, domain status untouched). New CTA kinds in the same PR: `declareVatPeriod` (row *Momsen för {period} verkar vara deklarerad och betald. Stämmer det?*, `vat-suggest-declared-{key}`, buttons **Ja, markera** / **Nej** = `hideAttentionAction`; raised when the pasted skattekontoutdrag or a booked Skatteverket bank payment matches the period's amount) and `closeReadyMonths` (`period-close-{key}`, **Stäng alla klara månader** with a `window.confirm`, see [Periodstängning](#periodstängning-bokforingperiodstangning)).
- Component: `src/components/attention-list.tsx`.

### User reminders (*Påminnelser*)

- **Purpose:** User/AI-created follow-ups (*Ring Karin…*).
- **Route:** Hem only (no dedicated page).
- **Groups:** Försenade, Idag, Kommande, Utan datum. First 3 then *Visa alla*.
- **Actions:** Klar, Snooza, Redigera, Ta bort (soft `DISMISSED`), Lägg till tid.
- **Components:** `home-reminders.tsx`. Actions: `completeReminderAction`, `dismissReminderAction`, `snoozeReminderAction`, `updateReminderAction`.
- **DB:** `reminders` — status `PENDING|COMPLETED|DISMISSED`; `due_at` nullable; `source` `assistant|user`; related `customer|quote|invoice|job`. **No DELETE grant.**
- **Invariants:** Completing a reminder does not change invoice/quote status.
- **Verify:** demo *Ring Karin Ek om bokhyllan imorgon* → Klar → row gone, DB `COMPLETED`. Script: `scripts/verify-reminders-browser.ts`. Tests: `reminders.test.ts`.

---

## Kunder

- **User-facing name:** Kunder
- **Purpose:** People/companies you work with. **Only the customer register** — jobs live under the primary nav item **Uppdrag** (`/uppdrag`); the old Kunder/Uppdrag tab strip is gone.
- **Routes:** `/kunder`, `/kunder/[id]`. Page: `src/app/(app)/kunder/page.tsx`. `?flik=uppdrag|forfragningar` redirects to `/uppdrag`; `?flik=kunder` is ignored.
- **How to get there:** Nav **Kunder**. Command *Ny kund* / *Hitta kund*. Attention/customer links.
- **Tabs:** none.
- **Subtitle:** *Alla du jobbar med eller pratar med – allt kopplas ihop automatiskt.*
- **Main actions:** **Ny kund** (`aria-label="Ny kund"`) → `NewCustomerModal`. Row → `/kunder/{id}` (`aria-label={name}`).
- **List filters:** Typ (Privat/Företag), Aktivitet, Betalning, sort Senast aktivitet / Kund / Att betala. Search: *Sök kund, företag, e-post eller telefon...*
- **Detail:** Företag / Privatperson. Header CTAs (`CustomerChainActions`): three buttons, no "+" prefix. Same glyphs as the object nav (`Hammer` from `nav.tsx` Uppdrag, `FileText` from Ekonomi/Offerter, `ReceiptText` from Ekonomi/Fakturor), 18px `currentColor` beside the label. Hierarchy: **Skapa faktura** filled (`primary`), **Ny offert** outline (`secondary`), **Starta uppdrag** / **Skapa uppdrag** ghost. One **Skapa faktura** (native picker or standalone - never a second **Fristående faktura** button). ROT/RUT section (privat). Activity list under *Aktivitet* ([PR #142](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/142): filter tabs + sortable columns; see below).
- **Skapa faktura** (main `683593bd` / `723ab705`; `CustomerInvoiceCreate` in `src/components/customer-chain-actions.tsx`, `customerChainCtas` in `src/lib/services/business-chain.ts`): one header control. `showInvoicePicker` = any quote or non-archived job (`quotes.length > 0 || jobs.length > 0`). No picker: `AppLink` `[data-invoice-create=standalone]` → `standaloneInvoiceHref` (`newInvoiceHref({ kund, fristaende: true, from })` = `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny` with `fristaende=1`). Picker: native `<details data-invoice-create="picker">` (opens without client JS; summary **Skapa faktura**; `max-lg:basis-full`, menu `absolute left-0 top-full` so it drops under the button on narrow screens). Menu `[data-invoice-picker][role=menu]`: **Från offert** (`invoiceQuotes` = approved quotes labeled `Offert #N`; one → button `createInvoiceFromQuoteAction`; several → nested `<details>`); **Från uppdrag** (`invoiceJobs` = non-archived jobs, href via `newInvoiceHref({ kund, job, from })` → `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny?job=` not `/uppdrag/`; one → `AppLink` `[data-invoice-create=from-job]`; several → nested `<details data-invoice-create="from-job-list">`); **Fristående** → `AppLink` `[data-invoice-create=standalone-choice]` to the standalone href. Invariant: never navigates to `/uppdrag/{id}` (old bug: `openJobId` → `jobHref`). No `fristaende_faktura` secondary CTA. Tests: `src/lib/customer-invoice-action.test.ts`, `src/lib/business-chain.test.ts` §41 (open job → picker + job href `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny?job=`; no-chain customer → picker false + standalone; seed Anna picker + Köksrenovering, Gläntan standalone). Live demo (GET with demo cookie, 2026-09-13, fetch only): `/kunder/cust-anna` renders `[data-invoice-create=picker]`, `[data-invoice-picker]`, **Från offert** (one approved quote → button), `[data-invoice-create=from-job-list]` (**Från uppdrag** → Platsbyggd garderob / Köksrenovering → `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny?job=`), **Fristående** `[data-invoice-create=standalone-choice]` with `fristaende=1` - no **Fristående faktura** label, no `/uppdrag/` href. `/kunder/cust-glantan` renders `[data-invoice-create=standalone]` → `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny?kund=cust-glantan&fristaende=1`.
- **Activity list** ([PR #142](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/142), [PR #159](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/159); `CustomerActivity` in `src/components/customer-activity.tsx`, sort/filter in `src/lib/customer-activity-sort.ts`, kinds/threshold in `src/lib/customer-activity-model.ts`): section *Aktivitet* on `/kunder/[id]`. Server feed (`customerActivityFeed`) is newest-first after chain grouping; the UI sorts the *current filter only* on top. Filter tabs **Alla · Offerter · Fakturor · Uppdrag · Betalningar** render whenever there is at least one row (`rows.length > ACTIVITY_FILTER_MIN`, `ACTIVITY_FILTER_MIN = 0` - the old threshold 8 hid the tabs after grouping on typical seed customers). Wrapper `[data-activity-tabs]`; tab buttons are Swedish label text only (no per-tab `data-*`). Columns **Datum · Händelse · Belopp · Status** are sortable (`button[data-activity-sort=datum|handelse|belopp|status]`); default = datum desc (same as the feed). First click on Belopp/Datum = desc; first click on Händelse/Status = asc; second click flips. Active `th` has `aria-sort="ascending"|"descending"`, others `aria-sort="none"`. List panel `[data-activity-list][data-activity-min-height]` sets `minHeight` only when the *visible* (filtered + paged) list is empty: ~5 rows (`ACTIVITY_LIST_HEAD_PX` + `ACTIVITY_LIST_EMPTY_MIN_ROWS` * `ACTIVITY_LIST_ROW_PX` via `activityListMinHeightPx(visibleCount)`). A full list is only as tall as its rows - it no longer reserves the unfiltered Alla height (that left a blank slab under Alla). Tab jump is avoided by pinning `[data-activity-tabs]` viewport Y across filter changes (`scrollBy`), not by stretching Alla. More than 20 events: first `ACTIVITY_LIST_PAGE_SIZE` rows plus **Visa fler** (`button[data-activity-visa-fler]`); no page numbers. Empty filtered state stays inside the panel: *Ingen aktivitet ännu.* Money line above the tabs is unchanged (`{kr} avtalat · {kr} fakturerat · {kr} obetalt`). Identity block and ROT/RUT are untouched. Tests: `src/lib/customer-activity.test.ts`. Header sort and tab-jump pin keep the #142 behavior (`9266a01`).
- **Omvänd byggmoms** ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109)): checkbox **Omvänd byggmoms** - *Kunden är ett byggföretag som redovisar momsen själv. Fakturor till kunden får 0 % moms och laghänvisning.* (Ny kund says *Fakturor får 0 % moms och laghänvisning.* without “till kunden”). Shown only for **företag**: Ny kund (`input[name=reverseChargeConstruction]`, företag form) and Redigera kund (autosave checkbox in `customer-details-form.tsx`, no id/name). Stored as `customers.reverse_charge_construction` (DB check `kind = 'foretag'`; `updateCustomer` throws *Omvänd byggmoms gäller bara företagskunder.* for privat). It is an explicit choice - Ferva never infers who is a byggföretag. Effect on invoices: see [Fakturor](#fakturor).
- **Ny kund vs ROT-paket** ([PR #141](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/141)): `#ny-kund-personnummer` is gone. **Ny kund** is name (`#ny-kund-namn`) plus optional e-post / telefon / adress; företag also **Fler uppgifter** (`#ny-kund-kontaktperson`, `#ny-kund-orgnr`, omvänd byggmoms). Personnummer and fastighet are **not** on Ny kund or the identity/edit form (`customer-details-form.tsx`). The kundkort home is the privat **ROT/RUT** block (`SectionTitle` + `CustomerRotSection` on `/kunder/[id]`). Invoice Skattereduktion can also write a personnummer to the customer; quotes only link here.
- **Personnummer:** `#kund-personnummer` via `PersonnummerAutosaveField` in `customer-rot-section.tsx`. Help: *Samma nummer för alla fastigheter. Behövs för ROT/RUT.* Masked in UI. Reveal via `revealCustomerPersonnummerAction`. Never in LLM, URLs, or logs. Invoice Skattereduktion can still collect a personnummer and commit it to the customer on blur (`TaxReductionFields` + `onPersonnummerCommit`); the quote form only shows the stored number or a *Lägg till personnummer* link to this field.
- **No year-cap widget** on the kundkort. The old *Ferva i {år}* / *ROT {kr} av {tak}* / *RUT {kr} av {tak}* card (`RotUsedField`) is gone. **No** editable *ROT använt {år}* input, no *hos andra*, no *Kvar att lova*, no `setCustomerTaxReductionUsedAction` in the UI (the action still exists in `actions.ts` with no caller). `customers.tax_reduction_used` remains in the schema as a legacy jsonb blob; `usedTaxReductionThisYear` does **not** add it (empty third-party is unknown, not 0 used). `remainingTaxReduction` still exists in `tax-reduction-used.ts` for cap math / invoice-quote honesty - see [ROT / RUT](#rot--rut).
- **Fastigheter:** CTA **Lägg till fastighet** (`WorkLocationForm`: address, postal, city, bostadstyp, beteckning / BRF + lägenhet; **no Namn field**). List label from `derivedPropertyLabel` (address or designation). Types: Fastighet/småhus, Bostadsrätt. Unused property: **Ta bort**. If a quote/invoice references it: block with `WORK_LOCATION_IN_USE_MESSAGE` (*Fastigheten är vald på en offert eller faktura och kan inte tas bort.*). Issued invoice freezes ROT in the snapshot, but `workLocationId` still blocks unlink. The same form is reused from the quote/invoice **+ Ny fastighet** footer — ROT still requires the document's own saved `workLocationId`.
- **Related:** Offerter, Fakturor, Uppdrag, ROT/RUT.
- **Components:** `customer-list.tsx`, `new-customer-modal.tsx` (`#ny-kund-namn`, `#ny-kund-epost`, `#ny-kund-telefon`, `#ny-kund-orgnr`, `#ny-kund-kontaktperson` — no `#ny-kund-personnummer`), `customer-details-form.tsx`, `customer-rot-section.tsx` (`#kund-personnummer`), `work-location-form.tsx`, `work-location-label.ts`, `customer-picker.tsx`, `customer-chain-actions.tsx` (`[data-invoice-create]`, `[data-invoice-picker]`), `customer-activity.tsx` (`[data-activity-tabs]`, `[data-activity-list]`, `data-activity-visible` / `data-activity-total`, `button[data-activity-sort]`, `button[data-activity-visa-fler]`), `customer-activity-sort.ts`, `customer-activity-model.ts`.
- **Address fields:** Ny kund (privat + företag), Redigera kund and ROT-bostad (`WorkLocationForm`) all use the shared `AddressFields` autocomplete (`address-input.tsx`) — existing field ids unchanged; picking a suggestion fills gata + postnummer + ort only. See [Address autocomplete](#address-autocomplete-shared).
- **DB:** `customers` (`kind` `privat|foretag`, `reverse_charge_construction`, `tax_reduction_used` leftover), `work_locations`.
- **Invariants:** Kind is required. Org.nr for företag. Personnummer for privat when ROT/RUT is used (on the customer, not on Ny kund). Omvänd byggmoms only on företag.
- **Desktop/mobile:** table vs cards.
- **Live demo customers:** Johan, Brf Eken, Eva, Karin, Anna, Nord Studio, Sara, Bertil, Gläntan, Eli.
- **Verify:** `/kunder` shows 10 rows (`a[href^="/kunder/cust-"]`, incl. `cust-eli`). Click *Anna Andersson* → `/kunder/cust-anna`. Create via `aria-label="Ny kund"` + `#ny-kund-namn` — do **not** expect `#ny-kund-personnummer`. On a privat customer: `SectionTitle` *ROT/RUT*, `#kund-personnummer`, **Lägg till fastighet** - no year-cap widget, no *Ferva i {år}*, no *ROT 0 av 50 000*. Activity ([PR #142](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/142), [PR #159](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/159)): open a seed customer with rows (e.g. Anna) → `[data-activity-tabs]` (**Alla · Offerter · Fakturor · Uppdrag · Betalningar**) → click `button[data-activity-sort=belopp]` → switch filter (e.g. **Betalningar**) and confirm tab Y does not jump / empty filter keeps ~4–6 row min-height / *Ingen aktivitet ännu.* inside `[data-activity-list]`; a full list has no blank Alla slab. When >20 events (or after POST `/api/dev/reset` `{mode:"activity-list"}`), first page shows 20 + **Visa fler** `[data-activity-visa-fler]`. Skapa faktura (main `683593bd` / `723ab705`): Anna → `[data-invoice-create=picker]` open → **Från offert** / **Från uppdrag** / **Fristående** (no **Fristående faktura** label; job hrefs are `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny?job=`, not `/uppdrag/`); a no-chain customer (e.g. Gläntan) → `[data-invoice-create=standalone]`. Tests: `customers.test.ts`, `customer-detail.test.ts`, `customer-activity.test.ts`, `customer-invoice-action.test.ts`, `business-chain.test.ts` §41, `customer-rot-package.test.ts`.

## Uppdrag

- **User-facing name:** Uppdrag
- **Purpose:** The work — registered time/material, invoicing left. Economy status is **not** baked into “Klart”. Jobs are not a project plan: no planned-start field or calendar.
- **Routes:** list `/uppdrag` (`src/app/(app)/uppdrag/page.tsx`, renders `UppdragList` — same filters/business logic as the former Kunder tab). Detail `/uppdrag/[id]` (`src/app/(app)/uppdrag/[id]/page.tsx`). Aliases `/jobb`, `/jobb/[id]`, `/kunder?flik=uppdrag` → redirect. Nav section `uppdrag` is active on both list and detail; crumbs *Uppdrag / {title}*; default back **Uppdrag** → `/uppdrag`. Order detail `/uppdrag/[id]/bestallning/[orderId]` (nav label *Materialbeställning*, back **Uppdraget**, crumbs *Uppdrag / {title} / Beställning FV-{n}*) belongs to [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders).
- **How to get there:** Nav **Uppdrag** (primary, desktop + mobile). Customer *Starta uppdrag*. Quote *Starta uppdrag*. Command *Skapa uppdrag*.
- **Subtitle:** *Vad som är beställt, vad som är fakturerat och vad som är kvar.*
- **Stored status:** `kommande | pagar | klart`. **UI:** Pågår / Klart / Arkiverat (`archivedAt`, not an enum). `startDate` / derived `planerat` stay in the data model for payment-plan gating only — never a list filter, badge, or create-form field.
- **Economy line:** *X kr kvar att fakturera* · *X kr väntar på betalning* · *Betalt ✓* · *Väntar på offert* (incoming lead without money yet).
- **List chips:** Aktiva, Klart, Alla, Arkiverade + Kvar att fakturera / Väntar på betalning / Betalt. Search: *Sök uppdrag, kund, företag eller adress …*
- **Leads / förfrågningar** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)): a website form (`submitContactForm`, `services/website.ts`) creates a job with `source: "web_form"`, `description` = the message and **`jobs.original_message`** (`Job.originalMessage`, plain text - no `requests` table, no inbox item). `isIncomingUnquotedJob` (`services/jobs.ts`) = source `web_form|email|phone|import`, not klart/archived, **no linked quote** - creating or linking a quote is what “handles” the lead. In the list such rows sit under **Aktiva** with the chip **Ny förfrågan** + source line *Via webbformulär* / *Via telefon* / … (`jobSourceLabel`) and the economy cell *Väntar på offert*; there is no separate leads section. On Hem they are attention rows *Nytt uppdrag: {title}* with a secondary **Skapa offert**. Job detail shows the card `[data-testid="job-incoming-message"]`: heading **Ny förfrågan från kunden** (still unquoted) / **Kundens förfrågan** (a quote exists), meta *{Via webbformulär} · {datum, tid}*, the message as a `<blockquote>`, the customer's phone (`tel:`) and e-mail (`mailto:`), and - while unquoted - the quiet line *Meddelandet följer med som beskrivning i offerten.* The card's own **Skapa offert** button was dropped on 2026-09-13: the page header already shows exactly that action as its single primary button for an unquoted job. No reply / mark-handled buttons. Seed: `job-kok` carries a web-form message (card reads *Kundens förfrågan*, quote #110 exists); Karin Ek (*Via telefon*) and Sara Nilsson (*Via webbformulär*) are the two open **Ny förfrågan** rows on driva-alpha.
- **Default sort** (`listJobsForTable`, `services/job-list.ts`; `sortering=` in `job-list-filters.ts`): no param = `standard` → rank 0 `pagar`, rank 1 incoming unquoted leads (newest first), rank 2 the rest via `compareJobsDefault` (non-klart before klart, then start/completed date). Clickable headers **Kund** → `sortering=kund`, **Ekonomi** → `sortering=belopp`; `sortering=datum` is parsed but has no control in the UI.
- **The job page is an economy log, not a field workspace** (narrowed 2026-09-13). Order on `/uppdrag/[id]`: one header row with the title, the description as a quiet subheading (only when it says something the title and the lead message do not), customer + status badge on one line, the address, the one quiet next-step line (`admin.nextStep`, e.g. *Offerten är ett utkast - skicka den när den är klar.*) and, to the right, the single primary button + the **…** menu; then the lead card when there is an incoming message, **Ekonomi** + the linked quote/invoice rows, the ROT banner, **Arbete och material**, **Materialbeställningar** (only when orders exist), the ROT/RUT card, **Anteckningar**. Gone from this page: the timer, *+1 tim*, *Rapportera dagens jobb*, the second **Skapa faktura** under the material list, the **Beskrivning** section (now the subheading), **Ändringar och tillägg**, **Kundvy och slutunderlag**, the **Tidslinje** with its Alla/Kund/Arbete/Ekonomi chips, and **Foton** as a section. Sizing is a requirement, not a nicety: a job with one time row and one draft quote fits on one screen at both 1280x900 and 375x812 (`scripts/verify-uppdrag-ekonomilogg.ts`), so section gaps are `mb-6` and the quote/invoice rows carry their status in the badge only. The field work the page no longer does - timer, notes, photos, receipts and material out on site - is [Fältläge](#fältläge-offline-field-mode-pwa-mobile-shell) at `/falt`, linked from the Uppdrag header. The services, server actions, tables and routes behind all of those are untouched - only the surfaces on this page went away.
- **Detail actions** (`job-controls.tsx`): **exactly one primary button** (`jobHeaderPrimary` in `lib/job-ui-types.ts`): offert i utkast → **Fortsätt offert**; else something billable → **Skapa faktura / delfaktura / slutfaktura** (`[data-testid="job-primary-invoice"]`); else no quote → **Skapa offert**; else **Visa offert**. Header status is the job badge (**Pågår** / **Klart**), not a money state - **Väntar på betalning** is not next to the button (that filter lives on the list / invoice rows). Everything else sits in the single **…** menu (`ActionMenu`): the invoice action *only when it is not already the primary* (so *Skapa faktura* renders at most once on the page), **Avsluta uppdrag** (the existing closeout - see [Avsluta uppdrag](#avsluta-uppdrag-ändringar-betalplan-tidslinje-kundvy-closeout)), **Återöppna uppdrag**, **Redigera uppdrag**, **Foton** (count when `> 0`), **Ta bort** last and destructive. **Ta bort** is enabled only when `jobRemovalPolicy.kind === "delete"` (no issued invoice, no accepted quote that would orphan money); otherwise the item is disabled with one Swedish line (`Uppdraget har en utfärdad faktura.` wins over `Uppdraget har en godkänd offert.`).
- **Ekonomi card on the job** (`jobMoney` / `jobCost` in `services/job-economy.ts`): three headline numbers **Avtalat · Fakturerat · Kvar**. Headline Avtalat / Kvar carry `data-job-avtalat={money.quoteAmount}` / `data-job-kvar={admin.remaining}` (no testid). **Avtalat** (`quoteAmount`) is the accepted governing quote only - a draft or sent-unaccepted quote is 0 kr (its amount is on the quote card as *Offert utkast {kr}*). **Fakturerat** is issued (`invoicedIssued`). **Kvar** is accepted minus invoiced (`remaining`), not draft quote lines and not `registered`. **Registrerat** and **Betalt** are not rendered on this strip (`jobMoney.registered` / `paid` stay in the domain for invoicing and closeout). **Inköp** + **Täckning** still appear when the job has costs. Draft quote/invoice rows in the document list have a trash icon (`DiscardDraftButton` → `discardQuote` / `discardInvoice`); sent, accepted, issued and credit notes do not. Live `job-kok` (accepted quote): Avtalat 85 000 · Fakturerat 25 500 · Kvar 59 500 · Inköp 12 400 · Täckning 13 100 kr.
- **Arbete och material** (`JobWorkSection`, `job-work.tsx`): **one list** of every actual entry (tid, material, resa, övrigt - sorted as `actualEntries` returns them, each row editable unless invoiced) and **one add control** `[data-job-add-entry]` (**Lägg till**). It opens `AddEntrySheet` - a single sheet titled *Lägg till på uppdraget* with a Tid / Material segment (`[data-job-add-material]` is the Material segment). **Tid** and **Material → Lägg till manuellt** reuse `LinesEditor` `variant="single"` (`[data-job-line-editor]`): same DocLine fields (typ, beskrivning, antal, enhet, pris, rabatt, moms). Persist via `addJobWorkEntry` / `jobWorkInputFromDocLine` (rabatt folds into à-priset). Choosing **Material** always opens `AddMaterialSheet` (`add-material-sheet.tsx`, title *Lägg till material*) - it does not swap directly to the wholesaler sheet. Chooser options `[data-add-material-choice]` (value = Swedish label): **Sök och beställ** (only when Grossistbeställningar is on and ≥1 active connection) → `WholesalerMaterialSheet`; **Fota kvitto** → modal `JobReceiptUpload` then redirect to `/bokforing/underlag/{id}/kontrollera`; **Lägg till manuellt** → the Material segment of `AddEntrySheet`. Grossist/kvitto are job-only sources, not on the quote/invoice editor. Work-row invoice chip (`jobWorkInvoiceChipLabel`, still via `entry.invoiceId`): uninvoiced → **Ej fakturerad**; draft → clickable **På utkast · {kr}** (title if no amount) to that draft; issued → clickable **På faktura #{n}**. There is no `PurchaseRefRow` / Inköpsreferens card on this page (opening the job must not mint `job.purchaseRef`). `InvoiceReadinessBlock` `[data-invoice-readiness]` still shows when there is unfinished or uninvoiced material to report. See [Materialkedjan](#materialkedjan-kvitto--faktura--beställning--uppdrag--faktura). Empty copy: *Inget registrerat än.* No timer, no *+1 tim*, no day report, no comparison block, no second invoice button.
- **Foton** (`JobPhotosModal`, `job-photos.tsx`; `services/job-photos.ts`): **…** menu item *Foton* (with a count when there are photos) opens a dialog `[data-testid="job-photos"]`; **Lägg till foto** = hidden `input[type=file][accept="image/*"][capture="environment"]` (not the shared dropzone), empty copy *Inga foton ännu. Ta ett när jobbet är gjort - bevis om kunden ifrågasätter.*, inline `<img>` grid, delete `aria-label="Ta bort foto"`. Successful add toasts **Foto tillagt** (`useToast`) and appends the returned thumb immediately; failure toasts the error and does not add a thumb. Stored **inline** as `jobs.photos` jsonb `JobPhoto[] { id, createdAt, dataUrl, caption? }` - `data:image/*` URLs ≤ 1 800 000 chars, max **24** photos, no bucket, no lightbox (`addJobPhotoAction` / `deleteJobPhotoAction`).
- **Kvitto → material** (`job_work_entries.expense_id`, mig 47): `addJobMaterialFromExpense` (`services/job-work.ts`) adds one material row (qty 1 `st`, unit price = the expense **net** amount, `source: "import"`, description from the expense or *Material från kvitto*) - called only from `bookExpenseToJob` (`services/expenses.ts`), i.e. the assistant's “book this receipt on job X” path; there is no button for it on the job or the expense. Ny utgift's **Uppdrag (valfritt)** (*Kopplas till uppdragets kostnader och lönsamhet.*) only sets `expense.jobId`, which is what *Inköp* reads.
- **Subflows:**
  - Invoice from job: `JobInvoiceModal` + `createInvoiceForJobAction`.
  - Work entries: `job_work_entries` planned vs actual; locked when invoiced.
  - Complete may warn if unbilled work remains.
  - ROT deadline banner `[data-rot-deadline]` above the ROT card (see [ROT / RUT](#rot--rut)).
  - **Delete vs archive** (`jobRemovalPolicy`): empty → hard delete from **Ta bort**; if signed quote / issued invoice / payments / posted books / invoiced work / **sent wholesaler orders** → the menu item is disabled with one reason (server still archives if called).
  - **Materialbeställningar** (`purchase-orders-section.tsx`): compact list of carts/orders for the job, shown only when something exists (also historical orders after the feature is turned off). See [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders).
- **Related:** Kunder, Offerter, Fakturor, ROT card on job when relevant.
- **Components:** `uppdrag-list.tsx`, `uppdrag-form.tsx` (`#nytt-uppdrag-titel`, `#uppdrag-titel`), `job-controls.tsx`, `job-invoice-choice.tsx`, `job-economy-docs.tsx` (`JobEconomyDocs` on the job Ekonomi strip), `job-work.tsx` (one list + add sheet + chooser), `add-material-sheet.tsx`, `invoice-readiness.tsx`, `job-photos.tsx` (dialog behind the **…** menu), `rot-deadline-banner.tsx`, `purchase-orders-section.tsx`. Still in the tree but no longer rendered by the job page: `job-timeline.tsx`, `job-changes-section.tsx`, `customer-share-section.tsx`, `day-report-sheet.tsx` - see the closeout section for where each capability lives now.
- **Empty states:** no jobs → *Inga uppdrag ännu* with **Ny offert** (`/ekonomi/offerter/ny`) + **Skapa uppdrag**; filtered → *Inga uppdrag matchar* + **Rensa sök och filter** (`[data-clear-filters]`).
- **Work address:** new uppdrag *ny adress* = shared `AddressFields` (names `newAddress` / `newPostalCode` / `newCity`); edit uppdrag address = single-line `AddressAutocomplete composeSelected="line"` (`gata, postnummer ort`). See [Address autocomplete](#address-autocomplete-shared).
- **DB:** `jobs` (`original_message`, `source`, `photos` jsonb), `job_work_entries` (`expense_id`). JSON: `housing`, `tax_reduction_application`, `checklist`.
- **Invariants:** Job describes **work**, not money. Quote/invoice linked to a job must share `customer_id`. Timer state is never persisted; only the booked entry is.
- **Desktop/mobile:** table vs cards; row `aria-label={title}`.
- **Live (2026-09-08):** Aktiva = Köksrenovering (Pågår, 59 500 kr kvar) first, then the two **Ny förfrågan** rows (Platsbyggd bokhylla i ek · Karin Ek · *Via telefon*; Byte av köksluckor och bänkskiva · Sara Nilsson · *Via webbformulär*; both *Väntar på offert*), then Altanrenovering, Platsbyggd garderob, Kontorsinredning etapp 2, Fasadarbete … Klart jobs (fönster, etapp 1, …) under **Klart**.
- **Verify:** `/uppdrag` → click *Köksrenovering* → `/uppdrag/job-kok`; sidebar/bottom **Uppdrag** has `aria-current="page"` on both; `[data-testid="job-incoming-message"]` reads *Kundens förfrågan · Via webbformulär*; **Lägg till** → Tid → `[data-job-line-editor]` → hours → a labour row (quoted labour description/price when available, else *Arbete*); **Lägg till** → *Material* → `AddMaterialSheet` chooser → **Lägg till manuellt** for the same single-line editor; **…** → *Foton* → pick an image → toast **Foto tillagt** + thumbnail in the dialog → `aria-label="Ta bort foto"`; exactly one primary button and at most one *Skapa faktura* on the page. Create from header **Uppdrag**. Tests: `job-lifecycle.test.ts`, `job-work.test.ts` (`registerJobTime`), `uppdrag-page.test.ts` (the narrowing + `jobHeaderPrimary`), `nav.test.ts` (huvudnavigation). Browser: `scripts/verify-nav-browser.ts`, `scripts/verify-origin-back.ts`.

## Avsluta uppdrag, ändringar, betalplan, tidslinje, kundvy (closeout)

- **What it is:** the product layer that takes a job from "arbetet är gjort" to "rätt faktura, kunden vet vad som gäller, inget dubbelfaktureras". Branch `cursor/ferva-closeout-61d8`, migration `48_closeout`. Spec: internal `ferva-closeout-spec.md` (Ferva project store). Nothing in this layer ever sends anything: invoices become **utkast**, customer links are copied/shared by the owner, mail is untouched.
- **Binding rules kept:** no writes to `verifications` (invoicing still goes through `issueInvoice` → `postVerification`), `src/lib/accounting/*` and `bas.ts` untouched, Swedish UI, hyphens only.
- **Narrowed on the job page (2026-09-13).** The job detail page was stripped back to an economy log, so four of this layer's surfaces no longer render there. **Nothing in the layer was deleted** - services, server actions, tables, migration 48 and every test are untouched, and the components are still in the tree. Where each one stands now:
  - **Avsluta uppdrag** - moved into the job page's **…** menu. Unchanged otherwise.
  - **Slutunderlag** - still reachable from the closeout flow's last step (**Slutunderlag** → `/uppdrag/[id]/slutunderlag`) and from the customer link.
  - **Ändringar och tillägg** - `job-changes-section.tsx` is no longer rendered. `/uppdrag/[id]/andringar/ny` and `/uppdrag/[id]/andringar/[changeId]` still work and the closeout **Kontrollera jobbet** step still names pending changes, but **there is no button that starts a new change any more**. Give it a home before relying on it.
  - **Kundvy** - `customer-share-section.tsx` is no longer rendered, so the owner has **no UI to open, close or configure a customer link**. Links already shared keep working (`/uppdrag-kund/[token]` and the proxy prefix are untouched) and `enableCustomerShare` / `disableCustomerShare` / `updateCustomerShare` are unchanged.
  - **Tidslinje** and **Rapportera dagens jobb** - no longer rendered; `jobTimeline` / `parseDayReport` / `saveDayReport` and their tests are unchanged.

### Billing allocation (the double-billing guard - owned by this layer, reusable by the material chain)

- **Service:** `src/lib/services/billing-allocation.ts`. **Types:** `BillingAllocation`, `BillingSourceType`, `BillingSourceRef`, `BillingDeferral` in `types.ts`. **Table:** `billing_allocations` (mig 48). **Tests:** `billing-allocation.test.ts`.
- **Shape:** one row = *this source row is (or was) billed on this invoice line*: `{ id, businessId, jobId?, sourceType, sourceId, invoiceId, invoiceLineId, qty?, amountExclVat, status: "draft" | "invoiced" | "released", createdAt, invoicedAt?, releasedAt?, releaseReason?: "utkast_kastat" | "rad_borttagen" | "faktura_krediterad" }`. `sourceType ∈ quote_line | payment_plan_part | quote_remainder | work_entry | change_line | expense | receipt_line | manual`; `sourceId` is the row id in that domain (payment plan part = `${quoteId}:${index}`, remainder = quote id).
- **Invariant:** at most **one live** (`status <> 'released'`) allocation per `(business_id, source_type, source_id)` and per `(business_id, invoice_id, invoice_line_id)` - both as partial unique indexes in SQL **and** as `strictAllocation` / `BillingConflictError` in the service, so JSON mode behaves identically. Draft → `invoiced` when the invoice is issued; → `released` when the draft is discarded, the line removed, or the invoice credited (a credited source becomes billable again).
- **API:** `syncAllocationsWithLines(invoice, refs)` (called from `createInvoice` / line edits - every invoice line with `sourceKind`/`sourceId` provenance gets an allocation), `sourceBillingState(ref)` → `{ status: "unbilled" | "draft" | "invoiced", invoiceId?, invoiceNumber?, allocationId? }` (merges allocations with older provenance - lines without an allocation and `JobWorkEntry.invoiceId` - so nothing counts twice), `allocateSources`, `allocationsForInvoice`, `liveAllocationsForJob`, `markAllocationsInvoiced(invoiceId, at)`, `dropDraftAllocations(invoiceId)`, `releaseAllocationsForInvoice(invoiceId, reason)`, `billingSourceRefForLine(line, invoice)`. Invoice line `sourceKind` gained `CHANGE_LINE` (`sourceId` = the change line id).
- **How the material chain plugs in:** confirmed "Till kunden" article lines become `JobWorkEntry` (`source` import/manual, `documentLineId` + `documentLineAllocationId`). Billing stays on closeout `billing_allocations` with `sourceType: "work_entry"`. Do **not** also allocate `receipt_line` for the same row. `receipt_line` remains reserved. Ask `sourceBillingState({ sourceType: "work_entry", sourceId: entry.id })` before offering "fakturera".

### Betalplan (förskott / del / slutfaktura)

- **Editor:** `payment-plan-editor.tsx` inside the quote form (`doc-form.tsx`). Default = one line *Betalning när arbetet är klart*; secondary button **Lägg till betalplan** (`[data-testid="payment-plan-add"]`) → presets **30 % vid start · 50 / 50 · Förskott i kronor · Tre delar**, parts `[data-testid="payment-plan-part"]` with `kind` forskott | delbetalning | slutbetalning (`PaymentPlanPart.kind`, `amount` in kronor for fixed förskott, last part = the rest), **Ta bort betalplan**. Pure helpers in `src/lib/payment-plan.ts` (`normalizePaymentPlan`, `paymentPlanPartAmount`, `paymentPlanPartKind`, `hasPaymentPlan`; tests `payment-plan.test.ts`). The plan is printed on the customer's quote (`quote-document.tsx`) and is part of what the customer approves.
- **Server calcs:** `nextPaymentPlanPartForQuote` (`business-chain.ts`) + `createPartInvoiceForQuote` account for prior invoices (by `paymentPlanIndex`, then cumulative amount), credits, rounding on the last part, VAT, ROT/RUT, omvänd byggmoms. Part invoices carry `PAYMENT_PLAN` provenance → `payment_plan_part` allocations, so the same part can never be invoiced twice.

### Ändringar och tillägg (job changes with customer approval)

- **Routes:** `/uppdrag/[id]/andringar/ny` (editor), `/uppdrag/[id]/andringar/[changeId]` (owner detail: editor while utkast, share card + **Ny version** while väntar, approval card `[data-testid="job-change-approved"]` when godkänd), public `/andring/[token]` (customer view + **Godkänn ändringen** `[data-testid="public-change-approve"]` with name `[data-testid="public-change-name"]`, **Avböj** with reason). Section component `job-changes-section.tsx` (`[data-testid="job-changes-section"]`, **Ny ändring** `[data-testid="job-change-new"]`, rows `[data-testid="job-change-row"]`) still exists but is **not rendered on the job page** since 2026-09-13 - the two routes above are the only way in.
- **Service:** `services/job-changes.ts` - `createJobChange`, `updateJobChange` (utkast only), `sendJobChange` (locks content, `contentHash`, status `vantar_pa_kunden`), `approveJobChange({ token, name })` (same evidence model as the quote: `approval { approvedAt, approvedByName, customerNameAtApproval, contentHash, statement, ip? }`), `declineJobChange`, `createJobChangeVersion` (new version replaces the old: status `ersatt`, blocked once a line is billed), `deleteJobChange` (utkast only), `approvedJobChanges`, `billableChangeLines`, `changeLineToDocLine` (→ `CHANGE_LINE` provenance). **Statuses (UI labels):** Utkast · Väntar på kunden · Godkänd · Avböjd · Ersatt av ny version, plus derived Delvis fakturerad / Fakturerad from allocations. Tests: `job-changes.test.ts`.
- **Time registered on a change:** `JobWorkEntry.changeId` - the entry shows *Ändring N* instead of *Tillägg* in Arbete och material and is **never** billed on its own (`billsAsExtra`, `job-work.ts`); the customer price is the change's approved lines.
- **Offertavvikelse:** `invoice-quote-deviation.ts` treats lines from an approved change as agreed (added to the approved baseline, excluded from *Tillagt efter offert*) and `PAYMENT_PLAN` / *Slutbetalning …* lines as bundle lines - so a delfaktura carrying an approved change no longer warns. Tests: `invoice-quote-deviation.test.ts`.
- **AI:** the assistant may structure a change text but never invents price, material or time - the editor is the only place lines get numbers.

### Avsluta uppdrag (guided closeout)

- **Entry:** the job detail **…** menu item **Avsluta uppdrag** (the only entry since 2026-09-13; it is never the page's primary button) opens `CloseoutFlow` (`closeout-flow.tsx`). Steps `[data-testid="closeout-step-kontroll|fakturera|satt|klart"]`: **1 Kontrollera jobbet** (checks: öppna checklistepunkter, ändringar som väntar på kunden / utkast, öppna fakturautkast, antal foton, registrerat arbete) → **2 Vad ska faktureras nu?** (grouped items `[data-testid="closeout-item"][data-state=fakturerbar|utkast|fakturerad|hantera_senare|inte_fakturerbart]`: *Enligt offerten* (rest / nästa del), *Godkända ändringar*, *Registrerade tillägg*; per item **Hantera senare** / **Inte fakturerbart** / **Gör fakturerbar igen**) → **3 Faktureringssätt** (`[data-testid="closeout-mode-slutfaktura|delfaktura|lopande|ingen"]`, recommended one badged *Föreslaget*) → **Skapa fakturautkast** (`[data-testid="closeout-create-draft"]`, never sends) → **4 Klart**: **Öppna fakturautkastet**, **Avsluta uppdraget** (`[data-testid="closeout-complete"]`), **Slutunderlag**. An unissued closeout draft is reused (`[data-testid="closeout-existing-draft"]` → *Visa utkastet*); items made billable again while a draft exists are picked up on the next closeout after the draft is sent.
- **Service:** `services/closeout.ts` - `closeoutBasis(jobId)` (items with `sourceType:sourceId` keys, amounts incl. VAT, states from `sourceBillingState` + `job.billingDeferrals`), `closeoutView`, `setBillingDeferral` / `clearBillingDeferral` (persisted on `jobs.billing_deferrals`, events `beslut_*`), `createCloseoutInvoiceDraft(jobId, { mode, selectedKeys? })` (idempotent: returns the open draft; `delfaktura` = next plan part + selected extras; `slutfaktura` = rest + approved changes + extras; `lopande` = extras only), `completeJobCloseout` (`job.closeout.completedAt` + `billingMode`, status klart, event `avslutat`), `reopenJobCloseout` (menu **Återöppna uppdrag**, event `oppnat_igen`), `logCloseoutEvent`. Events (`JobCloseoutEvent`) feed the timeline. Actions in `src/app/closeout-actions.ts`. Tests: `closeout.test.ts`.

### Uppdragstidslinje

- `JobTimeline` (`job-timeline.tsx`) - **not rendered since 2026-09-13**; the component still takes `TimelineEntry[]`, newest first, chips **Alla · Kund · Arbete · Ekonomi** (`[data-testid="timeline-filter-alla|kund|arbete|ekonomi"]`), rows `[data-testid="timeline-entry"][data-category]`, badge **Kunden** for customer-side events, *Visa alla N* / *Visa färre* (`[data-testid="timeline-toggle"]`). Derived on the server by `services/job-timeline.ts` (`jobTimeline(jobId)`) from job, quote (skickad/visad/godkänd), changes, work per day, photos per day, closeout events, invoices and payments - nothing stored, no DB jargon (types in the pure `job-timeline-types.ts`). Tests: `job-timeline.test.ts`.

### Kundvy + slutunderlag (customer share)

- **Owner:** section **Kundvy och slutunderlag** - **not rendered on the job page since 2026-09-13**, so the owner currently has no UI for it (`customer-share-section.tsx`, `[data-testid="customer-share"][data-share-path]`; **Dela med kunden** `customer-share-enable`, checkboxes `customer-share-quote|changes|invoices|paymentStatus|closeoutSummary`, per-photo toggles `customer-share-photo`, **Stäng länken** `customer-share-disable`, link **Förhandsgranska slutunderlag** `closeout-summary-preview` → `/uppdrag/[id]/slutunderlag` (A4 print view, `PdfPrintBar`, `[data-testid="closeout-summary-page"]`, `[data-closeout-summary]`)). Service `services/customer-share.ts`: `enableCustomerShare` (token via `publicToken()`, kept on re-enable, events `kundvy_delad` / `slutunderlag_skapat`), `disableCustomerShare` (`disabledAt`, audit `uppdrag_kundvy_stangd`), `customerShareView(job)`, `ownerCloseoutSummaryView`. Defaults: offert / ändringar / fakturor / betalningsstatus **on**, slutunderlag **off**, photos none.
- **Customer:** `/uppdrag-kund/[token]` (`[data-testid="customer-job-page"]`; sections `customer-quote`, `customer-changes`, `customer-photos`, `customer-invoices`, `customer-payment-status` (fakturerat · betalt · kvar · kommande enligt betalplan), `customer-summary`) and `/uppdrag-kund/[token]/slutunderlag` (404 unless shared). Disabled link → `[data-testid="customer-job-closed"]`. Only explicitly shared content; **never** inköpspriser, marginal, interna anteckningar, bokföring, AI-förslag, grossistrabatter, ej godkända ändringar or unshared photos - asserted by `customer-share.test.ts` (JSON view scanned for `INTERN`, `marginal`, `unitCostOre`, `wholesaler`, `notes`). Proxy `PUBLIC_PREFIXES` includes `/andring` and `/uppdrag-kund`; `demoStateHasToken` handles `job_change` / `job_share`.

### Rapportera dagens jobb (text capture, voice-ready)

- Button **Rapportera dagens jobb** (`[data-testid="day-report-open"]`) - **removed from the Arbete och material header on 2026-09-13**, so `DayReportButton` has no caller; the sheet is unchanged → `DayReportSheet` (`day-report-sheet.tsx`): textarea `day-report-text` → **Tolka** (`day-report-interpret`) → review list `[data-testid="day-report-item"][data-kind=tid|resa|material|andring|anteckning]` with checkboxes, editable description / qty / **price fields the user fills in** → **Spara N poster** (`day-report-save`) → `day-report-saved`. Parsing is local and deterministic (`src/lib/day-report.ts`: Swedish number words, `parseDayReport`) - no provider, nothing leaves the device before save. `saveDayReportAction` → `services/day-report.ts` `saveDayReport` routes through `registerJobTime` (price from the job/quote, never from the text), `addJobWorkEntry(type: "travel")`, `addJobMaterial` (user price or 0), `createJobChange` (empty utkast - priced in the editor), `appendJobNote`; logs event `dagsrapport`. Voice is **not** built (no secure voice architecture in the repo, no new external provider); a dictation later lands in the same textarea. Tests: `day-report.test.ts`.

### Verify (browser)

Reset demo (Inställningar → **Återställ demo**) → `/ekonomi/offerter/ny?kund=cust-johan` → **Lägg till betalplan** → Spara utkast → Skicka offert (demo mock) → open the customer link from the *Fler åtgärder* menu → **Godkänn offert** → job created. There is no **Ny ändring** button on the job page (the section is not rendered): either go to `/uppdrag/[id]/andringar/ny` (the orphan editor still works) → **Skicka till kunden** → `/andring/<token>` → **Godkänn ändringen**, or skip the change. Register time + material with **Lägg till** (`[data-job-add-entry]`) → Tid / Material. Closeout is **…** → **Avsluta uppdrag** (never a header button) → *Hantera senare* on the material → *Delfaktura* → **Skapa fakturautkast** → send the delfaktura → **Avsluta uppdraget** → **…** → **Återöppna uppdrag** → **…** → **Avsluta uppdrag** again → *Gör fakturerbar igen* → *Slutfaktura* → draft carries the material and the deviation card lists only the unapproved extra (if a change was approved). The job page does not render the **Tidslinje** or **Kundvy och slutunderlag** — the owner has no UI there to open a customer share; skip those checks (do not invent a job-page path). Same flow on a 375 px viewport. Screenshots from the run live in the Ferva project store under `media/closeout/`.

## Materialkedjan (kvitto / faktura / beställning → uppdrag → faktura)

- **What it is:** one chain so material can come from a Ferva order, a photographed/uploaded receipt, a supplier invoice, an e-mail confirmation, a purchase outside Ferva, or a manual line - then land on the right job, get a customer price, reach the invoice basis, and book the purchase once. Spec: internal `ferva-material-spec.md`. Migration `58_document_lines` (`20260913190000`). **Not** EDI, punchout, wholesaler API, or stored wholesaler passwords.
- **Chooser:** job **Lägg till** (`[data-job-add-entry]`) → **Material** (`[data-job-add-material]`) opens `add-material-sheet.tsx` with at most three choices (`[data-add-material-choice]`): **Sök och beställ** (only when Grossistbeställningar is on and ≥1 active connection), **Fota kvitto**, **Lägg till manuellt**. No new nav item. Desktop compact; mobile `min-h-11` / `size-11` (44 px).
- **Document lines:** `document_lines` (RLS, unique `(business_id, source, source_document_id, source_index)`). Service `services/document-lines.ts`. Operational split (customer / company / private / other job) lives on `document_lines.allocations` - **not** a second billing table. AI may fill optional `parsed.lines`; `document-line-math.ts` checks sum vs document total (tolerance 1 kr). Deviation → no auto-confirm; the whole receipt can still be booked.
- **Review:** `document-line-review.tsx` on Underlag → Kontrollera. Copy *Ferva hittade N varor för … kr* / *Vilka användes på '…'?*. Per line: Till kunden / Företagskostnad / Annat uppdrag / Privat. Allocations cannot exceed qty/amount. Supplier invoices default **Företagskostnad**.
- **Inbox:** plus-tag `{slug}+FV-1042@in.ferva.se` is a **job candidate after tenant is known**. Tenant still only from the random base-slug (`inboundSlugFromTo` strips `+tag`). UI claims plus-addressing in production only when `INBOUND_PLUS_ADDRESSING=verified`; otherwise *Ange referensen i ämnesraden, till exempel FV-1042.* (`inbox-address.tsx`). Uncertain PO match: `inbox-purchase-match.tsx` (*Fakturan verkar höra ihop med FV-…*, Ja, koppla ihop / Nej / Välj annan). Price file: `inbox-price-file.tsx` previews with the deterministic parser - **Använd nya priser** required; never auto-creates a connection; never sent to an LLM. CSV/TXT/XLSX/XML/ZIP may be stored inline (bucket is PDF/image only).
- **Purchase ref:** stable `purchaseRef` (`FV-n`) is assigned only when something needs one (`ensureJobPurchaseRef` / `ensureJobPurchaseRefAction` - inbox attach, purchase order, fota kvitto from a flow that is not the job page). Not during RSC ([PR #153](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/153)) and **not** because someone opened `/uppdrag/[id]` (the mount `useEffect` + `PurchaseRefRow` were leftover chrome and are gone). Inbox matching (`jobByPurchaseRef`, plus-tag, subject ref) still works against an existing ref. Same sequence as purchase orders (`ferva-reference.ts`). Not assigned in `createJob`. Seed jobs `job-kok` / `job-fasad` / `job-garderob` start without a ref.
- **Customer price:** `material-price.ts` hierarchy explicit → job rule → customer rule → connection → file sales price → missing. Missing price never becomes a 0 kr invoice line (`confirmDocumentLines` skips, `billableWorkEntryIds` filters).
- **Compare:** `wholesalers/compare.ts` only on E-number, RSK, GTIN, or exact manufacturer article + manufacturer. UI says *Lägst jämförbart pris*, not "billigast". Pack-normalized unit price on the card when `packSize > 1`. Separate carts; badge `Ahlsell 3 · Dahl 2` (`draftCartsBadge`).
- **Price list refresh:** settings **Be om ny prislista** → mailto draft (`price-list-request.ts`, Reply-To = Ferva inbox). Sending the mail does **not** mark the list updated. Stale copy *Priserna kan vara gamla* (`PRICE_LIST_STALE_DAYS` = 90).
- **Invoice readiness:** `invoice-readiness.ts` + `invoice-readiness.tsx` on the job. Blockers only: missing customer price, uncertain line, confirmation price deviation. Unfinished material stays on the job. ROT/RUT: MATERIAL is never eligible (`isTaxReductionEligible`).
- **AI:** `hintFromModelJson` is total - broken JSON → `undefined` → manual review. Never invents article numbers, amounts, VAT, or customer price. Personnummer / full documents / full price files are not sent to an LLM when a parser is enough.
- **Tests:** `document-line-math.test.ts`, `document-lines.test.ts`, `document-match.test.ts`, `inbox-plus-tag.test.ts`, `inbox-price-file.test.ts`, `invoice-readiness.test.ts`, `material-price.test.ts`, `wholesaler-compare.test.ts`, `receipt-bank-match.test.ts`, `extract-document-fallback.test.ts`, `material-rot.test.ts`, `material-capabilities.test.ts`, `material-ui.test.ts`. `scripts/db-validate.ts` + `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` cover `document_lines` RLS / unique source / tenant isolation.
- **Out of scope (honest):** EDI, API, punchout, scraping/auto-login on wholesaler sites, live cross-catalog compare on every product card (engine compares when identifiers match), automatic plus-addressing in production UI until verified.

## Fältläge (offline field mode), PWA, mobile shell

Spec §9. The only part of the app that works without network. Client is never the source of truth.

- **Route** `/falt` (`src/app/(app)/falt/page.tsx` → `FieldMode` in `src/components/field-mode.tsx`), linked from the Uppdrag header (**Fältläge**) and the manifest shortcut. Route meta in `nav.ts` (section `uppdrag`, back to `/uppdrag`). Demo sessions get a stub card (no binding).
- **Local store** (`src/lib/offline/idb.ts`): IndexedDB `ferva-offline` with `meta` (binding, cached jobs, active timer, non-extractable AES-GCM `CryptoKey`), `mutations` (payload encrypted; `status/kind/seq` plain for indexing) and `blobs` (photos/receipts, encrypted). No WebCrypto ⇒ stored plain and the field mode shows a red notice.
- **Queue logic** (`src/lib/offline/queue.ts`, pure): `nextBatch` (seq order, backoff, dependency gating on `localId` producers), `resolveLocalRefs`, `applyResult`, `applyTransportFailure` (2 s→5 min, `MAX_AUTO_ATTEMPTS = 8` then `failed`), `retryNow`, `bindingDecision` (bind/keep/wipe), `minimizeJob` (id, title, customerName, status, address, cachedAt - nothing else), `pruneSynced`.
- **Client adapter** (`src/lib/offline/client.ts`): `ensureBinding`, `enqueue(kind, payload, {blob, entityVersion})`, `syncNow` (single-flight; inlines blobs as dataUrl/base64; wipes the store on `signed_out`/`forbidden`; keeps queue on `read_only`), `retryMutation`, `discardMutation`, `subscribeOffline`.
- **Kinds** (`types.ts`): `work_time`, `work_note`, `job_photo`, `receipt`, `material`, `customer_draft`, `job_draft`. Capabilities per kind in `CAPABILITY_BY_KIND` (`change_jobs` / `manage_customers` / `write_accounting`).
- **Server** `POST /api/offline/sync` (`src/app/api/offline/sync/route.ts` → `applyOfflineBatch` in `src/lib/offline/server.ts`) runs inside `withBusiness` (write). Per item: parse → capability (`can(role, …)`) → idempotency lookup → `jobGate` (job missing ⇒ conflict; job `klart` while cached as open ⇒ conflict) → apply through `registerJobTime` / `appendJobNote` / `addJobPhoto` / `addJobMaterial` / `createCustomer` / `createJob` / `storeInboxAttachment`+`ingestUploadedDocument` (no AI interpretation) → receipt row in `offline_mutations` (migration 56, unique `(business_id, idempotency_key)`, immutable, platform-context RLS; JSON mode: `platformRegistry().offlineMutations`). Responses: 401 `signed_out`, 403 `forbidden`, 423 `read_only`, 400 `bad_request`, 500 `server_error`.
- **Wipe points**: `LogoutRow` (before `logoutAction`), `PwaRegister session={null}` on `/login`, binding mismatch in the app layout, and 401/403 from sync.
- **PWA**: `src/app/manifest.ts` (`/manifest.webmanifest`, shortcuts to `/falt`, `/uppdrag`, `/bokforing`), icons in `public/icons/` (placeholders, `scripts/generate-pwa-icons.ts`), SW served by `/sw.js` (`src/app/sw.js/route.ts`, version = `appRelease()`), source in `src/lib/pwa/service-worker-source.ts` (allowlist: `/_next/static/*` cache-first, icons/manifest/`/offline` stale-while-revalidate, navigations network-only with `/offline` fallback; everything else - `/api`, auth, admin, redovisning, offert/faktura/andring/uppdrag-kund tokens, RSC/prefetch - bypassed). `PwaRegister` registers only in production or `NEXT_PUBLIC_PWA_DEV=1`. `ConnectivityStatus` (pill above the bottom nav) uses `useOffline` + queue summary and auto-syncs on `online`. Public paths added to `proxy.ts`: `/manifest.webmanifest`, `/sw.js`, `/offline`, `/icons`.
- **Mobile shell**: `mobile/` (Capacitor 7, `server.url` from `FERVA_APP_URL`), excluded from tsc/eslint, own `package.json`; not store-ready - checklist in `mobile/README.md`.
- **Tests**: `src/lib/offline/offline.test.ts` (queue, binding, minimisation, server idempotency/conflict/capability/receipt, SW policy via `node:vm`).
- **Verify (browser)**: log in → Uppdrag → **Fältläge** → tick a job → DevTools *Network: Offline* → **Starta tid** … **Stoppa** (≥ 15 min or use *Timmar*), *Spara anteckning*, *Ta foto* → queue rows show **Väntar** and the pill says *Offline · N ändringar väntar* → go online → pill turns *Synkar…* then disappears; the job's *Arbete och material* lists the time and note. Mark the job **Klart** in another tab, queue another time entry offline, go online → row shows **Konflikt** with the Swedish message and *Försök igen / Ta bort*.
