## Offerter (Quotes)

- **User-facing name:** Offerter (tab). Detail: **Offert #{n}**
- **Purpose:** Price and scope the job; the customer accepts it on the public link by typing their name and pressing **Godkänn offert**. Accepting is not payment.
- **Feature key:** `offer.accept_simple` — **core / icp_loop** (map / product vocabulary only; there is no such constant in code). One canonical service `acceptQuote(token, name)` (`src/lib/services/quote-accept.ts`), reached only via the public server action `acceptQuoteByTokenAction`. The AI has no accept tool; the owner cannot accept for the customer.
- **Tombstone:** customer-facing **BankID accept is removed** (`bankid-flow.tsx`, `/api/bankid/*`, `BankIDApproval`). `src/lib/services/bankid.ts` (MockBankIDProvider) remains as a dead hook: demo-gated, no route calls it, and its `finalizeApproval` delegates to the same `finalizeQuoteAcceptance`. Never re-add a BankID button, "e-legitimation", "avancerad underskrift", a drawn signature or a personnummer gate on the accept path.
- **Routes:**
  | Route | Page |
  |-------|------|
  | `/ekonomi?flik=offerter` (default Ekonomi tab) | `ekonomi/page.tsx` + `QuoteRegister` |
  | `/ekonomi/offerter/ny` | new |
  | `/ekonomi/offerter/[id]` | detail |
  | `/ekonomi/offerter/[id]/redigera` | edit |
  | `/offert/[token]` | public customer |
  | `/offert/[token]/pdf` | Commercial offer PDF (A4). Once accepted: quiet line *Godkänd {datum} av {namn}* — no green box |
  | `/offert/[token]/underlag` | Evidence certificate *Intyg om godkännande av offert* (lawyer-readable; hashes in collapsed *Teknisk kontroll*) |
  | `/offert/[token]/underlag/pdf` | Certificate print/PDF (A4). Same facts; hashes only in a short footer |
- **How to get there:**
  - Nav **Ekonomi** (lands on Offerter).
  - Header **Ny offert** (`aria-label="Ny offert"`).
  - Customer / job / command *Skapa offert*.
  - Query: `?kund=`, `?job=`, `?tillaggFran=`.
- **Statuses (domain → UI):**
  | Domain | Badge |
  |--------|-------|
  | `utkast` | Utkast |
  | `skickad` | Väntar på godkännande (list: *Öppnad · väntar på godkännande* if viewed) |
  | `godkand` | Godkänd (timeline/underlag: *Godkänd av {namn}* via `acceptedByLabel`) |
  | `avbojd` | Avböjd |
  | `utgangen` | Utgången (**derived** when sent + `validUntil` passed) |
- **Filters:** Alla, Utkast, Väntar på godkännande, Godkända, Avböjda, Utgångna.
- **Main actions — utkast:** **Redigera** · **Kasta utkast** · **Skicka offert** on one header row (`flex shrink-0 flex-nowrap …`; draft does not wrap these in `PageActions`). Checklist `#quote-send-blockers` *Innan offerten kan skickas* is **hard blockers only** (company settings, ROT/RUT, title/lines/payment plan/valid-until — `quoteHardSendBlockers` / `isQuoteContactSoftBlocker`). `buyer_email` is filtered out, so *Lägg till e-post* is **not** in this list ([PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165)). Soft contact gaps are an under-title banner plus e-post/telefon fields in the Skicka dialog — not inline fields + banners as a `QuoteDraftSend` card. Existing draft rules stay (not forced into one primary + …).
- **Draft send header + modal** (`QuoteDraftSend`, `quote-draft-send.tsx`; helpers `src/lib/quote-send-contact.ts`; [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) added the message, the ROT callout and the share row; [PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165) made contact a soft gap and added SMS / channel choice; [PR #167](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/167) moved contact into the Skicka dialog and flattened the draft header): no floating contact card (`min-w-[18rem]` / `shadow-card` beside the header is gone). Header left: title, status, subtitle; when draft + `quoteContactGapCopy(...).banner`, a soft-gap banner under the subtitle (plain `<p>`, no id). Header **Skicka offert** opens the dialog when hard blockers are gone (`canSend` / `quoteHardSendBlockers`); missing e-post/telefon alone does **not** disable the header button. Hard blockers still disable header Skicka with title *Komplettera uppgifterna ovan innan offerten kan skickas.* Contact fields live **inside** the Skicka `Modal`: **E-post** `#offert-skicka-epost-{documentId}` (placeholder *namn@exempel.se*) and **Telefon** `#offert-skicka-telefon-{documentId}` (placeholder *070-123 45 67*). Blur and send persist onto the customer (`updateCustomerDetailsAction` / `sendQuoteAction` → `resolveCustomerEmail` + `resolveCustomerPhone` with overwrite). Typed values count. Helper *Uppgifterna sparas på {kundnamn}.* Error ids `#offert-skicka-epost-fel-{documentId}` / `#offert-skicka-telefon-fel-{documentId}`. Soft-gap banners (`quoteContactGapCopy`; no id, under the draft title): both missing → *Kunden saknar e-post och telefon. Fyll i minst en när du skickar - det sparas på kunden.*; email only → *Kunden saknar e-post. Du kan skicka med SMS eller fylla i adressen när du skickar.*; phone only → *Kunden saknar telefon. Du kan skicka med e-post eller fylla i numret när du skickar.*; both filled → no banner. Never "kan inte skicka". Modal confirm **Skicka offert** is enabled only when e-post **or** telefon is present/typed (`quoteSendButtonEnabled` with `hardBlockers: 0` inside the modal) **and** selected channel(s) are ready (`selectedReady`). Otherwise grey with title/helper *Fyll i e-post eller telefon för att skicka.* Confirm `Modal` title *Skicka offert* (not *Skicka offert?*) with customer name, amount, *Giltig till {datum}*, the ROT/RUT callout `[data-rot-share]` (*Kunden betalar {belopp}. ROT {avdrag} begärs hos Skatteverket när arbetet är klart och kundens del är betald.*, `RotCustomerShareCallout`). Radiogroup `role="radiogroup"` `aria-label="Hur ska offerten skickas"`: **E-post** / **SMS** / **E-post och SMS** (`role="radio"`, no `data-*`). Default `defaultQuoteSendChoice`: both contacts → both, phone only → SMS, else e-post. A channel is enabled only when that contact is filled (`quoteChannelEnabled`). Descriptions: e-post on → *Skickas till {e-post}* or *E-post är inte konfigurerad i den här miljön - offerten markeras som skickad.*; e-post off → *Fyll i e-post ovan först*; SMS on → *Öppnar SMS till {telefon}*; SMS off → *Fyll i telefon ovan först*; both on → *Skickar mejl och öppnar SMS med kundlänken*; both off → *Fyll i både e-post och telefon*. Textarea **Personligt meddelande** only when the choice is **not** SMS-only (placeholder *Valfritt - syns överst i mejlet.*; no id, no max length; passed as `sendAction({ message, channels, email, phone })` → `quoteEmail({ message })` on the mail path, rendered at the top of the customer mail, **not** stored on the quote). SMS-only (`channels: ["sms"]`): `sendQuoteWithEmail` skips the e-post requirement, marks the quote `skickad` when phone exists, and the UI opens SMS with the customer link (`smsShareHref`). Email / both still use the mail path (`assertQuoteReadyToSend` when e-post is in the channels). A **Förhandsgranska och dela** row = `ShareCustomerLink` (Kopiera kundlänk · Dela · SMS - it is the public link, not a rendered mail preview), **Avbryt** / **Skicka offert** (*Skickar ...*, *Försök igen*). Success → `router.replace(detailHref + ?skickad=1|manuell|demo)`. Invoice send (`InvoiceDraftSend`) is **unchanged** by #165/#167: same ROT callout and share row, **no** personal message field, and `#invoice-send-blockers` still treats `buyer_email` as a hard blocker. The customer mail carries the attachment `offert-{n}.pdf` built by `quotePdfBytes` → `buildSimplePdf` (`src/lib/pdf/simple-pdf.ts`, real PDF 1.4 bytes - a one-page summary that points to the link, not the A4 offer document, which stays at `/offert/[token]/pdf`).
- **Automatic follow-up** (`runAutomaticReminders` in `src/lib/services/automatic-reminders.ts`, tests `automatic-reminders.test.ts`): a `skickad` quote that is still valid, has no `followUps` yet and was sent ≥ **7 days** ago gets one `followUpQuoteByEmail` (same subject shape as the manual **Skicka påminnelse**: *Påminnelse: offert från {företag} – {rubrik}*). Driven by `GET /api/cron/reminders` - allowlisted in the proxy since [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134); the handler still answers 401 without `CRON_SECRET`, see [Fakturor](#fakturor). No opt-out setting exists.
- **Action bar matrix (sent / accepted / declined)** (`QuoteOwnerPageActions`, wrapper `[data-quote-owner-actions]`, primary Fakturera `[data-quote-owner-fakturera]`): never two `…` menus. Visible = **one primary + one …** (`aria-label="Fler åtgärder"`). Customer name in the subtitle is an `AppLink` to `/kunder/{id}` — **Öppna kundvyn is not in the bar**. **Ny version** and **Dra tillbaka offerten** are overflow-only. Since [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) the overflow always starts with **Kopiera offert** and the `ShareCustomerLink` items (**Kopiera kundlänk** · **Dela** (only when `navigator.share` exists) · **SMS**), verified on driva-alpha for #115 / #110:
  | State | Primary | Overflow (in order) |
  |-------|---------|----------|
  | `skickad` (not accepted) | **Skicka påminnelse** (existing) | Kopiera offert · Kopiera kundlänk · (Dela) · SMS · Skriv ut / PDF · Ny version · **Dra tillbaka offerten** (`[data-quote-withdraw]`) |
  | `godkand`, no job | **Starta uppdrag** | Kopiera offert · Kopiera kundlänk · (Dela) · SMS · Skriv ut / PDF · Ny version · Intyg om godkännande |
  | `godkand`, job linked | **Fakturera** (`createInvoiceFromQuote`; hidden when fully invoiced) | Kopiera offert · Kopiera kundlänk · (Dela) · SMS · Skriv ut / PDF · Ny version · Intyg om godkännande |
  | `avbojd` | — | Kopiera offert · Kopiera kundlänk · (Dela) · SMS · Skriv ut / PDF · Ny version |
- **Kopiera offert** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)): `duplicateQuoteAction` → `duplicateQuote()` (`services/quotes.ts`) creates a **new utkast immediately** (no `?kopieraFran=` form) and redirects to `/ekonomi/offerter/{copy.id}`: same customer, job/work location, ROT, payment plan, terms and rich text, lines re-id'd, title prefixed *Kopia av …*, fresh validity from settings.
- **Dela kundlänk without a mail provider** (`ShareCustomerLink`, `src/components/share-customer-link.tsx`; pure helpers `customerShareText` / `smsShareHref` / `canUseWebShare` in `src/lib/customer-link-share.ts`, tests `customer-link-share.test.ts`): **Kopiera kundlänk** (`CopyLinkButton`, toast *✓ Kundlänken är kopierad* + *Klistra in den i ett sms eller mejl till kunden.*), **Dela** = `navigator.share({ title, text, url })` (hidden without Web Share; failure → toast *Kunde inte öppna delning*), **SMS** = `<a href="sms:{digits}?body={text}%0A{url}">` (no phone → `sms:?body=…`). Text = *Offert från oss* / *Faktura från oss* (*Offert #N från oss* / *Faktura #N från oss* when the caller passes the number - the send modals and the invoice overflow do, the quote overflow does not; the invoice overflow also prefills the customer's phone: `sms:086402210?body=Faktura%20%231042…` on #1042). The server renders the href with the placeholder origin `https://placeholder.local` and rewrites it with `window.location.origin` **on click** - do not assert the href before clicking. Sharing never changes the status; only the send path marks `skickad`. Rendered in the quote/invoice send modals, the quote overflow and the invoice overflow. No selectors.
- Never show **Starta uppdrag** and **Skapa faktura/Fakturera** together. No **Nästa steg** card that repeats the job or a second invoice CTA. **Kopplat till** is the only job card (name + Pågår/Klart). Unlinked draft: Inte kopplat + Koppla / Skapa uppdrag (`documentLinkView`).
- **Dra tillbaka** (`withdrawQuote`): allowed only when `status === "skickad"` (viewed or not) and not accepted. Reuses domain status **`avbojd`** with `declineReason = "Tillbakadragen"` (Hem **Inte aktuell** uses `"Inte längre aktuell"`). Confirm dialog; overflow item; idempotent if already owner-withdrawn. Does not delete versions/snapshots. No email required. Public: *Offerten är tillbakadragen* (not “Avböjd” — that word is the customer’s decline). Owner list leaves *Väntar på godkännande* (badge Avböjd); the owner timeline row reads *Tillbakadragen* instead of the avböjd label (`isQuoteWithdrawnByOwner`). Accept blocked (`declined`). Not allowed on `godkand`.
- **Ny version / supersede-on-accept:** overflow when a sent or accepted version exists. Editing a **sent, not accepted** quote still reverts to utkast and must be resent (replacing the waiting snapshot is OK). Editing an **accepted** quote creates/updates a pending unlocked version **without** changing `currentVersionId`, `status`, or the acceptance — the locked snapshot stays governing (hash, job, intyg). Sending the pending draft snapshots it; public then shows that waiting version + one Godkänn form (not the old accepted banner). When the new version is accepted, `finalizeQuoteAcceptance` locks it, replaces the one `signatures` row, sets `currentVersionId`, `createJobFromQuote` (idempotent — no second job).
- **Acceptance banner (owner):** compact one line: *Godkänd av {namn} · {tid} · Intyg om godkännande* (`/offert/[token]/underlag`). No IP, user-agent, SHA-256, method essay, emails, or “Version N är låst…” paragraph. Header shows **Godkänd** only - no *Version N låst* badge next to Offert #N. Forensics live on the intyg page / PDF.
- **Public `/offert/[token]`:** utkast → **404**. The A4 **QuoteDocument** is the commercial offer only (villkor + seller footer; once accepted a *quiet* line *Godkänd {datum} av {namn}* — **no green in-card Godkänd box**). **No CTA inside the card** — no **Godkänn offerten** block, no name field, no accept button. Accept chrome sits **below** the card: validity *Offerten är giltig till …*, **Ditt namn** (prefilled: person name, or company contact person; editable; button disabled while blank), one sentence *Genom att godkänna accepterar du offerten “{rubrik}” från {företag} daterad {datum} till ett totalt belopp om {totalt}.* (ROT/RUT adds *, varav … preliminärt ROT/RUT-avdrag*), primary **Godkänn offert** (`data-testid=public-quote-accept`) — real submit, **no chevron / no jump link**, footnote *Godkännandet sparas tillsammans med offertens innehåll och tidpunkt.*, quiet **Avböj offerten** (`public-quote-decline`). Mobile sticky (`md:hidden`, `data-quote-accept-bar`, `public-quote-accept-bar`) repeats only that submit (and the name field if empty) — same `acceptQuote` call, not a second form inside the card. Footer *Skriv ut eller spara som PDF* (`/pdf`, commercial offer) stays outside the document; once accepted also *Skriv ut intyg om godkännande* (`/underlag/pdf`). **No “Ställ en fråga”.** No BankID button, no draw-to-sign, no checkbox. First open → `viewedAt` / *Öppnad av kunden*.
- **Accept states:** success → inline *Offerten är godkänd* + receipt (who, when, amount) then `router.refresh()` + `announceLiveRefresh("quote-accepted")` (other tabs in the same browser, see below) → **one** server banner *Offerten är godkänd* / *Godkänd av {namn}, {tid} · {belopp}* + **Intyg om godkännande**; document shows the quiet *Godkänd {datum} av {namn}* line (not a second green box); **already accepted** = read-only, no second accept unless a newer sent version is waiting (then one Godkänn form for that version); **avbojd** → *Offerten är tillbakadragen* if owner-withdrawn (`isQuoteWithdrawnByOwner`) else *Offerten är avböjd*; **expired** → Swedish explanation, no form; **utkast** → 404 (also for the action: `not_found`).
- **Owner live refresh after the customer's decision** ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127)): the public **Godkänn offert** (`quote-accept.tsx`) and **Avböj offerten** (`quote-public-actions.tsx`) call `announceLiveRefresh("quote-accepted" | "quote-declined")` after success (silent no-op without `BroadcastChannel`). The owner's register (`/ekonomi?flik=offerter`) and detail (`/ekonomi/offerter/[id]`) then show **Godkänd** / **Avböjd** without a full navigation: at once via the `driva-live-refresh` channel when the owner tab is in the **same browser** (the demo), otherwise when the owner returns to the tab - browser Back/Forward, bfcache restore, or refocus after ≥ 1 s away - through `LiveRefresh` in the app layout ([Layout](#layout)). The server was already consistent ([PR #89](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/89), `demo_sessions` + `state_version`); what remained was the client: the App Router restores Back/Forward from its cache with zero server fetch, an open tab never re-reads on its own, and the `revalidatePath("/", "layout")` that `acceptQuoteByTokenAction` / `declineQuoteByTokenAction` run only reaches the customer's tab. No server / storage change in #127.
- **Certificate `/offert/[token]/underlag`:** title *Intyg om godkännande av offert*. 4–6 sentence Swedish summary (who, offer number + rubrik, company, total, date/time, method = typed name + **Godkänn offert** on the sent link). Facts table in this order: Avsändare, Kund, Godkänd av, Tidpunkt, Offert, Belopp — hashes are not first. *Det kunden såg* = stored `statement`. Status *Dokumentet är oförändrat* / *har ändrats* (plain Swedish; `acceptance.contentHash` vs `quoteVersionHash(version)`). Native `<details>` **Teknisk kontroll** collapsed on web (version, SHA-256, hash now, method, send-to email, IP + device). Closing sentence: enkel elektronisk underskrift, ingen e-legitimation. Primary action **Skriv ut eller spara som PDF** → `/underlag/pdf`. Same stored evidence fields; presentation only. Owner detail does **not** repeat these forensics.
- **Certificate PDF `/offert/[token]/underlag/pdf`:** A4 `@page`, `PdfPrintBar`, no SaaS chrome. Same facts and summary; hashes only in a short footer. Demo generates the PDF view; no email.
- **Accept service (`acceptQuote`):** rate limit (10/token, 40/IP per 10 min) → token lookup (utkast = not_found) → idempotent return if already accepted → `normalizeAcceptName` (trim, collapse, ≤120; empty → `name_required`) → status (`declined` / `expired` / `not_acceptable`) → `expectedContentHash` from the rendered page must equal `quoteVersionHash(version)` (`changed`) → `finalizeQuoteAcceptance`: sets seller/buyer snapshots, `lockedAt`, `contentHash`, pushes the `QuoteAcceptance` (`method: simple_accept`, `acceptedAt`, `acceptedByName`, `customerNameAtAccept`, `acceptedByEmail`, `contentHash`, `statement`, `ip`, `userAgent`, `linkSentTo`), `status = godkand`, `decidedAt`, `createJobFromQuote` (idempotent — never a second job), `logActivity`, one `save()`. Errors are `QuoteAcceptError` with Swedish `QUOTE_ACCEPT_TEXT`.
- **Confirmation mail:** `prepareQuoteAcceptedNotices` returns 0–2 envelopes (customer `quote_accepted_customer` + business `quote_accepted`). Empty in demo / `is_demo` / no mail provider. Sent with `after()` so the customer never waits on Resend; **either mail failing never blocks the accept**. Customer copy: typed name + Godkänn offert; links to `/offert/[token]` and `/underlag`. No BankID language.
- **Customer e-mail subjects** ([PR #101](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/101); `src/lib/email/rubrik.ts`, `templates.ts`, `services/document-mail.ts`): **Skicka offert** → `Offert från {företag} – {rubrik}`; **Skicka påminnelse** (`followUpQuoteByEmail`) → `Påminnelse: offert från {företag} – {rubrik}`. Rubrik = the current version's `title`, collapsed to one line and cut at 120 chars with `…` (`collapseSubjectRubrik`, `SUBJECT_RUBRIK_MAX`). **No `#n` in the subject** — the offer number stays in the body. ` – {rubrik}` is dropped when the title is empty or only a document-type word (*Offert* / *Faktura* …) (`documentFromCompanySubject` / `reminderFromCompanySubject`). Demo / `is_demo` only simulates the send, but the prepared `MailMessage` has the same subject.
- **Delete rules (critical):**
  - **Kasta utkast** only if `status === "utkast"` and no issued invoices linked.
  - Sent: *Skickade offerter kan inte kastas. Markera dem som inte aktuella i stället.*
  - `discardQuote` unlinks draft invoices/jobs; deletes versions, acceptances (`signatures`), legacy bankid orders.
  - Redirect: `/ekonomi?flik=offerter&kastat=offert` (toast via `FlashToast`, see [Layout](#layout)).
  - There is **no** hard delete for sent/signed quotes.
- **Related:** ROT on form; job link (`LinkedToBox` — **Kopplat till** is the only job card; do not add a second **Nästa steg** that repeats the job). Invoices from the quote appear under **Fakturor**. Next invoice is the header **Fakturera** (same `createInvoiceFromQuote` as the old per-part chip).
- **Components:** `economy-register.tsx`, `doc-form.tsx` (QuoteForm; order = header → Prisrader → `DocDescriptionCard` → Skattereduktion; Bostad via `tax-reduction-document-property.tsx` / `SearchableCombobox`), `discard-draft-button.tsx`, `quote-draft-send.tsx` + `src/lib/quote-send-contact.ts` (contact in Skicka dialog + channel choice, [PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165), [PR #167](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/167)), `send-checklist.tsx`, `quote-document.tsx` (quiet acceptance line only — no `acceptForm` slot, no green Godkänd box), `acceptance-certificate.tsx` (web + A4 PDF), `quote-chain-actions.tsx` (`QuoteOwnerPageActions` = one primary + one overflow on owner detail, incl. **Kopiera offert**), `share-customer-link.tsx` + `copy-button.tsx` (Kopiera kundlänk · Dela · SMS), `rot-customer-share.tsx` (`[data-rot-share]` callout in the send modals), `lines-editor.tsx` (see [Fakturor](#fakturor) for the shared line editor: rabatt, rubrikrader, **Från register**), `quote-accept.tsx` (`QuoteAcceptForm` below the card + mobile sticky submit), `quote-public-actions.tsx` (Avböj only) - both announce `quote-accepted` / `quote-declined` on the `driva-live-refresh` channel after success (`src/lib/live-refresh.ts`); the listener `LiveRefresh` lives in the app layouts, not on the public page.
- **Form ids:** `#offert-saknas`, `#offert-kund`, `#offert-rubrik`, `#offert-rot-rut`, `#offert-fastighet` (`#offert-fastighet-val` Bostad combobox; RUT `#offert-fastighet-adress`), `#offert-betalplan`, `#prisrader`; draft send: `#offert-skicka-epost-{documentId}`, `#offert-skicka-telefon-{documentId}` (errors `-fel-`); public accept: `#godkann-offert`, `#godkann-namn`.
- **DB:** `quotes`, `quote_versions` (payload JSONB is hash-frozen), `signatures` (= acceptances; migration 28 adds `method`, makes `order_ref` / `signer_personal_number_masked` / `environment` nullable; `evidence` JSONB holds contentHash, statement, customerNameAtAccept, acceptedByEmail, ip, userAgent, linkSentTo; `signatures_quote_uq` keeps one per quote; `apply-pending-schema.ensureQuoteAcceptanceSchema` mirrors it), `bankid_orders` (legacy).
- **Invariants:** Locked versions immutable — a sent/accepted quote is a snapshot. Later edits of an **accepted** quote create a pending version and **never** change what was accepted until the new version is accepted (`pendingDraftQuoteVersion` / `publicQuoteVersion` / `governingQuoteVersion` in `data.ts`). Public only via unguessable `token`. Totals panel says **Offertvärde**, not Att betala. Quote↔job same customer. Accept never requires personnummer; ROT fields only when ROT/RUT is on the document. ROT send requires personnummer + a `workLocationId` saved on the quote (customer having fastigheter is not enough); RUT send requires personnummer only. Demo/`is_demo` accept makes zero external HTTP.
- **Desktop/mobile:** register table + cards. Form: sticky save on mobile (`DocStickyActions`). Public: accept chrome below the document; mobile sticky repeats **Godkänn offert** (real submit, safe-area).
- **Live draft:** Offert **#116** `/ekonomi/offerter/quote-bokhylla` — ROT blockers: personnummer + bostad. Public `/offert/demo-eva-bokhylla` is **not** viewable. Acceptable public: `/offert/demo-bertil-fasad`.

### How an agent verifies (quote delete / send / accept)

**Discard draft**

1. `/demo` → Ekonomi → Offerter.
2. Open **#116** / `quote-bokhylla`.
3. Button *Kasta utkast* `[data-testid=discard-draft-trigger]` (`aria-label` on icon variant: *Kasta offertutkast*).
4. Dialog *Kasta offertutkast?* → *Kasta utkast*.
5. Land on Offerter with toast; #116 gone. Sent rows still listed.

**Do not expect a delete control on #115 / #110.** Use owner overflow **Dra tillbaka offerten**, Hem **Inte aktuell**, or public **Avböj offerten**. All three set `avbojd`; owner withdraw / Inte aktuell use carpenter `declineReason` so the public page does not claim the customer declined.

**Send**

1. New offert or fix #116 blockers (Eva: `#kund-personnummer` in ROT/RUT on `/kunder/cust-eva`, then pick **Bostad** on the quote — `#offert-fastighet-val`; customer having properties is not enough).
2. Header *Skicka offert* opens the dialog when `#quote-send-blockers` is empty (`canSend` / `quoteHardSendBlockers`); missing e-post/telefon alone does **not** disable it. Fill `#offert-skicka-epost-{id}` / `#offert-skicka-telefon-{id}` **inside the Skicka modal** after opening header Skicka. Modal confirm *Skicka offert* is enabled when e-post **or** telefon is present/typed (`quoteSendButtonEnabled` with `hardBlockers: 0`) **and** selected channel(s) are ready (`selectedReady`). Otherwise grey with title/helper *Fyll i e-post eller telefon för att skicka.* Hard blockers still disable header Skicka with title *Komplettera uppgifterna ovan innan offerten kan skickas.*
3. After send: status **Väntar på godkännande**; demo e-post/both → banner about simulated mail. SMS-only marks the quote sent without e-post (phone required) and opens SMS with the customer link.
4. Prepared subject is `Offert från Södermalms Snickeri AB – {rubrik}` (no `#n`); `#{n}` appears only in the body. Same shape for **Skicka påminnelse** with the `Påminnelse: offert från …` prefix. Domain test: `src/lib/email-subjects.test.ts`.

**Back from komplettera**

1. Open a draft quote from Ekonomi (Back on the quote is **Ekonomi** or **Offerter**).
2. Missing e-post/telefon: open header *Skicka offert* (when hard blockers are clear) and fill `#offert-skicka-epost-{id}` / `#offert-skicka-telefon-{id}` inside the Skicka modal — do **not** expect *Lägg till e-post* in `#quote-send-blockers` ([PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165), [PR #167](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/167)). Hard blockers still leave the page via checklist links: click *Komplettera företagsuppgifter* / *Lägg till personnummer* / *Välj bostad* in `#quote-send-blockers`.
3. Customer or Inställningar Back must be **Offert #{n}**, not Ekonomi.
4. After save, that Back still returns to the same quote.

**Accept (demo, ~3 minutes)**

1. `/demo` → open `/offert/demo-bertil-fasad` (or Ekonomi → Offerter → #115 → overflow **Kopiera kundlänk** — there is no *Öppna kundvyn* on the quote page).
2. Scroll **past the document card** to the accept chrome (or tap **Godkänn offert** in the mobile sticky — it submits, it does not jump). **Ditt namn** is prefilled *Bertil Lindqvist* — clear it and the button disables; type a name again. The card itself has no Godkänn block and no **Ställ en fråga**.
3. Read the sentence, press **Godkänn offert** → *Offerten är godkänd* + receipt; page reloads to the read-only state with **one** banner *Godkänd av …* and **Intyg om godkännande**; the card has only the quiet *Godkänd {datum} av {namn}* line. Reload → no form, no second accept.
4. Open **Intyg om godkännande** (`/offert/…/underlag`): readable certificate, hashes collapsed under *Teknisk kontroll*. **Skriv ut eller spara som PDF** → `/underlag/pdf`. From the accepted offer footer, *Skriv ut eller spara som PDF* is the commercial offer; *Skriv ut intyg om godkännande* is the certificate.
5. Back in the app: `/ekonomi/offerter/quote-fasad` shows badge **Godkänd**, compact banner *Godkänd av Bertil Lindqvist* + time + **Intyg om godkännande** (no IP/hash/method on this screen, no *Version 1 låst* header badge). Timeline row *Godkänd av Bertil Lindqvist*. `/kunder/cust-bertil` chain: Offert → Uppdrag (`job-fasad`, no duplicate) → Faktura.
6. Stale owner tab ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127)) - the register/detail must **not** keep *Väntar på godkännande* after the customer decided outside that tab:
   - **Same browser, second tab:** leave `/ekonomi?flik=offerter` (or `/ekonomi/offerter/quote-fasad`) open, accept in another tab → the #115 row flips to **Godkänd** on its own via the `driva-live-refresh` BroadcastChannel - no focus, no navigation needed (this is the demo case; Avböj → **Avböjd** the same way).
   - **Back/Forward or return to the tab:** owner tab → customer accepts elsewhere → browser **Back/Forward**, a bfcache restore, or refocusing the tab after **≥ 1 s** away → `router.refresh()` → **Godkänd**. Without #127 the App Router served Back/Forward from its client cache with zero server fetch.
   - F5 / typing the URL / a new navigation works as before (server already consistent, [PR #89](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/89)).
   - Do **not** expect a refresh while an editor is open (`[data-editor-shell]` / `[data-site-editor-shell]`), within 1,5 s of the previous one, or after a sub-second focus flicker. `LiveRefresh` renders nothing - assert on the row/badge text or `[data-quote-accepted-banner]`, or on the RSC re-fetch in the network log; there is no selector.
7. Negative checks: `/offert/demo-eva-bokhylla` (utkast) → 404. No request to any BankID host. Puppeteer: `[data-testid=public-quote-accept]`, `[data-quote-accepted-banner]`, `[data-quote-acceptance-line]`, `[data-acceptance-certificate]`.

Scripts: `scripts/verify.mjs`, `verify-validation-ux.ts`, `verify-tax-reduction.ts`, `verify-attention-browser.ts`. Tests: `quote-accept.test.ts` (happy path, empty name, already accepted, declined/expired/changed hash, rate limit, demo isolation, customer+business mail, DB mapping), `quote-acceptance-certificate.test.ts` (summary/facts order, intact vs changed Swedish, web `<details>`, PDF footer hashes), `draft-actions.test.ts`, `flows.test.ts`, `quote-terms.test.ts`, `quote-send-contact.test.ts` (soft `buyer_email`, gap copy, typed destination, SMS-only marks `skickad` without e-post), `live-refresh.test.ts` (gate: popstate/pageshow/broadcast always refresh with the 1,5 s threshold, a return needs ≥ 1 s away, flicker and initial focus ignored, hidden + blur = one refresh; message shape + a real `BroadcastChannel` round trip).

## Fakturor

- **User-facing name:** Fakturor (Ekonomi tab). Detail: **Faktura #{n}** or **Faktura (utkast)**.
- **List first column** (`invoiceListTitle` in `src/lib/invoices/display.ts`): issued `#{n}` (+ type chip **Kredit** / **Delbetalning** / **Slutfaktura**). Draft: invoice rubrik, else the linked quote version's title, else the job title (`invoiceTitle || quoteTitle || jobTitle`), else first non-empty line description, else `Faktura till {kund}`. Never the word **Utkast** as the name — that stays on the status chip. Drafts never get a number. **Delbetalning** only when `type === "delbetalning"`.
- **Purpose:** Get paid. Issue is atomic (number + snapshot + books).
- **Routes:** `/ekonomi?flik=fakturor`, `/ekonomi/fakturor/ny`, `/[id]`, `/[id]/redigera`, public `/faktura/[token]`, `/pdf`.
- **How to get there:** Ekonomi → Fakturor. Header **Ny faktura**. From job/customer/quote/command. Query `?kund=`, `?job=`, `?fristaende=1`.
- **Statuses:** Utkast, Skickad, Delbetald, Betald, Krediterad. Overdue **derived:** *Förfallen* / *Förfallen N dagar*. Credit badge **Kreditfaktura** (never overdue). Types: faktura, delbetalning, slutfaktura, kredit.
- **Filters:** Alla, Utkast, Obetalda, Förfallna, Betalda, Krediterade.
- **Main actions — utkast:** **Redigera faktura** · Kasta utkast · **Skicka faktura** (issues **then** emails — `issueInvoice` + `emailInvoice`). Checklist `#invoice-send-blockers` (still includes `buyer_email` / *Lägg till e-post* — [PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165) softened **quotes** only). The confirm modal (`InvoiceDraftSend`) carries the ROT/RUT callout `[data-rot-share]` and the `ShareCustomerLink` row (Kopiera kundlänk · Dela · SMS) - no personal message field (quotes only).
- **Issued:** Visa kundvy, **Skicka påminnelse** if overdue, overflow (`aria-label="Fler åtgärder"`, order verified on driva-alpha #1042 / #1045): **Kreditera** (only while `status === "skickad"` and not a credit note - `canCredit` on the page; the service itself also accepts `delbetald`/`betald`, reachable via Verifikationer → **Fakturan är fel**), Kopiera kundlänk, **SMS** (`sms:{kundens telefon}?body=Faktura #{n} från oss …`, see [Offerter](#offerter-quotes)), **Ladda ner PDF**, **Skicka igen** (reads **Försök skicka igen** while `sentAt` is unset), **Simulera inbetalning** (demo **and** an active mock bank connection — hidden after *Koppla från*).
- **Kreditera - hel eller delkredit** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130); `CreditInvoiceConfirmDialog` + `CreditInvoiceForm` in `money-widgets.tsx`, service `creditInvoice()` in `services/invoices.ts`): modal **Kreditera faktura** / *Faktura #{n}* / *Kunden får en kreditfaktura med eget nummer. Originalet och dess verifikation står kvar – krediten bokförs som en egen händelse.* Radiogroup `aria-label="Vad ska krediteras"`, radios `name="kredit-lage"`: **Hela fakturan · {kvar att kreditera}** (*Fakturan markeras som krediterad och räknas inte längre som en fordran.*) or **En del av beloppet** (*Raderna krediteras proportionellt per momssats. Fakturan fortsätter gälla för resten.*) → field **Belopp att kreditera, inkl. moms** (placeholder *högst {belopp}*, `useId()` - no stable id, suffix *kr*). ROT/RUT invoices hide the partial option (*Fakturor med ROT/RUT-avdrag krediteras alltid i sin helhet …*). Buttons **Avbryt** / **Kreditera hela fakturan** or **Kreditera {belopp}** (*Krediterar …*). The form is remounted on every open (`{open ? <CreditInvoiceForm/> : null}`) so the choice always starts at *hela* (commit `c094e4d`). Result: a new invoice `type: "kredit"`, status `skickad`, own number + OCR + snapshot, `creditsInvoiceId` → original, lines from `shareLines` (positive qty 1, proportional unit price, *Delkredit av faktura #N*), booked as the reversal (`entriesCredit`). Original: full credit → `krediterad`; partial → **status unchanged** while something is outstanding (`invoiceOutstanding` = toPay − paid − `invoiceCreditedAmount`), → `betald` when paid covers the rest, → `krediterad` when nothing remains. The credit document says *… med {belopp}. Resterande belopp på fakturan gäller fortfarande.* while the original is not `krediterad`. **Customer mail for both** (`prepareCreditInvoiceNotice` → `creditInvoiceEmail`, sent with `after()`): subject *Kreditfaktura från {företag} – Kreditfaktura #{m}*, body *Faktura #N är delvis krediterad med kreditfaktura #M på {belopp}. Kvar att betala …* / *… krediterad i sin helhet … Du ska inte betala …*, CTA **Visa kreditfakturan**; demo / no provider / no customer e-mail → nothing sent; mail failure never rolls back the credit. Tests: `credit-invoice-mail.test.ts` (delkredit mail), `payment-flows.test.ts` (delkredit keeps `skickad`, outstanding drops).
- **Paid:** *Betald och bokförd.*
- **Payment block + PDF on mails** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)): `emailInvoice`, **Skicka igen** and `remindInvoiceByEmail` all render the **Så betalar du** table (`src/lib/invoices/payment-copy.ts`: Bankgiro / PlusGiro / IBAN / BIC / Bankkonto rows as configured, **OCR**, **Förfallodatum**, **Belopp**) and attach `faktura-{n}.pdf` (`invoicePdfBytes` → `buildSimplePdf`, `src/lib/pdf/simple-pdf.ts` - a one-page PDF 1.4 summary with the same payment block, not the A4 document; the full invoice stays behind `/faktura/[token]/pdf`). Quotes attach `offert-{n}.pdf` the same way (`src/lib/email/service.ts`). Demo builds the attachment but the send is simulated.
- **Automatic reminders + cron** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130); `runAutomaticReminders` in `src/lib/services/automatic-reminders.ts`, route `src/app/api/cron/reminders/route.ts`, `vercel.json` `crons: [{ path: "/api/cron/reminders", schedule: "0 7 * * *" }]`): an overdue `skickad` / `delbetald` non-credit invoice gets `remindInvoiceByEmail` at the earliest **7 days** after the due date and then every 7 days, **max 2** automatic reminders; sent quotes get one follow-up after 7 days (see [Offerter](#offerter-quotes)). The handler requires `CRON_SECRET` in `Authorization: Bearer …` **only** (`src/lib/platform/cron-auth.ts`, `isAuthorizedCronRequest`, `crypto.timingSafeEqual` on equal-length buffers; the `?secret=` query path was removed because a secret in a query string is written to Vercel access logs, Sentry breadcrumbs and every intermediate proxy - anything else, including an unset or empty `CRON_SECRET`, is 401 *Obehörig.*), then JSON mode = the one local business, Supabase = every tenant from `listActiveBusinessIds()` (`is_demo = false and disabled_at is null`) via `runWithTenant({ access: "write", retry: false })`; response `{ ok, businesses, quotes, invoices, errors }`. **Proxy allowlist ([PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134), merge `d82238d`):** `/api/cron` is in the proxy's `PUBLIC_PREFIXES` (`src/proxy.ts`), so the cookie-less request Vercel Cron sends reaches the handler; auth is still the route's own `CRON_SECRET` check. Before #134 the proxy redirected it to `/login?next=%2Fapi%2Fcron%2Freminders` (307, seen on driva-alpha 2026-09-08) and the daily job never ran. Live on driva-alpha 2026-09-10: `GET /api/cron/reminders` with no cookie and no `Authorization` → 401 `{"ok":false,"error":"Obehörig."}` (`content-type: application/json`, `x-matched-path: /api/cron/reminders`), and a wrong `Authorization: Bearer` → the same 401 - not 307. A run with the real secret was not made for this map, so a successful reminder send is **not** claimed; the schedule in `vercel.json` is unchanged. There is **no** owner setting to switch the automatic reminders off (Notiser only covers mails *to the owner*). Tests: `automatic-reminders.test.ts`.
- **Customer e-mail subjects** ([PR #101](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/101)): **Skicka faktura** and **Skicka igen** (both `emailInvoice`) → `Faktura från {företag} – {rubrik}`; påminnelse (`remindInvoiceByEmail`) → `Påminnelse: faktura från {företag} – {rubrik}`. **No `#n` in the subject** — number, OCR, förfallodatum and bankgiro stay in the body. Rubrik from `invoiceEmailRubrik` (`src/lib/email/rubrik.ts`): linked quote version title → first non-empty line description → linked job title → first line of övrig information (rich text) → document-type label without number (which is then omitted, so the subject is just `Faktura från {företag}`). Credit notice (`creditInvoiceEmail`) still reads `Kreditfaktura från {företag} – Kreditfaktura #{n}` (title = `invoiceHeading(credit)`) and since [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) also goes out for a **delkredit** (`credit-invoice-mail.test.ts`). Demo / `is_demo` simulates the send with the same subject.
- **Public:** utkast 404. *Fakturan är betald* / *Fakturan har förfallit*. Ladda ner PDF.
- **Omvänd byggmoms (reverse charge construction)** ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109); `src/lib/invoices/reverse-charge.ts`): driven by the customer flag, frozen on the invoice. `createInvoice` / `updateInvoice` set `invoice.reverseCharge = true` and force every line to **0 %** (`withoutVat`) when `reverseChargeAppliesTo(customer)` (företag + flag); the form shows the note *Omvänd byggmoms. Kunden är markerad som byggföretag …* above **Fakturarader**, `LinesEditor` gets `defaultVatRate 0` + `reverseCharge`, and lines coming from a quote/job are zeroed too. Issue blockers (`collectReverseChargeBlockers`): any line with VAT (*… så alla rader ska ha 0 % moms*), missing org.nr (*Vid omvänd byggmoms måste köparens momsregistreringsnummer stå på fakturan.*, link `/kunder/{id}`), ROT/RUT on the same invoice. Document (`invoice-document.tsx`): totals row reads **Moms – omvänd byggmoms** instead of *Moms 0 %*, plus section **Omvänd byggmoms** with the ML 1 kap. 2 § note (`REVERSE_CHARGE_CONSTRUCTION_NOTE`), **Köparens momsreg.nr** (derived from org.nr, `SE…01`) and **Moms att redovisa av köparen** (25 %). Posting: sales to **3231** (VAT code `BYGG`, ruta 41) instead of 3004, no utgående moms; credit notes inherit the original's flag (`invoiceHasReverseCharge` reads the issued snapshot, never the customer's current flag). Purchase side: expense category **Inköpt byggtjänst (omvänd byggmoms)** → 4425 + 2614/2647 (`entriesReverseChargePurchase`, VAT codes `OST`/`OU1`/`IO`). `VAT_CODES` in `src/lib/accounting/vat.ts`. Tests: `invoices/reverse-charge.test.ts`.
- **Related:** ROT application card; `DeniedReductionCard`; quote deviation; payments / bank match.
- **Components:** `doc-form.tsx` (InvoiceForm; order = header → Fakturarader → `DocDescriptionCard` → Skattereduktion; Bostad via `tax-reduction-document-property.tsx` / `SearchableCombobox`; no side text under Save), `lines-editor.tsx` (shared with quotes), `invoice-document.tsx`, `invoice-draft-send.tsx`, `invoice-issue-checklist.tsx`, `money-widgets.tsx` (`CreditInvoiceConfirmDialog`, `UploadReceiptButton`, …), `denied-reduction-card.tsx`, `share-customer-link.tsx`.
- **Form ids:** `#faktura-saknas`, `#faktura-kund`, `#faktura-rot-rut`, `#faktura-fastighet` (`#faktura-fastighet-val` Bostad combobox; RUT `#faktura-fastighet-adress`), `#faktura-betalvillkor`.
- **Line editor (`LinesEditor`, `lines-editor.tsx`; shared by quote and invoice forms; [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) added rabatt, rubrikrader, kopiera/flytta and the article picker):** container `#prisrader` / `[data-line-editor]`, one row per line `[data-line-id={id}]`, field ids `rad-{id}-typ|beskrivning|antal|enhet|pris|rabatt|moms` (`lineFieldId`; Enter walks `LINE_EDITOR_FIELDS` in that order - `src/lib/line-editor-nav.ts`, tests `line-editor-nav.test.ts`). Kinds `arbete | material | resor | ovrigt` (UI Arbete / Material / Resor / Övrigt). **Rabatt**: column **Rabatt** (mobile *Rabatt %*), `aria-label="Rabatt i procent"`, `discountPercent` 0–100 → `lineTotal = qty × unitPrice × (1 − %/100)`; documents print the undiscounted à-pris and the discounted **Summa** - there is **no** separate *Rabatt* totals row; ROT/RUT base uses the discounted labour total (`docTotals` in `calc.ts`). **Rubrik**: button **Rubrik** → `createHeadingLine()` (`isHeading: true`, kind `ovrigt`, input `aria-label="Rubrik"`, placeholder *Rubrik, t.ex. Arbete i kök*) - rendered as an uppercase section header on the documents, excluded from every sum. Per row: `aria-label="Kopiera rad"`, **Flytta upp** / **Flytta ner** (aria buttons, no drag handle), remove → toast *Raden borttagen* with action **Ångra** (`LINE_DELETED_TOAST`; ⌘/Ctrl+Z undo stack). **Från register** opens the modal *Artikelregister* (articles from `meta.articles`, see [Inställningar](#inställningar); empty copy points to *Inställningar → Fakturering*). It stays under the quote/invoice list only (`fromRegister`; hidden on the job single-line editor and on job-change). There is no per-row `+` / *Spara i registret* - articles are added under Inställningar → Fakturering. The description field is `LineDescriptionInput` (autocomplete from the company's own history, separate from the register). Job add/edit uses the same fields via `variant="single"` (`[data-job-line-editor]`). Tests: `line-defaults.test.ts`, `line-editor-nav.test.ts`, `services/articles.test.ts`.
- **DB:** `invoices` (`number` null until issue, `reverse_charge`), `invoice_line_items` (`discount_percent`, `is_heading`), `invoice_issued_snapshots` (immutable legal copy - also carries `reverseCharge` + buyer VAT number), `payments`.
- **Invariants:** Number only via atomic `app.issue_invoice`. Issued UI reads snapshot. Partial pay → `delbetald`. Credit = reversal verification, not new revenue; the original and its verification stay. **Both full and partial credit** notify the invoice customer (`prepareCreditInvoiceNotice` + `after` + `sendMail`) - the pre-#130 statement that partial credit was not a product path is **false** now. A partial credit never changes the original's status while something is outstanding. ROT/RUT invoices can only be credited in full. Rest-invoice after denied ROT: `deniedReductionOf`, no new revenue. Issued OCR is Bankgirot **OCR-10 soft** (invoice-number digits + modulus-10 check, no length digit) via `ocrForInvoice` in `src/lib/ids.ts` — assigned once at `issueInvoice`, frozen on `issuedSnapshot`, reused on reminders/email/PDF. Drafts have no customer-facing OCR. SQL twin: `app.ocr_for_invoice`.
- **Desktop/mobile:** same register pattern as offerter.
- **Live:** `#1042` Förfallen 6 dagar (Brf Eken); `#1047` Skickad delbetalning; Utkast Brf Eken (`inv-1048`, list title from first line); two 0 kr Eli drafts (`Luckor i ek`, `Bänkskiva i ask`).
- **Verify:** Ekonomi → Fakturor → first column is document title (not “Utkast”; `[data-testid=invoice-list-title]` on the title span in table and card view — the row link itself has only sr-only text *Faktura {label}*). Open #1042 → **Fler åtgärder** → **Kreditera** → modal *Kreditera faktura* with `input[name="kredit-lage"]` × 2; pick **En del av beloppet** → *Belopp att kreditera, inkl. moms* (placeholder *högst 23 000*) → **Kreditera {belopp}** → a new *Kreditfaktura* row in the register while #1042 keeps `status: "skickad"` (still listed as overdue) with a smaller `invoiceOutstanding`. Discard: only the Utkast row / detail `[data-testid=discard-draft-trigger]`. Public: `/faktura/{token}` for a sent invoice. Tests: `src/lib/invoices/display.test.ts`, `economy-list.test.ts`, `payment-flows.test.ts`, `financial-invariants.test.ts`, `credit-invoice-mail.test.ts` (subject/body, demo null, send-throw isolation, customer not carpenter), `email-subjects.test.ts` (subject shapes without `#n`, `invoiceEmailRubrik` fallback order) + `email.test.ts` (same subjects through `emailInvoice` / `remindInvoiceByEmail` / `followUpQuoteByEmail`). Script: `scripts/verify-financial-browser.ts`.

## ROT / RUT

- **User-facing name:** ROT/RUT, Skattereduktion, Preliminärt ROT-avdrag / RUT-avdrag
- **Purpose:** ROT / RUT on **arbete** lines; apply to Skatteverket after work + customer share paid. V1 is **manual** (no SKV API). V1 does get a **HUS file export**: the carpenter downloads Skatteverket's Begäran-XML (schema v6) and imports it in the e-service *Rot och rut – företag* — Ferva never submits anything.
- **Rates and caps** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) / commit `ad3e70d`; `src/lib/calc.ts`, `src/lib/tax-reduction-used.ts`, tests `tax-reduction.test.ts`, `tax-reduction-used.test.ts`): `ROT_ANDEL = 0.3`, `RUT_ANDEL = 0.5`. **Documents** (quotes, unpaid invoices) use `taxReductionRate(type)` = today's rate (ROT 30 %, RUT 50 %) - so #115 still shows *Preliminärt ROT-avdrag −14 025 kr* and #116 *−7 425 kr* on 2026-09-08. **The real rate follows the customer's payment day**: `taxReductionRateOn(type, paidDate)` gives ROT **50 %** for payments **2025-05-12 … 2025-12-31** and 30 % otherwise (incl. from 2026-01-01); RUT is always 50 %. The underlag text notes the payment-day rate once `paidAt` is set (`services/tax-reduction.ts`). Caps per person and year: `ROT_TAK = 50 000`, `RUT_TAK = 75 000` and the **combined** `ROT_RUT_GEMENSAMT_TAK = 75 000`. `usedTaxReductionThisYear` counts **only** Ferva invoices paid (or issued) that year — `customer.taxReductionUsed` (legacy hos-andra jsonb) is **not** added. `remainingTaxReduction(used, type)` = `min(own cap − own used, 75 000 − rot − rut)` still exists in `tax-reduction-used.ts` but the customer page no longer shows *Kvar att lova*. `resolveTaxReductionAmounts` still accepts `remainingCap` but the quote/invoice forms do **not** pass it, clamp on it, or warn (no *kunden har redan använt …* blocker). The *Hur räknas detta?* text prints the statutory cap for the chosen type. `prisbasbeloppFor(year)` (`src/lib/accounting/prisbasbelopp.ts`: 2022 48 300 · 2023 52 500 · 2024 57 300 · 2025 58 800 · 2026 59 200, unknown years clamp) feeds traktamente and the inventarie threshold, not ROT.
- **Where it lives (not its own nav item):**
  - Customer (privat): `SectionTitle` ROT/RUT + `CustomerRotSection` — `#kund-personnummer`, **Lägg till fastighet** (`work-location-form.tsx`; see [Kunder](#kunder)). No year-cap widget.
  - Quote/invoice form: **Skattereduktion** (`#offert-rot-rut`, `#faktura-rot-rut`) — ROT **Bostad** combobox `#offert-fastighet-val` / `#faktura-fastighet-val`; RUT *Adress där arbetet utförs*
  - Send modals: callout `[data-rot-share]` (`rot-customer-share.tsx`) *Kunden betalar {belopp}. ROT {avdrag} begärs hos Skatteverket när arbetet är klart och kundens del är betald.*
  - Documents: deduction line + **ROT/RUT-avdrag** terms
  - Invoice/job: `TaxReductionApplicationCard` (incl. **Importera beslut från Skatteverket**), `DeniedReductionCard` (`#nekat-belopp`)
  - Job detail: `RotDeadlineBanner` `[data-rot-deadline]` (`rot-deadline-banner.tsx`, `src/lib/tax-reduction-deadline.ts`, tests `tax-reduction-deadline.test.ts`) - **Ansök om ROT senast 31 januari** / **Sista dagen för ROT har passerat**, *Arbete från {år} ska begäras hos Skatteverket senast {datum}.* + *{n} dagar kvar.* or *Skapa underlaget nu – efter det kan utbetalningen nekas.* Shown from **1 December** of the work year through January (and while overdue) when the job has a `taxReductionApplication` that is still `preliminar` / `redo_att_ansokas`; work year = `paidAt` or the work end date. Not on Hem, Bokföring or the Ekonomi lists.
- **How to get there:** Open a privat customer, or a quote/invoice with ROT (demo: #116, #115, #113).
- **Application statuses:** Preliminär → Redo att ansökas → Väntar på Skatteverket → Godkänd / Delvis godkänd / Nekad (`TAX_REDUCTION_STATUS`: `preliminar`, `redo_att_ansokas`, `underlag_skapat`, `godkant`, `delvis_godkant`, `nekat`).
- **Quote/invoice Skattereduktion** ([PR #141](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/141)): form order in `doc-form.tsx` is header → rader → Beskrivning (`DocDescriptionCard`) → Skattereduktion. Side text under Save (*Nummer tilldelas när du skickar.* / *Koppling till offert är oförändrad.*) is gone. **ROT:** one **Bostad** `SearchableCombobox` in `tax-reduction-document-property.tsx` (same chrome as the customer picker); footer **+ Ny fastighet** reuses `WorkLocationForm`. Helper *ROT kräver att bostaden är vald och sparad på offerten/fakturan. Kundens fastigheter räcker inte.* ROT send/issue needs that saved `workLocationId`. **RUT:** no property picker — *Adress där arbetet utförs* (defaults from the customer postal address); RUT requires personnummer, not a selected fastighet; send/issue without `workLocationId` is OK. Preliminärt avdrag defaults to max eligible work-cost deduction (`TaxReductionAmountPanel`). One **Ändra** with pencil opens a whole-kronor input (`aria-label` *ROT-avdrag att använda* / *RUT-avdrag att använda*, 0–max); the kronor amount is not a second link. Over-max is rejected while typing and clamped on blur. Att betala / Offertvärde = total − chosen deduction. **Använd max** when manually adjusted. Same on quote.
- **Main actions:** toggle ROT/RUT; pick saved bostad (ROT); *Skapa ansökningsunderlag*; **Ladda ner fil till Skatteverket** (HUS-XML, on the underlag card, `#hus-fil`); mark Godkänt / Delvis / Nekat by hand **or** import Skatteverket's decision (`RotBeslutImport`, `rot-beslut-import.tsx`, in the underlag phase of the card: heading **Importera beslut från Skatteverket**, *JSON från e-tjänsten. Ferva fyller i utfallet så du inte skriver om beloppet.*, `input[type=file][accept="application/json,.json"]` + sr-only **Importera**; `parseBeslutJson` in `src/lib/tax-reduction-beslut.ts` reads `beslut`/`utfall`/`outcome`/`status`, `godkantBelopp`/`nekatBelopp` (`approvedAmount`/`deniedAmount`) and `diarienummer`/`referens` → `importTaxReductionBeslutAction` → `setTaxReductionDecision(godkant | delvis_godkant | nekat, deniedAmount?)`; tests `tax-reduction-beslut.test.ts`; no selector on the file input); on nekat create rest invoice.
- **HUS file (Skatteverket Begäran v6):** section *Fil till Skatteverket* inside `TaxReductionApplicationCard` while status is *Väntar på Skatteverket*. `GET /api/skatteverket/hus?jobb=<id>|faktura=<id>` builds, schema-checks and downloads `rot-begaran-…xml` / `rut-begaran-…xml`; the download is noted (`hus.fileDownloadedAt`, audit `rot_fil_nedladdad`) but status stays `underlag_skapat` — never auto-Godkänt, no BankID, no SKV API. One `Arenden` per paid invoice: `Kopare` = customer personnummer as 12 digits, `BetalningsDatum` = last registered payment (bank match / manual), `PrisForArbete` = arbete incl. VAT, `BetaltBelopp` = arbete incl. VAT − avdrag, `BegartBelopp` = avdrag, `Ovrigkostnad` = resor + övrigt incl. VAT, `Materialkostnad` = material incl. VAT under the chosen **arbetsområde** (ROT default Bygg; RUT must be chosen; `#hus-arbetsomrade`). **Arbetade timmar** are read from hour-priced `arbete` lines (`tim`/`h`); fixed-price lines block the export until the user types the hours (`#hus-timmar-<invoiceId>`) — hours are never invented. Other blockers: missing payment date → link to the invoice; personnummer not 10/12 digits → `/kunder/{id}#kund-personnummer`; ROT without bostad → `#faktura-rot-rut`. ROT and RUT never share a file (`RotBegaran` vs `HushallBegaran`). Schema vendored under `docs/skatteverket/hus/` (see its README); the file carries no utförare — that is the logged-in company in the e-service.
- **Related:** customers.personnummer, work_locations, quote/invoice terms snapshot.
- **Components:** `tax-reduction-fields.tsx`, `tax-reduction-document-property.tsx`, `searchable-combobox.tsx`, `work-location-form.tsx`, `work-location-label.ts`, `tax-reduction-application.tsx` (missing `workAddress` fill = `AddressAutocomplete composeSelected="line"`; `HusExportSection` with `[data-testid=hus-download]`; hosts `RotBeslutImport`), `denied-reduction-card.tsx`, `rot-deadline-banner.tsx`, `rot-beslut-import.tsx`, `rot-customer-share.tsx`. Logic: `src/lib/services/tax-reduction.ts`, `tax-reduction-gaps.ts`, `tax-reduction-send.ts`, `tax-reduction-amount.ts`, `tax-reduction-used.ts`, `tax-reduction-deadline.ts`, `tax-reduction-beslut.ts`, `tax-reduction-terms.ts`, `src/lib/hus-begaran.ts` (pure XML builder + rules), `src/lib/services/hus-export.ts` (case → file, blockers, `patchHusExportFields`, `markHusFileDownloaded`). Bostad address uses `AddressFields` in `work-location-form.tsx`.
- **DB:** `customers.personal_identity_number`, `customers.tax_reduction_used` (legacy jsonb `{ year, rot, rut }`, mig 47 — unused by the kundkort UI); `work_locations` (beteckning / BRF / lägenhet; no separate namn); `jobs.housing`, `jobs.tax_reduction_application` (JSONB, now also `hus.{workCategory,laborHoursByInvoice,fileDownloadedAt}` — no migration); `invoices.rot`, quote/invoice `workLocationId`, `tax_reduction_*` JSONB.
- **Invariants:** Personnummer on the **customer** (Ny kund / identity forms do not collect it). Only `arbete` lines reduce (after any radrabatt). Cap per person and year: **ROT 50 000 kr**, **RUT 75 000 kr**, **ROT + RUT together 75 000 kr** (`ROT_TAK` / `RUT_TAK` / `ROT_RUT_GEMENSAMT_TAK`, `taxReductionCap(type)` in `src/lib/calc.ts`). The rate is decided by the payment day, never by the quote date. Denied rest invoice is collection, not new sales. ROT send blockers if personnummer or document `workLocationId` is missing; RUT send blockers personnummer only (property not required). HUS file never leaves Ferva unless it passes `validateHusBegaran` (schema ranges + e-service rules: begärt ≤ betalt, begärt + betalt ≤ arbetskostnad, same payment year, ≤ 100 köpare).
- **Live (code on main after #141; 2026-09-13):** #116 draft still blocked on PIN + bostad (Eva has neither in the seed), *Preliminärt ROT-avdrag −7 425 kr* (30 % of 24 750 kr arbete inkl. moms), Offertvärde 33 325 kr. Public #115 shows *Preliminärt ROT-avdrag − 14 025 kr*, Offertvärde 58 350 kr. `/kunder/cust-anna` → ROT/RUT is personnummer + fastigheter only - **no** year-cap widget, **not** *Ferva i 2026* / *ROT använt* / *Kvar att lova*. Quote/invoice Skattereduktion may still mention remaining Ferva-year cap when ROT/RUT is on (`taxReductionAmountHelp`, *Hur räknas detta?*). Deploy on driva-alpha may lag this merge; treat main code as source of truth.
- **Verify:** `npx tsx scripts/verify-tax-reduction.ts`. Open `#offert-rot-rut` on a new quote → ROT **Bostad** `#offert-fastighet-val` (not a `<select>`). Nekat path: `#nekat-belopp`. Do **not** type into *ROT använt {år}* - that input is gone; the kundkort has **no** year-cap widget. HUS: on a paid ROT job with underlag, `#hus-fil` shows arbetsområde + timmar and `[data-testid=hus-download]` is a link (or disabled with the gap list `#hus-luckor`); `xmllint --noout --schema docs/skatteverket/hus/begaran/V6/Begaran.xsd <fil>` validates. Tests: `customer-rot-package.test.ts` (Ny kund / identity have no PIN or fastighet; unused property removable; RUT send without `workLocationId`; preliminärt avdrag clamp), `tax-reduction.test.ts` (incl. rate by payment day + combined cap), `tax-reduction-used.test.ts` (Ferva invoices only; hos-andra ignored), `tax-reduction-deadline.test.ts`, `tax-reduction-beslut.test.ts`, `tax-reduction-send.test.ts`, `hus-begaran.test.ts` (golden ROT småhus / bostadsrätt / RUT fixtures in `src/lib/__fixtures__/hus/`, XSD via xmllint when present, no ROT/RUT mixing), `hus-export.test.ts` (blockers for timmar / betalningsdatum / arbetsområde, status untouched on download).

## Underlag (the former Inbox)

- **User-facing name:** Underlag (Bokföring tab, page title *Underlag · Ferva*). Until [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) (merge `8b135bb`) this was the nav item **Inbox** at `/inbox`; the code still calls it inbox everywhere (`inbox_items`, `InboxList`, `InboxUploadZone`, `listInbox`, `/api/inbox/*`, copy such as *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna* and the notice CTA *Öppna i inboxen*), only the routes, the nav and the visible labels changed.
- **Purpose:** Inbound supplier invoices, receipts, other economic docs. **Not** website contact forms.
- **Routes:** `/bokforing/underlag` (`src/app/(app)/bokforing/underlag/page.tsx` - the old `inbox/page.tsx` with `title="Underlag"`), `/bokforing/underlag/[id]`, `/bokforing/underlag/[id]/kontrollera` (title *Kontrollera belopp*, crumbs *Underlag / {avsändare} / Kontrollera*). `/inbox`, `/inbox/[id]`, `/inbox/[id]/kontrollera` are `next.config.ts` redirects (`permanent: false` → 307) plus `redirect()` pages *kvar som redirect i minst två releaser* - see [Legacy path rewrites](#legacy-path-rewrites-srclibnavts). Inbound HMAC `POST /api/inbox/inbound`. Resend Receiving `POST /api/inbox/inbound/resend` (Svix + `receiving.get`). Attachments `/api/inbox/bilaga/...`. Payment file `/api/betalfil/[id]`. Nav: `ROUTES` has `/bokforing/underlag` (back **Bokföring**), `/bokforing/underlag/:id` (label *Underlag*, back **Underlag**) and `…/:id/kontrollera` (*Kontrollera belopp*, back **Underlag**); `bokforingDetailTabForPath` keeps the **Underlag** tab active on all three; `kunderInboxHref()` = `/bokforing/underlag`.
- **How to get there:** Bokföring tab **Underlag** (in both enkelt and avancerat läge - the second tab of the row). Bokföring badge (`countInboxBadge()` is summed into it). Hem / Bokföring attention rows `inbox-mail-<id>` (*Kontrollera belopp för {avsändare}-{kvittot|fakturan}* / *Granska {kvitto|faktura} från …*; CTA **Kontrollera** → `…/kontrollera`, else **Öppna underlaget** - the primary CTA label **Öppna i inboxen** is gone, the overflow `viewLabel` still reads *Visa i inboxen*), owner notices (`inkorg`, `orderbekraftelse`) and the assistant now link to `/bokforing/underlag/…`. The `/bokforing` overview also embeds the same upload zone + inbound address (*Skicka underlag hit* card while there are open questions).
- **Subtitle:** *Leverantörsfakturor, kvitton och andra ekonomiska dokument samlas här.* (unchanged)
- **Main actions:** filter Öppna / Alla (`?visning=alla`); checkbox **Bara ekonomiska dokument** (on by default; unticked = `?ekonomiska=alla`, which is the only way to see orderbekräftelser in the list - `listInbox({ economicOnly })`, [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)); search; drop or pick files in the **Släpp kvitton och fakturor här** zone (`InboxUploadZone`, in the page body under the header - there is no **Lägg till dokument** button any more); copy inbound address (**Kopiera**); detail: Kontrollera belopp, Godkänn uppgifter, Skapa bankfil, Visa PDF. Empty list: *Inget öppet underlag* / *Inga underlag* (was *Inget öppet i inboxen* / *Inboxen är tom*) + **Visa allt som kommit in**.
- **Types:** Leverantörsfaktura (ska betalas) · Kvitto (redan betalt) · ekonomiskt dokument · **Orderbekräftelse** (`orderbekraftelse`, only when the company has sent wholesaler orders — never enters the invoice/receipt pipeline and hidden from the list unless **Bara ekonomiska dokument** is unticked; see [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders)). An orderbekräftelse renders `inbox-order-confirmation.tsx` (no invoice/payment fields), shows *Orderbekräftelse* → *Välj beställning* (candidates, `[data-inbox-order-candidates]` + **Det är den här**) → status badge *Kopplad till beställning* (`workflow.ts`) with the card heading *Kopplad beställning* (`[data-inbox-linked-order]`), and `/bokforing/underlag/[id]/kontrollera` sends it back to `/bokforing/underlag/[id]`. An unmatched one is the only wholesaler attention row: *Välj beställning för orderbekräftelsen från {avsändare}* (`inbox-mail-<id>`, CTA **Öppna underlaget**) on Hem / Bokföring.
- **Stored status:** `ny` → `behandlad` → `bokford`. UI may show richer lifecycle (*Kontrollera belopp*, *Bokförd · Redo att betala*, *Bankfil skapad*).
- **Related:** Utgifter & kvitton, Bank, Bokföring questions, payment files (pain.001).
- **Upload zone** (`InboxUploadZone`, `inbox-upload.tsx` → `ReceiptUpload` landing, `receipt-upload.tsx`, wrapper `[data-receipt-upload]`; rendered in the page body between the header and the inbound-address card, not in the header actions - `InboxUploadButton` and **Lägg till dokument** are gone since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125), the queue came with `90d43e3`; both landed on main with [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), merge `a8f6284`; since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) the same zone is also rendered on the `/bokforing` overview): zone `div[role="button"]` with `aria-label="Släpp kvitton och fakturor här"` (= visible title), subtitle *Eller tryck för att välja, fota med kameran eller klistra in en skärmdump. De läses av och hamnar i inboxen.*, formats *PDF, JPG, PNG, HEIC · max 8 MB per fil* (`ReceiptUpload` default; 5 MB before #137). Input paths: drag/drop (title flips to *Släpp filerna här*), click / Enter / Space → hidden `input[type=file][accept="image/*,.pdf,.heic,.heif"][multiple]` (`sr-only`, a sibling of the zone, no `<label>`), **Fota kvitto** (`[data-dropzone-camera]`, a separate `capture="environment"` input; only rendered visible on coarse pointers), Ctrl/Cmd+V on the zone **or anywhere on the page** (`pasteAnywhere`: skipped while a `[role="dialog"][aria-modal="true"]` is open or the paste target is a field). Every file gets a queue row `li[data-receipt-upload-row][data-status="vantar"|"laser"|"klar"|"fel"]` (thumbnail for images, file icon for PDF/HEIC; *Väntar · {size}* → *Läser av …* → *Bokfört* / *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna* / the error), the zone title reads *Läser av {n} av {total} …* while the queue runs and the files are uploaded **one at a time** on the unchanged path `inboxDocumentForm(file)` → `uploadInboxDocumentAction(FormData)` ([PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120)) - the file travels as `File` in `FormData`, never as a data-URL, and it is the **same action and path** as Ekonomi → Utgifter **Släpp kvitton här** (see [Utgifter & kvitton](#utgifter--kvitton-flikutgifter)): `receiptFileFromForm` + `validateReceiptFile` (PDF/JPEG/PNG/WebP/HEIC) → `interpretDocumentFile` → `storeInboxAttachment` (before the item) → `ingestUploadedDocument`; one `router.refresh()` when the queue has finished with ≥ 1 success. Client cap **8 MB** per file (`RECEIPT_MAX_BYTES` = `UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES`, `src/lib/uploads/limits.ts`, [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) - was 5 MB): an oversize file becomes a `fel` row *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* with zero requests (the helper's *Filen är för stor (max 8 MB).* is no longer reached from the UI; a failed file never stops the others). Errors are Swedish via `userFacingStorageError` (fallback *Kunde inte spara dokumentet. Försök igen.*) on the file's row. Footer *1 fil klar* / *N filer klara* (+ *· något gick fel, se raden*) with **Rensa**; row remove `aria-label="Ta bort ur listan"`; the list is `aria-live="polite"`.
- **Components:** `inbox-list.tsx`, `inbox-address.tsx`, `extraction-review.tsx`, `inbox-upload.tsx` (`InboxUploadZone`; + shared `receipt-upload.tsx`, `file-dropzone.tsx`), `payment-file-actions.tsx`, `document-line-review.tsx` (article lines on Kontrollera), `inbox-price-file.tsx` (**Använd nya priser**), `inbox-purchase-match.tsx` (uncertain order link). See [Materialkedjan](#materialkedjan-kvitto--faktura--beställning--uppdrag--faktura).
- **DB:** `inbox_items`, `business_settings.inbound_mail_slug` (unique; allocated at company create, see below), `payment_files`, `supplier_payments`. Bucket `inbox_attachments` is **in use** since [PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109) (`src/lib/inbox/attachment-file.ts`): `inboxBucketAvailable()` = Supabase + `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` **and not a demo tenant** - a `/demo` session or `is_demo` company (`state.meta.demo`) **never** calls the bucket, the same rule as receipts ([PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120)) → file at `<business_id>/<externalId>/<filename>` (uploads: `<business_id>/upload-<timestamp>/<filename>`; PDF/PNG/JPEG/WebP/HEIC; the store's `MAX_INBOX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES` is 10 MB for inbound mail attachments, uploads are already cut at 8 MB by `validateReceiptFile` since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)) and the item carries only `storagePath`; otherwise (JSON, no key, demo) inline base64 ≤ 1,5 MB as before. Bytes are stored **before** ingest (`persistInboundAttachments` for mail, `storeInboxAttachment` for uploads); `/api/inbox/bilaga/...` reads both (`attachmentBytes`). A file that was too large keeps its metadata and the viewer says *Innehållet finns inte lagrat.*
- **Invariants:** Tenant from **To** slug (`{slug}@in.ferva.se`, alias `in.driva.se`), never From. **No DELETE** on inbox_items. Dedup `(business_id, external_id)`. Badge ≠ open filter. Autopilot books only at high amount confidence or after `reviewedAt`. Website forms → jobs, not inbox.
- **Inbound address = slug + domain** ([PR #100](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/100); `src/lib/inbox/inbound-slug.ts`, `inbound-mail.ts`): displayed domain is `INBOUND_MAIL_DOMAIN` (default `in.ferva.se`); the webhook also accepts alias `in.driva.se` (`INBOUND_MAIL_ALIAS_DOMAINS`) — tenant is resolved from the **local-part only**, never the domain.
  - **Allocation** (`slugFromCompanyName` → `allocateInboundMailSlug`): lowercase, å/ä→a, ö→o, drop the whole words `ab` `hb` `kb` `eftr` `aktiebolag`, keep `[a-z0-9]` only, max 24 chars; fewer than 3 chars → `foretag`. **Reserved** local-parts count as occupied: `demo`, `inbox`, `postmaster`, `noreply`, `support`, `admin`, `mail`, `www`, `root`, `ferva`, `driva` (so *Support AB* → `support2`). Collision → `bas`, `bas2`, `bas3`, …
  - **Where:** Supabase `insertSettingsWithAllocatedSlug` (`adapter-supabase.ts`) in the same transaction as the settings insert; a unique-violation race retries with the next number. JSON/onboarding: `src/lib/onboarding.ts`. Demo seed keeps `demo` (→ `demo@in.ferva.se`); a developer-seeded demo business in Supabase never gets `demo` (`demo-reset.ts` uses the company-name slug and keeps it across reset).
  - **Locked:** the customer never chooses or edits the address. `updateCompanySettings` (`services/settings.ts`) does **not** touch `inboundMailSlug` — renaming the company does not remint.
  - **Legacy hex remint (one-shot):** slugs matching `^[0-9a-f]{12}$` (migration 13 default) are reminted to a readable slug **only if the business has no inbound mail items yet** (`kind === "mail"` or `source` `email`/`vidarebefordrad`). If mail exists, the hex slug stays. Supabase: `remintHexInboundMailSlugs` in `apply-pending-schema.ts`; JSON: `normalize()` in `store.ts` (`shouldRemintHexInboundSlug`).
- **Kvitto pipeline → receipt file ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), commit `8e7a594` *kvittofil följer med från inboxen*):** a `kvitto` inbox item that books (`createExpenseFromKnownReceipt`, `src/lib/services/inbox.ts` → `expenses.ts`) creates `expenses` + a `receipts` row and now passes `item.attachments[0]` along: an **inline** attachment (`contentBase64` + contentType + size) is copied onto the receipt row; a bucket-stored or larger attachment stays on `inbox_items.attachments`, but `src/lib/receipts/receipt-source.ts` (`inboxAttachmentForReceipt`, `receiptFileAvailable`, `receiptsWithAvailableFile`) resolves it through inbox item → `expense_id` → receipt, so **Visa kvitto** (`/api/kvitto/<id>`, `receiptFileContent` falls back to the inbox attachment) and *Kvitto · Bokfört* now appear for inbox-booked kvitton too. *Bokfört · kvittouppgifter utan fil* remains only for rows with no viewable file anywhere (seed data, metadata-only items). Inbox items are never deleted, which is what makes the lookup safe. Pre-#130 this map said the file was **not** copied - outdated.
- **Live (2026-09-12, after [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)):** with the demo cookie `/inbox` lands on `/bokforing/underlag` (title *Underlag · Ferva*, no error card); cookie-less `/inbox` → 307 `/bokforing/underlag` → 307 `/login?next=%2Fbokforing%2Funderlag`, `/inbox/<id>` → 307 `/bokforing/underlag/<id>`. Address `demo@in.ferva.se`. Open items as before: Byggmax *Kontrollera belopp*, Beijer *Bokförd · Redo att betala* (RSC-verified on 2026-09-08 under the old route; not re-read row by row on 2026-09-12). The old separate badge **1** is now part of **Bokföring 7**.
- **Verify:** `/bokforing/underlag` shows the upload zone, the inbound card and Byggmax. Open row → `/bokforing/underlag/inbox-mail-byggmax` (or type `/inbox/inbox-mail-byggmax` and follow the 307). Script: `scripts/verify-nav-browser.ts` expects `demo@in.ferva.se`, but it was written before #137 and still asserts *IA sidebar has Inbox* / *Mer starts with Inbox, Bokföring* - those steps fail on current main by design; do not read that as a regression (not run for this map). Tests: `inbox.test.ts`, `inbox-resend.test.ts`, `inbound-slug.test.ts` (normalisation, reserved → `2`, collision suffixes, hex remint, rename does not remint), `nav.test.ts` (`/inbox*` → `/bokforing/underlag*` in `sanitizeReturnTo`, `defaultBack`, `labelForHref`, crumbs and scroll keys). `scripts/db-validate.ts` asserts DELETE denied. SMTP→Inbox requires verified Receiving MX on `in.ferva.se` (not claimed live from code alone).

## Bokföring

- **User-facing name:** Bokföring
- **Purpose:** Auto-posted ledger; user only answers exceptions + VAT/year-end. Since [PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109) also the full aktiebolag chain: kontoregister, manuella verifikat, skattekonto, lön + AGI, periodstängning, SIE-övertagande, bokslutsbilagor, avstämning, K2-årsredovisning, INK2, myndighetsfiler + inlämning, sjuårsarkiv. Subsections below.
- **Live regression 2026-09-08 - fixed by [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134) (merge `d82238d`):** the production deploy of `a8f6284` rendered the `(app)/error.tsx` card *Sidan kunde inte visas* on every `/bokforing/*` URL (layout-segment chunk `E{"digest":"3069063826"}`, while `curl` looked healthy) because [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) had `src/app/(app)/bokforing/layout.tsx` read `bookkeepingMode()` / `bookkeepingHasPayroll()` / `fiscalYears()` (`db()`) without a tenant load, and a nested layout renders in parallel with `(app)/layout.tsx` and the page - so in Supabase mode (demo included) `db()` threw *Ingen tenantkontext*; JSON dev never reproduced it. #134 made the layout `async` and `await ensurePageBusiness()` (React `cache()`, the same load the shell and the page await) before those reads; no user-facing copy changed. Live on driva-alpha 2026-09-10 with a fresh demo cookie: `/bokforing` (*Kommande till Skatteverket*), `/bokforing/moms`, `/bokforing/skattekonto`, `/bokforing/verifikationer`, `/bokforing/verifikationer/nytt` (zone *Släpp underlaget här*) and `/bokforing/ingaende-balans` (zone *Släpp SIE-filen här*) answered 200 with their `<title>`s, the enkelt tab row of that build (**Översikt · Moms · Skattekonto** - replaced by [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), see below), `button[data-bokforing-mode]` and no error card or layout digest; logged-out `/bokforing` still 307s to `/login?next=%2Fbokforing`. Digest `3069063826` is history - do not re-file it as current.
- **Status 2026-09-12 (main `8b135bb` = [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) *Bokföring för snickaren*, serving on driva-alpha):** everything below is code-verified against `8b135bb`; the live facts marked 2026-09-12 were read with a fresh demo cookie from status codes, `<title>`s and the HTML/RSC text, not clicked through. With the demo cookie: `/bokforing` → *Bokföring · Ferva*, tab row **Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Skatt**, toggle **Visa redovisningsvy**, `[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"]`, `button[data-bokforing-mode="enkelt"]`; `/bokforing/underlag` *Underlag · Ferva*, `/bokforing/bank` *Bank · Ferva*, `/bokforing/skatt` *Skatt · Ferva*, `/bokforing/konton` *Konton · Ferva*; deep links `/bokforing/moms`, `/bokforing/skattekonto`, `/bokforing/verifikationer`, `/bokforing/verifikationer/nytt`, `/bokforing/konton` render with their old titles plus the line *{Sidnamn}. Tillbaka till Att göra* under the row **without** joining the row as an extra tab; with `driva_bokforing_lage=avancerat` the row is **Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Verifikationer · Huvudbok · Rapporter · Moms · Skattekonto · Lön · Bokslut** (Lön and Bokslut show although the seed has no employee and the year is open; **Skatt** is not in that row), toggle **Visa enkel vy**, `[data-bokforing-tabs="avancerat"]`, and `/bokforing/konton` shows the report chips **Saldobalans · Resultat · Balans · Konton**. Cookie-less, all four new routes 307 to `/login?next=…`. No error card, no layout digest. The click-throughs this map marks as JSON-dev-only (declare moms, save OCR, Skapa nästa år, …) were not redone.
- **Enkelt / avancerat läge** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), reshaped by [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137); `src/lib/accounting/bookkeeping-mode.ts`, client-safe keys in `bookkeeping-mode-keys.ts` + `bookkeeping-mode-keys.test.ts`): **missing = `enkelt`**, also for every existing company and the demo. Enkelt = `simpleBookkeepingKeys()` = **Att göra** (`oversikt`, `/bokforing`) · **Underlag** · **Bank** · **Skatt**, plus **Lön** when an employee exists (`bookkeepingHasPayroll()` = `employees().length > 0`) and **Bokslut** when the open year is within 2 months of its end, already past it, or any year is `stangt` (`showYearEnd` in `bokforing/layout.tsx`). Avancerat = `BOKFORING_DETAIL_TABS` **minus `skatt`** (10 tabs: Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Verifikationer · Huvudbok · Rapporter · Moms · Skattekonto · Lön · Bokslut - Lön and Bokslut unconditionally) + the report chips Saldobalans · Resultat · Balans · **Konton** under Rapporter. Hidden tabs are hidden, **not** removed: deep links render, and in enkelt läge a page outside the row (`/bokforing/moms`, `/bokforing/skattekonto`, `/bokforing/verifikationer[/nytt]`, `/bokforing/huvudbok`, the report pages, `/bokforing/konton`, `/bokforing/periodstangning`, `/bokforing/ingaende-balans`, Lön / Bokslut while hidden) gets the line *{Sidnamn}. Tillbaka till Att göra* (label from `matchRoute`, link → `/bokforing`) under the row instead - the pre-#137 behaviour where the hidden tab joined the row while active is **gone**. In avancerat no such line is shown. Toggle = the small text button right-aligned under the row, `button[data-bokforing-mode="enkelt"|"avancerat"]`, label **Visa redovisningsvy** (in enkelt) / **Visa enkel vy** (in avancerat) - not *Visa avancerat* / *Visa enkelt*, and not the demo menu's **Visa redovisningsvyn** (see [Demo mode](#demo-mode)). Wrapper `div[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"|"avancerat"]`. **What the toggle does** (`toggleMode` in `bokforing-advanced-nav.tsx`): flips client state, writes the cookie `driva_bokforing_lage=<mode>` (`BOKFORING_MODE_COOKIE`, `path=/`, `max-age` one year, `samesite=lax`) **and** fires `persistBookkeepingModeAction(next)` without awaiting it (`bokforing-actions.ts`: `withBusiness(…, { capability: "write_accounting" })` → `setBookkeepingModeForUser(userId, mode)` → `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser[userId]` + `save()`; no `revalidatePath` / `refresh()` in that action, so the UI never waits on it). On the first switch to avancerat it also sets the cookie `driva_bokforing_lage_hint=1` and toasts *Redovisningsvyn visar verifikationer, huvudbok och rapporter. Bokföringen är densamma.* (once per browser). **How the mode is read** (`bokforing/layout.tsx`): the cookie wins; without a cookie `bookkeepingMode()` → `bookkeepingModeForUser(currentActor().userId)` → `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser[userId]` → `companyBookkeepingMode()` = the old company-wide `meta.bookkeepingMode` (only a fallback for users who never chose) → `enkelt`. `setBookkeepingModeAction` / `setBookkeepingMode` are gone. Owner surface only - the consultant `CLIENT_TABS` are unchanged. Anything in this map that says “8 tabs” predates #137; the avancerat row has 10.
- **Navigering i ytan (prestandapasset, [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) commit `dd91b05`):** `bokforing/loading.tsx` puts the Suspense boundary **below** the tab row, so a tab change only swaps the content area (`DetailSkeleton`) while the row, the toggle and the app shell stay mounted; `(app)/loading.tsx` would replace the row too and read as a reload (`underlag/loading.tsx` and `underlag/[id]/loading.tsx` add their own skeletons). The tabs use Next's **default** prefetch (the shell down to that boundary) and full-prefetch a single tab only on intent - pointer resting 120 ms (`HOVER_INTENT_MS`), focus or touch (`prefetch={true}` once the key is in `warm`). The old `prefetch={true}` on every tab plus a `router.prefetch()` loop over `BOKFORING_PREFETCH_HREFS` (removed from `nav.ts`) fully rendered every tab view on the server whenever the row was visible, so opening Skattekonto in avancerat also rendered Huvudbok, Verifikationer, Lön, Bokslut and Rapporter - each a full tenant snapshot in Supabase mode.
