Files are rebuilt from the books and listed as `filename · size byte · SHA-256 …`; submit rebuilds and compares - changed underlag drops back to *Fil genererad* with *Underlaget har ändrats sedan signeringen …*. A retry on a kvitterad/avvisad period becomes a **new row** (history kept).
- Signing (`signing.ts`): `selectFilingSigner` = mock BankID (`bankid_mock`, note *Demosignatur – ingen riktig BankID-signering har genomförts.*) only where `bankidSigningAvailable()`; a real business has no signer → button disabled + *Signering med BankID är inte påslagen för det här företaget än. Filen går att hämta och lämna in för hand.* BankID signs the **file content** (`filingSignText` embeds the checksums), never the declaration as an idea. DB checks enforce signature / provider id / receipt per status. Audit `inlamning_genererad|signerad|inlamnad|kvitterad|avvisad`. Errors `role="alert"`.
- Formats (`src/lib/accounting/`): `eskd.ts` (moms, eSKDUpload 6.0, ISO-8859-1), `agi-xml.ts` (AGI, UTF-8), `sru.ts` (INK2 + INK2R + INK2S → INFO.SRU + BLANKETTER.SRU, ISO-8859-1), `ixbrl.ts` (årsredovisning, K2 taxonomy, XHTML UTF-8); `filing-format.ts` (`orgNumber10`, `FilingDataError`). Every file is downloadable without a provider.
- Live demo (mock): Moms periods show **Inlämning · Utkast** with **Förbered inlämning**. Tests: `filing/filing-submission.test.ts`, `accounting/filing.test.ts`.

### Arkiv (sjuårsarkivering)

- **Ladda ner arkivet** on each closed year in Bokslut (`/api/bokforing/export?typ=arkiv&ar=<år>`, `buildFiscalYearArchive`; the API also works for an open year). Zip `<company-slug>-driva-arkiv-<år>-<yyyymmdd>.zip`: `LÄSMIG.txt` (summary + the full BFL 7 kap. 2 § policy), `bokforing/bokforing.se` (SIE 4, PC8), `bokforing/verifikationer.csv`, `huvudbok.csv`, `saldobalans.csv`, `resultatrakning.csv`, `balansrakning.csv`, `underlag/register.csv` (one row per verification: archive path **or why the underlag is missing**), `underlag/kundfakturor.csv`, `underlag/<A12 2026-03-14 Beskrivning>/<file>` per verification (kvitto file, inbox attachment, verifikat bilaga, or a generated `lonespecifikation-<månad>.txt`), `arsredovisning/arsredovisning.txt` when a report exists. Missing files are stated honestly (*Kvittot saknas – köpet har bokförts utan underlag.*, *Behöver inget separat underlag – verifikationen vilar på bokföringen.*), never replaced by placeholders.
- Retention (`src/lib/archive/retention.ts`): *Bevaras till och med {år+7}-12-31 – sju år efter utgången av det kalenderår då räkenskapsåret avslutades* (BFL 7:2 counts from the end of the calendar year the fiscal year ended, not from invoice dates). Ferva **never auto-purges**; a business with bookkeeping cannot be deleted.
- Live demo: `s-dermalms-snickeri-ab-driva-arkiv-2026-20260905.zip`, 10 entries (LÄSMIG, 6 bokföring files, 2 registers, one Beijer Bygg PDF). Tests: `archive-export.test.ts`.

---

## Ekonomi — Utgifter & kvitton / Bank (Bank lives under Bokföring since #137)

Not separate nav items; tabs on `/ekonomi`. Since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) `EKONOMI_TABS` has **three** tabs - **Offerter · Fakturor · Utgifter & kvitton** - the **Bank** tab moved to **Bokföring → Bank** (`/bokforing/bank`; `?flik=bank` → `redirect()` there, see [Legacy path rewrites](#legacy-path-rewrites-srclibnavts)). Every Ekonomi tab now shows the `EconomyBankCard` between the tab row and the register: *Företagskonto* + the connected balance (or *Inget saldo*), *Avstämt till {datum}* when the bank reconciles else *Bankhändelser hanteras under Bokföring*, and the button **Hantera bankhändelser** → `/bokforing/bank` - a summary card, **not** the transaction workspace (seen live 2026-09-12). Page header: subtitle still *Alla offerter, fakturor, utgifter och banktransaktioner – sök och hitta.* and three CTAs **Ny utgift** (`aria-label="Ny utgift"`, → `/ekonomi/utgifter/ny`, [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)) · **Ny faktura** · **Ny offert**. `?atgard=<actionId>` (`src/lib/economy-atgard.ts`, tests `economy-atgard.test.ts`) lands on the right tab and highlights the row: `question-` / `receipt-` / `utgift-` / `supplier-` → Utgifter (`#utgift-<id>`, `data-highlighted="1"` + ring, scrolled to by `ScrollToId`); `bank-<txId>` is now read by `/bokforing/bank?atgard=bank-<txId>` (`highlightFromAtgard(…, "bank")`, `#tx-<id>`, opens the kind picker) and `actionResolveHref` / the attention rows / verification corrections point there. Used by attention CTAs, bank booking, verification corrections and **Ny utgift**'s redirect. There is no `data-atgard` attribute. Default tab is still Offerter.

### Utgifter & kvitton (`?flik=utgifter`)

- **Copy:** *Kvitton och leverantörsfakturor. Åtgärder som behövs dyker upp på Hem och Bokföring.*
- **Upload zone (top of the tab, under the copy - not a header button):** **Släpp kvitton här** = `UploadReceiptButton label="Släpp kvitton här"` (`src/components/money-widgets.tsx`, default label *Lägg till kvitto*) without `expenseId` → `ReceiptUpload` landing (`src/components/receipt-upload.tsx`, wrapper `[data-receipt-upload]`, zone `div[role="button"]` with `aria-label="Släpp kvitton här"`, subtitle *Eller tryck för att välja, fota med kameran eller klistra in en skärmdump. Kvittot läses av och bokförs när uppgifterna räcker.*, formats *PDF, JPG, PNG, HEIC · max 8 MB per fil* (5 MB before [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)); several files by drop / click / Enter / Space, **Fota kvitto** `[data-dropzone-camera]` on touch screens, Ctrl/Cmd+V anywhere on the page while no modal is open). The old **Ladda upp kvitto** button is gone since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) (commit `44c05e7`) + `90d43e3`, both landed on main with [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130) (the command-bar entry **Ladda upp kvitto** still exists and only navigates here). Each file is a queue row (`[data-receipt-upload-row][data-status="vantar"|"laser"|"klar"|"fel"]`, thumbnail, *Väntar · {size}* → *Läser av …*) uploaded **one at a time** as a standalone receipt through the Inbox on the same `FormData` transport as the purchase receipt ([PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120) - no data-URL, no `FileReader`): `inboxDocumentForm(file)` (`src/lib/receipts/read-file.ts`, `RECEIPT_MAX_BYTES` = `UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES` 8 MB) → `uploadInboxDocumentAction(form)` → `receiptFileFromForm` + `validateReceiptFile` (PDF/JPEG/PNG/WebP/HEIC) → `interpretDocumentFile` (`src/lib/services/inbox.ts`, which calls `extractReceipt` in `src/lib/ai/extract-document.ts`) → `storeInboxAttachment` (bytes stored **before** the item, key `upload-<timestamp>`; bucket vs inline rules under [Underlag](#underlag-the-former-inbox) - demo never calls the bucket) → `ingestUploadedDocument` with the file content. **Same action and path as the Underlag zone Släpp kvitton och fakturor här** (`InboxUploadZone` on `/bokforing/underlag` and the Bokföring overview). The reading is booked automatically only when the evidence carries it (see *Kvittotolkning* below); otherwise the receipt lands under Underlag as *Kontrollera belopp*. Row copy after upload: *Bokfört* (`autoBooked`) or *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna* - the old button copy *Kvitto bokfört* / *Kvitto i inboxen* no longer exists. Client cap **8 MB** per file ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), `UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES`; was 5 MB): an oversize file becomes a `fel` row *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* with zero requests (the helper's *Filen är för stor (max 8 MB).* is not reached from this UI). Errors are Swedish via `userFacingStorageError` (fallback *Kunde inte spara dokumentet. Försök igen.*) on the file's row; one `router.refresh()` when the queue finishes with ≥ 1 success. Under the zone: **Registrera för hand:** **Utlägg** · **Milersättning** · **Traktamente** · **Representation** (`ManualExpenseShortcuts` → `/ekonomi/utgifter/ny?typ=…`, see *Ny utgift* below) and, when the company owes the owner, the `OwnerLiabilityBanner` *Bolaget är skyldigt dig {belopp}* / *utlägg och ersättningar som inte förts över*. No demo lock and no template data.
- **Receipt for a specific purchase (file is stored):** CTA **Lägg till kvitto** on the *Kvitto saknas – {leverantör}, {belopp}* attention item (Hem / Bokföring, `attention-list.tsx`, `cta.type === "uploadReceipt"`) or `UploadReceiptButton expenseId=…` on the Utgifter register row / Bank row. Both are **compact drop zones** since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125): the attention item renders the shared `FileDropzone` directly (`div[role="button"]` with `aria-label="Lägg till kvitto"`, formats `UPLOAD_MAX_FORMATS` = *PDF, JPG, PNG, HEIC · max 8 MB* since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) (was *PDF, bild · max 5 MB*), hidden `input[type=file][accept="image/*,.pdf,.heic,.heif"]`, one file, `aria-busy` while pending, *Fota kvitto* icon `aria-label="Fota kvitto"` on touch screens, no queue), the rows render `ReceiptUpload` compact (same zone, `formats` *PDF, JPG, PNG, HEIC · max 8 MB*, no `[data-receipt-upload]` wrapper in this mode; while uploading the zone is replaced by *Läser av …*). Client sends the `File` in a `FormData` (`receiptUploadForm`, `src/lib/receipts/read-file.ts`, max **8 MB** → *Kvittot är för stort (max 8 MB).* as the attention row's error text; the compact `ReceiptUpload` reports *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* under the zone instead) — **not** a data-URL string: React Flight caps string action arguments at ~1e6 chars, so a data URL for anything over ~730 KB failed in decoding with a raw Next error → `uploadReceiptAction(form)` → `receiptFileFromForm` + `validateReceiptFile` (PDF/JPEG/PNG/WebP/HEIC, else *Kvittot måste vara en bild (JPEG/PNG/WebP/HEIC) eller PDF.*; empty browser type falls back to the extension). **Order inside `withBusiness`:** `expenseAwaitingReceipt` (purchase exists, has no receipt) → `storeReceiptFile` against a pre-generated receipt id → `interpretDocumentFile` (AI vision; skipped without `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`) → `uploadReceiptForExpense(…, { id, …stored }, interpreted)` → assert `receiptFileStored(receipt)`. The file is stored **before** any state mutation: `uploadReceiptForExpense` calls `save()` and books the purchase, and a demo session persists dirty state in `finally` even when the action throws — storing first is what guarantees a failed upload leaves *Saknar kvitto* in place (attention item stays, register row stays *Saknar kvitto*, no receipt row). `ok: true` **always** means a stored file (`fileStored: true`, `receiptId`); a filename-only receipt is never a success on this path. Storage failure → `{ ok: false, error }` (the attention row's error text, or the line under the compact zone). Success copy: attention *Kvitto sparat och matchat*; compact zone *Kvitto sparat*. One receipt per purchase (*Köpet hos {leverantör} har redan ett kvitto kopplat.*).
  - **Demo** (`/demo` session file or `is_demo` business — `state.meta.demo`) → **always** inline `content_base64`, max **1,5 MB**; the bucket is never called (demo tenant ids `demo-<…>` are not uuids and demo sessions never touch Supabase). Larger → *Kvittot är för stort för demon (max 1,5 MB). Välj en mindre fil.*
  - Real business, Supabase + `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` → private bucket **`receipts`** at `<business_id>/<receipt_id>/<filename>` (`storage_path`). If the bucket rejects the upload (RLS denial because the configured key is not accepted as service role, network) the error is logged server-side (`[driva:kvitto]`) and the file is stored inline instead when ≤ 1,5 MB; larger → *Kvittot kunde inte sparas i fillagringen just nu. Försök igen om en stund eller välj en fil under 1,5 MB.*
  - JSON store or no service key → inline `content_base64`, max **1,5 MB** (`MAX_INLINE_ATTACHMENT_BYTES`); larger → *Kvittot är för stort för att sparas utan fillagring (max 1,5 MB). Sätt SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY för att aktivera bucketen.*
  - **Errors are always Swedish user-safe:** `uploadReceiptAction` maps everything through `userFacingStorageError` (raw Postgres/RLS/Storage text such as *new row violates row-level security policy* → *Kunde inte spara kvittot. Försök igen.*). Routing logic: `storeReceiptFileWith` (`receipt-file.ts`), unit-tested with a fake bucket.
- **Viewing the file:** `GET /api/kvitto/[receiptId]` — **protected** (`withBusinessRead`, not a public prefix), `Content-Disposition: inline` for PDF/JPEG/PNG/WebP, `attachment` (+ `application/octet-stream`) for HEIC; filename sanitised to `[\w.\-åäöÅÄÖ ]`; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`. 404 *Kvittot finns inte.* for an unknown id; 404 *Kvittofilen finns inte sparad – endast uppgifterna om köpet.* when the row has no stored file.
- **Register rows (`ExpenseRegister`, labels from `economy-list.ts`):** bokförd + receipt **with** file → *Kvitto · Bokfört* + link **Visa kvitto** (`<a href="/api/kvitto/{receiptId}" target="_blank">`, table and card view). bokförd + receipt **without** file (seed data, Inbox-booked kvitton, pre-PR #77 rows) → *Bokfört · kvittouppgifter utan fil*, no link. `ExpenseTableRow.receiptId` is only set when a file exists (`receiptFileStored`).
- **Filters:** Alla, Behöver åtgärd, Redo att betala, Klara.
- **Batch:** *N fakturor är redo att betalas* + **Skapa bankfil**.
- **Live (2026-09-08):** Telia Bankfil skapad; Beijer/Trygg-Hansa redo (*2 fakturor är redo att betalas · totalt 21 205 kr* → **Skapa bankfil**); Söderport *Betalningsuppgifter saknas*; Grand Hôtel *Välj kategori*; Clas Ohlson *Saknar kvitto*. Seeded receipts have no stored file, so all seeded bokförda rows read *Bokfört · kvittouppgifter utan fil*.

### Ny utgift (`/ekonomi/utgifter/ny`)

- **User-facing name:** Ny utgift ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), commit `256674e`). Page `src/app/(app)/ekonomi/utgifter/ny/page.tsx` + `ManualExpenseForm` (`src/components/manual-expense-form.tsx`); pure planner `planManualExpense` / `ownerLiability` / `manualExpenseCategories` in `src/lib/expenses/manual-expense.ts`, schablon tables in `src/lib/accounting/allowances.ts` + `prisbasbelopp.ts`, service `createManualExpense` (`src/lib/services/manual-expense.ts`), action `createManualExpenseAction` (`src/app/actions.ts`); tests `manual-expense.test.ts`.
- **Purpose / copy:** heading **Ny utgift**, subtitle *Köp utan kvitto i banken, privata utlägg, milersättning, traktamente och representation. Ferva räknar schablonerna och konterar.* Back = `tillbaka` / **Utgifter** (`/ekonomi?flik=utgifter`).
- **How to get there:** Ekonomi header **Ny utgift**; the Utgifter tab's **Registrera för hand:** links; direct `?typ=kop|utlagg|milersattning|traktamente|representation` (unknown → `kop`); optional `?uppdrag=<jobId>` (alias `job`) preselects the job. Nothing on Hem, the command bar or the job page links here yet.
- **Form:** `div[role="radiogroup"][aria-label="Typ av utgift"]` with `role="radio"` cards **Köp** (*Betalt med företagets kort eller konto*) · **Utlägg** (*Du betalade privat och ska få tillbaka*) · **Milersättning** (*Körning i tjänsten, skattefri schablon*) · **Traktamente** (*Tjänsteresa med övernattning*) · **Representation** (*Måltid eller fika med kund eller personal*). Köp / Representation show **Vem betalade** (`role="radiogroup"`): **Företagets kort eller konto** (*Dras från företagskontot (1930)*) or **Jag betalade privat** (*Bolaget blir skyldigt dig pengarna (2893)*); Utlägg forces privat, Milersättning / Traktamente always book against 2893; only Milersättning shows the banner *Bokförs som skuld till dig (2893). För över pengarna från företagskontot när det passar – överföringen känns igen i banken och bockar av skulden.* Traktamente uses the one-line card *Hur bokförs det? Skattefritt traktamente (7321) mot skuld till dig (2893), ingen moms.* (see the next bullet). Other fields: supplier/description, date (`DateField`), amount + **Varav moms** (quick rates 25 / 12 / 6 / 0 %, default 25 %, 12 % for representation, *Momsen står på kvittot.*), category (`manualExpenseCategories`), **Uppdrag (valfritt)** (*Kopplas till uppdragets kostnader och lönsamhet.*), receipt via an inline `FileDropzone` **Släpp kvittot här** (`manualExpenseReceiptForm`; **not rendered for Traktamente** since [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170) - the schablon is the underlag), and per type: Milersättning km + bilslag (**Egen bil** / **Förmånsbil (bensin, diesel, hybrid)** / **Förmånsbil (el)** - `VEHICLE_LABELS`, `mileageRatesFor(year)`, `perDiemRatesFor(year)`, `PER_DIEM_DISTANCE_KM = 50` in `allowances.ts`; hint *Schablon {år}: {kr} kr per mil ({kr} kr per km) skattefritt.* - 25 / 12 / 9,5 kr per mil 2023–2026, 18,5 / 6,5 / 9,5 for 2022), Traktamente = the reseräkning fields in the next bullet (the **Hela dagar** / **Halva dagar** / **Nätter utan betald logi** steppers are gone since [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170)), Representation **Sorts representation** (`role="radiogroup"`: **Måltid med kund** · **Fika med kund** · **Personalfest eller intern måltid** · **Fika för personalen**, `REPRESENTATION_LABELS`) + stepper **Antal personer** (`REPRESENTATION_RULES`: VAT base 300 kr/person, schablon 36 kr food / 46 kr with alcohol, *enklare förtäring* deductible up to 60 kr/person). A **Så bokförs det** card previews the kontering (Traktamente shows the one-line card instead, see the next bullet). Submit **Bokför {belopp}** / **Bokför utgiften** (*Bokför …* while pending), **Avbryt**. Stable selectors since [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170): `#expense-kind-{kop|utlagg|milersattning|traktamente|representation}`, `#expense-submit` and the `#traktamente-*` fields below; the other inputs still have neither ids nor names, only the radiogroup / stepper `aria-label`s (*Typ av utgift*, *Vem betalade*, *Sorts representation*, *Varav moms*, *Minska …* / *Öka …*).
- **Traktamente = Skatteverkets reseräkning** ([PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170)): only the blankett's fields. **Vem** (`[data-traktamente-vem]`, read-only: `travellerName()` in `services/manual-expense.ts` = the logged-in actor, generic demo names *Du* / *Ägare* / *Användare* fall back to `settings.name`, same rule as `ordererDefaults`), **Land** (`#traktamente-land`: *Sverige* | *Annat land* → `#traktamente-landnamn`), **Resmål eller arbetsort** (`#traktamente-resmal` = the shared `AddressAutocomplete` with `composeSelected="trip"`, `TRIP_PRIMARY_TYPES = ["geocode"]` and `TRIP_REGION_CODES = []` → city **or** street in any country; prefilled from the selected `job.address`; `formatTripDestination` writes *Göteborg* / *Oslo, Norge* / *Vasagatan 33, 411 24 Göteborg*; `demoTripSuggestions` gives cities with country when there is no Google key), **Avresa** / **Hemkomst** = `DateField` + `input[type="time"]` (`#traktamente-avresetid` / `#traktamente-hemkomsttid`; the avresa date **is** the bookkeeping date, the generic **Datum** field is hidden for this type), **Anledning** (`#traktamente-anledning`, defaults to the uppdrag title), **Fri kost** (`#traktamente-frikost`: *Ingen fri kost* / *Frukost* / *Lunch eller middag* / *Lunch och middag* / *Alla måltider*), **Logi** (`#traktamente-fri-logi` = the company paid the hotel → no nattraktamente) and the 50 km checkbox (`#traktamente-50km`, *Resmålet låg mer än 50 km från både bostaden och den vanliga arbetsplatsen.*). `[data-traktamente-dagar]` shows the derived *1 heldag, 1 halvdag, 1 natt*; `[data-traktamente-belopp]` the skattefritt amount, under it the single line *Hur bokförs det? Skattefritt traktamente (7321) mot skuld till dig (2893), ingen moms.* (the always-visible explanation + notes essay, incl. *ruta 050*, is no longer rendered for this type - the **verification `explanation` is unchanged and complete**, and now also carries the times, the anledning and the fri kost). No kvitto dropzone.
- **Traktamente schablon** (`src/lib/accounting/allowances.ts`, tests `accounting/traktamente.test.ts` + `traktamente-reserakning.test.ts`): `tripDays(departure, arrival)` derives hel dag (avresa **before** 12.00), hel hemkomstdag (**after** 19.00), hela mellandagar and one natt per dygnsbyte; `null` = no övernattning / hemkomst before avresa → the plan refuses with *Fyll i avresa och hemkomst med datum och tid. Hemkomsten måste ligga ett senare dygn - traktamente kräver övernattning.* `freeMealsDeduction` takes Skatteverket's 15 % frukost / 35 % lunch eller middag / 70 % båda / 85 % helt fri kost off the day amount (2026: hel 300 → 45 / 105 / 210 / 255; halvdag 150; the natt is never reduced). **Utland:** `FOREIGN_PER_DIEM_RATES` is an **empty** normalbelopp table (SKV's country list is not in the repo), so `perDiemTripRates` refuses a non-SE trip with **`Utland - saknar schablon för {land}`** instead of silently using the Swedish 300 kr; an unnamed foreign country gives *Skriv vilket land resan gick till.* `PER_DIEM_DISTANCE_KM = 50` is still a question to the user, never a map distance.

- **Posting** (`planManualExpense`): Köp → category account (+ 2641 ingående moms, or 2647/2614 for reverse-charge purchases) against **1930** (företagskonto) or **2893** (privat); Milersättning → **7331** / 2893, no VAT; Traktamente → **7321** / 2893, no VAT; Representation → 6071/6072 (external) or 7631/7632 (internal) + the capped deductible VAT, against 1930 or 2893. Constants `FORETAGSKONTO`, `SKULD_TILL_AGARE`, `INGAENDE_MOMS`, `SKATTEFRI_BILERSATTNING`, `SKATTEFRITT_TRAKTAMENTE`. Everything goes through `postVerification` like every other booking.
- **Storage:** `expenses.paid_by` (`foretagskonto | privat`; NULL = företagskonto, i.e. every pre-#130 purchase), `kind` (`kop | milersattning | traktamente | representation` - **utlägg is stored as `kop` + `paid_by = privat`**, there is no `utlagg` kind), `details` jsonb (`mileage` / `perDiem` / `representation` inputs as they were when booked - körjournal / traceability; `perDiem` also carries `departure` / `arrival` / `freeMeals` / `countryCode` / `countryName` / `paidLodging` / `reason` since [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170), and `schablonPlan` in `services/expenses.ts` replays them so a re-booking gives byte-identical entries and explanation - no migration, the column is jsonb); migration `44_manual_expenses`, pending-schema twin since [PR #134](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/134) (see [Data model overview](#data-model-overview)). Types `Expense.paidBy` / `kind` / `details` in `src/lib/types.ts`.
- **After submit:** redirect `/ekonomi?flik=utgifter&atgard=utgift-{expenseId}` (row highlighted) + toast *{titel} är bokförd* (privat: follow-up text about the 2893 balance; an asset-sized purchase gets *… är registrerat* + the inventarie question with **Till Hem**). Register row (`listExpensesForTable`): category *Milersättning* / *Traktamente* / the representation label / the purchase category, supplier = *Milersättning* / *Traktamente* / the shop, status **Bokfört · betalt privat** (`paidBy === "privat"`) or **Bokfört · utan kvitto** (manual booking without a file); `reference` = the description. The owner's 2893 balance surfaces as the `OwnerLiabilityBanner` on the Utgifter tab; a later bank transfer to the owner is recognised by the bank kinds (*Återbetalning till ägaren*, 2893).
- **Live (driva-alpha 2026-09-08):** `/ekonomi/utgifter/ny` and `?typ=milersattning` answer 200 with the demo cookie and render the radiogroup + *Så bokförs det* + the 2893 hint; the form was **not** submitted on the live demo for this map (JSON dev / `manual-expense.test.ts` cover the postings).

### Kvittotolkning (AI vision)

- **Hook:** `extractReceipt` in `src/lib/ai/extract-document.ts`. Reads an image (`image_url`) or PDF (OpenRouter's `file` part) with the fast model, `tools: []` — the model can never reach the business tools, only the document. Returns an `InboundParsedHint` with per-field confidence. Usage is logged to `assistantAudit` as `llm_document_extract`.
- **The model never gets the last word.** Its self-reported confidence is capped at `MODEL_CONFIDENCE_CEILING = 0.9`, below `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLDS.AUTO` (0.98). A reading only crosses the auto-booking threshold on evidence outside the model (`src/lib/inbox/corroborate.ts`): VAT arithmetic that resolves to a legal Swedish rate (25/12/6 %, ±1 kr) raises it to 0.95 (a strong suggestion), and an exact amount match against an unbooked outgoing bank transaction raises it to 0.99 (auto). Document confidence is the *weakest* of supplier/amount/VAT, never an average.
- **Where it runs:** before ingest, inside the tenant context — `interpretInboundPayload` for inbound mail (both `/api/inbox/inbound` and `/api/inbox/inbound/resend`) and `interpretDocumentFile` for uploads. A sender-supplied `parsed` always wins, so nothing is re-interpreted. A failed or absent interpretation leaves the payload untouched and the document goes to `/inbox/[id]/kontrollera` — the same path as before AI.
- **Not configured:** without `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` the interpretation is skipped entirely and every document lands in the Inbox for review. Nothing is fabricated.
- **Tests:** `src/lib/receipt-extraction.test.ts` (parsing, ceiling, evidence rules, ingest wiring).
- **DB:** `expenses`, `receipts` (`storage_path`, `content_type`, `size_bytes`, `content_base64` — migration `26_receipt_content`; check `receipts_one_storage_chk`: never both `storage_path` and `content_base64`), `supplier_invoices`, `supplier_payments`. Bucket `receipts`.
- **Verify:** `/demo` → `/bokforing` (*Behöver lösas*; on Hem it may sit inside the grouped *N bokföringsfrågor* item) → *Kvitto saknas – Clas Ohlson, 349 kr* (`exp-clas`) → the compact **Lägg till kvitto** zone (`[role="button"][aria-label="Lägg till kvitto"]`; drop a file on it or set the hidden `input[type=file]` next to it) → a PNG/PDF ≤ 1,5 MB (a 1,2 MB file must work) → row resolves with *Kvitto sparat och matchat* → Ekonomi → Utgifter → Clas Ohlson row shows *Kvitto · Bokfört* + **Visa kvitto** → link opens `/api/kvitto/<id>` with the file. Negative: a file > 1,5 MB in demo → inline *Kvittot är för stort för demon (max 1,5 MB). Välj en mindre fil.*, the attention item **stays**, the register row **stays** *Saknar kvitto* (no *kvittouppgifter utan fil*, no receipt row). `/api/kvitto/<id>` in incognito → `/login?next=…`. Standalone upload ([PR #120](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/120), UI from [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) + `90d43e3`, landed on main with [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)): Ekonomi → Utgifter → **Släpp kvitton här** (or `/bokforing/underlag` → **Släpp kvitton och fakturor här**) with a ~1,2 MB PNG/PDF must work (the old data-URL path failed above ~730 KB) → a `[data-receipt-upload-row]` ends with `data-status="klar"` and *Bokfört* / *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna*; a file > 8 MB → row `data-status="fel"` *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* with **zero** requests ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137); the cap was 5 MB before); in demo a 1,5-8 MB file is accepted (interpreted when `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` is set) but its bytes are not kept (item carries metadata only, viewer *Innehållet finns inte lagrat.*). Tests: `src/lib/receipt-file.test.ts` (incl. the `FormData` round-trips for kvitto / inboxdokument / underlag and the client caps mirroring the server caps), `src/lib/server-action-errors.test.ts` (inbox upload and manual verifikat take `File` in `FormData`, store before the row/booking, answer in Swedish; the client components contain no `readAsDataURL` / `dataUrl`), `src/lib/inbox-attachment-file.test.ts`; adapter round-trip (*kvitto med inline-fil rundresas*) in `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` (`npm run test:adapter`).

### Bank (`/bokforing/bank`) — Open Banking AIS via Tink

- **Where:** since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) the bank workspace is the Bokföring tab **Bank** at `/bokforing/bank` (`src/app/(app)/bokforing/bank/page.tsx`, `<title>` *Bank · Ferva*, in both enkelt and avancerat läge; `ROUTES` label *Bank*, back **Bokföring**), header title **Bank**, subtitle *Transaktioner, matchning och regler. Banken bokförs här, inte under Ekonomi.*, body `BankWorkspace` (`src/components/bank-workspace.tsx` = the connection card `BankConnectionCard`, the `BankInboxStrip` with `filterHref="/bokforing/bank?status=atgard"`, the `BankRegister` and the `BankRulesCard`; the old inline card in `ekonomi/page.tsx` is gone). `/ekonomi?flik=bank[&…]` redirects here (see [Legacy path rewrites](#legacy-path-rewrites-srclibnavts)); Ekonomi keeps only the summary `EconomyBankCard` (*Företagskonto* · saldo · **Hantera bankhändelser**). Everything that used to link to `/ekonomi?flik=bank` now links to `/bokforing/bank`: the Hem money tiles, attention rows / `actionResolveHref` (`?atgard=bank-<txId>`), *Banken stämmer inte mot bokföringen*, the moms / periodstängning / bokslut checklists (**Öppna banken**), `connectBankAction`'s mock redirect (`/bokforing/bank?bank=kopplad`), the Utgifter empty state **Koppla företagskontot**, the owner-liability banner **Till banken**, the Kom igång task `connect_bank`, Inställningar → Fakturering *Hantera bankkoppling →* (`connectedBankSummary`) and `verification-correction`'s *Matchningen är fel*. **Not** updated in #137: `GET /api/bank/tink/callback` still `303`s to `/ekonomi?flik=bank&bank=kopplad|avbrutet|fel[&meddelande=…]` and `revalidatePath("/ekonomi")` - the Ekonomi redirect then keeps only `q` / `status` / `sida` / `atgard` / `sort` / `direction`, so after a **live Tink** round trip the user lands on `/bokforing/bank` **without** the `bank=` param and the `FlashToast` (*Banken är kopplad* / *Kopplingen avbröts* / the error) never fires; the mock **Koppla företagskonto** path (client `router.push` to `/bokforing/bank?bank=kopplad`) still toasts. Code-read only - the Tink flow is not exercisable on the demo; UNKNOWN whether the dropped toast is intended. Live 2026-09-12: `/bokforing/bank` with the demo cookie renders the subtitle, the seeded account *Företagskonto* (`bank_accounts.name`) with *SEB ···· 4512* and the *Demo-bank* badge; cookie-less → `/login?next=%2Fbokforing%2Fbank`.
- **Purpose:** Fetch balance + transactions from the business account and run them through payment matching. **Account information only** — Ferva never moves money (no PIS/VRP; payments are pain.001 files).
- **Empty:** *Ingen bank kopplad ännu* + existing explanation + primary **Koppla företagskonto** + line *Du loggar in hos banken via Tink. Ferva hämtar saldo och transaktioner för att matcha fakturor. Vi kan inte föra över pengar.* (`BANK_SECONDARY_LINE`, also shown under the card while not connected).
- **Connection states** (`BankConnectionStatus`, labels in `status-labels.ts` → `BANK_CONNECTION_STATUS`): `disconnected` *Ingen bank kopplad* · `pending` *Väntar på banken* · `connected` *Kopplad* · `error` *Kopplingen misslyckades* · `revoked` *Frånkopplad*. Card shows bank name + masked account (`SEB · ···· 4512`) + *Senast uppdaterad …* + balance. Demo adds badge *Demo-bank*.
- **Actions (connected):** **Uppdatera** (fetch new tx → `registerBankTransactions` → matching; shows *N nya transaktioner*; same `externalId` is not re-imported or re-matched but **Motpart/Beskrivning/referens are refreshed** from Tink). **Koppla från** (confirm modal → revokes Tink credentials; **transactions and verifications stay**, status `revoked`, list still visible, **Koppla företagskonto** offered again). Pending: *Fortsätt hos banken* / *Avbryt kopplingen*. Error: *Försök igen*.
- **Filters:** Alla, Behöver åtgärd, Bokförda. **Default is *Behöver åtgärd* whenever unbooked rows exist** (no `status` param, no search, no `?atgard=`); otherwise *Alla*. Status chip is never a bare *Behöver åtgärd* when the row knows more: *Förslag: Bankavgift* (info), *Förslag: Leverantörsbetalning*, *Kvitto saknas*, *Välj kategori*, *Välj typ*, or the attention label (*Matcha betalning*).
- **Inbox strip (`BankInboxStrip`, `bankInboxSummary()` in `economy-list.ts`):** *N transaktioner att hantera* · *N har förslag* · *N väntar på kvitto* · open in/out sums · badge *Banken stämmer* / *Obokade rader kvar* / *Oförklarad skillnad X kr* (from `bankReconciliation`). **Bokför N föreslagna** opens a modal listing exactly what will be booked (counterpart · date · label · amount) → `bookSuggestedBankTransactionsAction(txIds)` → `bookSuggestedBankTransactions` (each row booked via its own domain path, one failure never stops the others; failures shown inline). Empty: *Alla banktransaktioner är hanterade* + reconciled-through date.
- **Bank kinds (`src/lib/banking/bank-kinds.ts`, pure catalog):** what a tx is when it is not a customer payment, supplier payment or card purchase. Outgoing: *Köp med kort* (→ receipt flow), *Inbetalning till skattekontot* (1630/1930), *Löneutbetalning* (no posting — link to `/bokforing/lon`; the payroll run already credited 1930), *Bankavgift* (6570), *Ränta på lån* (8410), *Amortering på lån* (2350), *Företagsförsäkring* (6310), *Utbetald utdelning* (2898), *Återbetalning till ägaren* (2893), *Överföring till eget konto* (1940). Incoming: *Kundbetalning* (→ invoice picker), *Insättning från ägaren* (2893), *Nytt lån utbetalt* (2350), *Ränteintäkt* (8310), *Utbetalning från skattekontot* (1630), *Övrig ersättning* (3990). Both: *Redan bokförd* (link to an existing verification), *Något annat* (link to Verifikationer). Each kind: `label`, `hint`, `direction`, `entries(amount)`, `explanation`, `pattern` (regex heuristics; specific patterns are ordered before *överföring*), `learnable`, `matchedType` (only existing DB enum values `utgift|skatt|ovrigt` — no migration).
- **Suggestions (`bankKindSuggestion`, `services/bank-booking.ts`), in order of strength:** (1) **learned counterpart rule** (`meta.bankCounterpartRules`, key = `merchantRuleKey(counterpart)`, `{ kind, count, lastUsedAt, counterpart }`): count 1 → SUGGEST, count ≥ 2 (`RULE_AUTO_THRESHOLD`) → AUTO_EXECUTE; *redan_bokford* and *kortkop* rules are automatic after one confirmation; (2) **already booked** (`alreadyBookedCandidates`): unlinked, uncorrected verifications whose net on 1930 equals the tx amount within 10 days (payroll is the typical case) → SUGGEST when exactly one; (2b) an owner reimbursement match (`ownerReimbursementSuggestion`); (3) **pattern** → SUGGEST, **except two auto cases added by [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)** (`bank-booking.ts`): a *bankavgift* whose counterpart matches the connected bank's own name (`ownBankFeeSuggestion`, reason *Avgiften kommer från din egen bank ({bank}) och bokförs som bankavgift*) and an outgoing *skattekonto* transfer whose text says skatteverk / skattekonto / SKV **and** whose amount equals `fSkattPerMonth`, a `deklarerad` moms report's `attBetala` or an AGI's `attBetala` (`skattekontoExactSuggestion`, reason *{kr} matchar en F-skatt, en deklarerad moms eller en AGI som väntar på inbetalning*) are `AUTO_EXECUTE` at import time; every other pattern stays a suggestion. The seeded *SEB · Månadsavgift Företagspaket −79 kr* predates the import path, so on the demo it still sits in the queue as the one-click *Bokför SEB 79 kr som bankavgift?*. `paymentSuggestionForTransaction` returns `kind: "bank_kind"` (+ `bankKind`, `bankKindLabel`, `bankKindSource: regel|verifikation|monster`, `verificationId`) only after invoice / ROT / supplier / refund matching found nothing. `processIncomingTransaction` auto-books AUTO_EXECUTE kinds via `bookBankTransactionAs(by: "auto")` (verification `createdBy: "auto"`, explanation starts with the rule reason); a *kortkop* rule forces `createExpenseFromBankPurchase(tx, { force: true })`.
- **Row actions (`BankRowActions`):** `book_kind` → primary *Bokför som bankavgift* / *Koppla till A123* + checkbox *Kom ihåg för {motpart}* (hidden when the suggestion already comes from a rule); `confirm_supplier_payment` → *Boka betalningen* (`confirmSupplierPaymentMatch`, closes the old dead end); `categorize` → *Välj vad det är* opens the **kind picker** (or a link: *Öppna Lön* for salary before the payroll run); every unbooked row also gets *Annat …* / *Inte ett köp …* / *Inte en kundbetalning …* → picker. **Picker (`BankKindPicker`, `[data-bank-kind-picker]`):** radio rows per direction (`bankKindsFor`), *Redan bokförd* expands the candidate verifications (radio) or explains that none matches within ten days, *Kundbetalning* hands over to the invoice picker, kinds with `href` show *Öppna*, footer *Kom ihåg för {motpart}* (learnable kinds only) + **Bokför** / **Koppla** / **Vänta på kvitto**. Existing rule shown as *Regel: … · Glöm regeln*. Booking a kind removes an auto-created *Kvitto saknas* placeholder expense (never one that already has a receipt — error instead).
- **Learned rules card (`BankRulesCard`, under the register):** *Ferva har lärt sig N motparter* → list counterpart → kind, count, last used, badge *Automatiskt* / *Förslag*, **Ta bort** (`forgetBankCounterpartRuleAction`). Rules live in `DB.meta.bankCounterpartRules` = the `businesses.meta` jsonb (next to `merchantCategoryRules`) — no migration.
- **Hem:** `bookBankKind` / `confirmSupplierPayment` CTAs render as one-click buttons in `AttentionList`; rows without a safe guess get *Välj typ* (deep link `/bokforing/bank?atgard=bank-<id>` opens the picker) or *Öppna Lön*. Server actions: `bookBankTransactionAsAction(txId, kind, { verificationId?, remember? })` → `{ ok, summary, learned?: "suggest"|"auto" }`, `confirmSupplierPaymentMatchAction`, `forgetBankCounterpartRuleAction`, `bookSuggestedBankTransactionsAction`. Tests: `src/lib/bank-inbox.test.ts`. Demo seed includes an unbooked *SEB · Månadsavgift Företagspaket −79 kr* so the suggestion flow is visible.
- **Tx columns:** **Motpart** = `merchantInformation.merchantName` else payer/payee name (counterparty on the other side first) else `descriptions.display` else `descriptions.original`, else *Okänd*. **Beskrivning** = `descriptions.original` else `detailed.unstructured` else `display`, omitted when it equals Motpart (Demo Bank often only has one text). `reference` is appended when present. Mapping in `tink/transaction-labels.ts`.

**Three modes (`selectBankProvider`, `src/lib/banking/select.ts`):**

| Mode | When | Behaviour |
|--|--|--|
| **Mock** (`MockBankProvider`) | `/demo`, `is_demo` business, JSON store, or `DRIVA_DEMO=1` — regardless of env | Connect is instant, no redirect. Creates account *SEB ···· 4512* (opening balance 48 250 kr) + synthetic tx: OCR payment for the oldest open invoice (auto-booked), a payment without OCR (*Matcha betalning*), a card purchase without receipt (*Behöver åtgärd*). **Zero HTTP to tink.com / link.tink.com.** |
| **Sandbox / live** (`LiveTinkProvider`) | Real business **and** `TINK_CLIENT_ID`, `TINK_CLIENT_SECRET`, `TINK_REDIRECT_URI` all set (`TINK_MARKET` default `SE`, `TINK_ENV` default `sandbox`) | Server creates a permanent Tink user (`external_user_id` = business id), delegates, builds the Tink Link URL (Transactions · connect-accounts, `market=SE`, `locale=sv_SE`, `state`=nonce.businessFingerprint, `test=true` when sandbox), client does **`window.location.assign`** (full page, never iframe). Callback validates state, exchanges token, imports 90 days of booked tx. **No `financial_services_segments`** — `BUSINESS` routes Link to `business-transactions`, which breaks the permanent-user flow (`REQUEST_FAILED_CREATE_AUTHORIZATION_CODE` after bank login). If a consent already exists on the Tink user (bank approved but the callback never reached us → `INVALID_STATE_DUPLICATE_CREDENTIALS` on retry), **Koppla** adopts it via `GET /api/v1/credentials/list` instead of a new Link round. |
| **Production-not-enabled** (`UnconfiguredBankProvider`) | Real business, env missing/incomplete | Every action returns *Bankkoppling är inte konfigurerad*; nothing crashes, nothing is faked. `TINK_ENV=production` merely drops `test=true` — Tink production access is a separate commercial step, not enabled by this code. |

- **Routes:** server actions `src/app/bank-actions.ts` (`connectBankAction`, `refreshBankAction`, `disconnectBankAction`, `cancelBankConnectAction`); callback `GET /api/bank/tink/callback` (= `TINK_REDIRECT_URI`, **requires the session cookie** — not a public prefix; proxy bounces to `/login?next=` and back). Callback still redirects to `/ekonomi?flik=bank&bank=kopplad|avbrutet|fel` (unchanged in [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)), which Ekonomi forwards to `/bokforing/bank` **without** the `bank=` / `meddelande=` params - see *Where* above; the mock connect goes straight to `/bokforing/bank?bank=kopplad` → toast, param stripped.
- **Files:** `src/lib/banking/provider.ts` (interface + CSRF state), `providers/{mock,tink,unconfigured}.ts`, `select.ts`, `connection-state.ts` (`bankConnectionView()` — the only thing the UI reads; never tokens), `errors.ts` (Swedish texts, no raw Tink JSON), `tink/{config,client,amounts,transaction-labels}.ts` (15 s timeouts, injectable transport for tests, `unscaledValue/scale` → whole kronor at the boundary, ADR-1; Motpart/Beskrivning from distinct Tink fields). UI: `src/components/bank-connection.tsx` (buttons), `src/components/bank-workspace.tsx` (`BankConnectionCard` + `BankWorkspace`, page `src/app/(app)/bokforing/bank/page.tsx`), the summary `EconomyBankCard` in `src/app/(app)/ekonomi/page.tsx`.
- **DB:** `bank_accounts` (+ `external_id` = Tink account id, idempotent re-import), `bank_transactions` (unique `external_id`), **`bank_connections`** (migration 27: status, `tink_user_id`, `credentials_id`, `access_token` + expiry, pending CSRF state, bank name, masked account, `last_sync_at`, `last_error`). RLS grants **`driva_app` only** — no `authenticated` policy, so tokens are unreachable via the Data API. Demo reset deletes the row.
- **Env:** `TINK_CLIENT_ID`, `TINK_CLIENT_SECRET`, `TINK_REDIRECT_URI` (byte-for-byte equal to the Console redirect URI), `TINK_MARKET`, `TINK_ENV`. Server-only; never `NEXT_PUBLIC_TINK_*`. Demo Bank usernames are never stored in env or repo.
- **Errors (user-facing only):** *Banken godkände inte kopplingen. Försök igen.* (auth/declined/401/403) · *Tillfälligt fel hos banken. Försök igen.* (network, timeout, 5xx, bad JSON) · *Bankkoppling är inte konfigurerad* · *Kopplingen kunde inte verifieras. Försök igen.* (state mismatch). `USER_CANCELLED` from Tink Link is **not** an error → *Kopplingen avbröts.*
- **Invariants:** production without bank must not fake payments; **Simulera inbetalning** / demo receipt tx require `hasConnectedBank()` (mock only); a demo business can never reach Tink even if the live provider were selected (`assertNotDemo`); a live business never gets mock data.

**Verify (mock, JSON dev on :3123):** `POST /api/dev/reset {"mode":"empty"}` → `/bokforing/bank` (or `/ekonomi?flik=bank`, which redirects there) shows empty state with **Koppla företagskonto** + secondary line → click → card *SEB · ···· 4512* · *Kopplad* · *Senast uppdaterad* · Uppdatera / Koppla från, toast *Banken är kopplad*; tx list has *Clas Ohlson* → **Uppdatera** shows *N nya transaktioner* → **Koppla från** → confirm → *Frånkopplad*, list still populated, **Koppla företagskonto** back. Puppeteer request listener: no request to `tink.com`. Tests: `src/lib/bank-connection.test.ts` (selection, CSRF, öre→kr, error mapping, mock + live via fake transport), `economy-list.test.ts`, `bank-inbox.test.ts`.

**Verify (sandbox, Vercel with env, real non-demo business):** Bokföring → Bank → **Koppla företagskonto** → full-page redirect to `link.tink.com/1.0/transactions/connect-accounts?…&test=true` → choose **Demo Bank** → log in as Tink's *Demo Bank User 1* (credentials from Tink docs, not stored here) → approve → the callback 303s to `/ekonomi?flik=bank&bank=kopplad`, Ekonomi forwards to `/bokforing/bank` and the card reads *Demo Bank · ···· NNNN* · *Kopplad* - expect **no** *Banken är kopplad* toast after the live round trip until the callback targets `/bokforing/bank` directly (code-read, see *Where*). Transactions appear; Motpart and Beskrivning differ when Tink sent both. A matching OCR payment books an invoice. **Uppdatera** re-syncs (7-day overlap, idempotent) and refreshes labels on existing rows. **Koppla från** → `DELETE /api/v1/credentials/{id}` → *Frånkopplad*, history intact. Cancel in Tink Link → *Kopplingen avbröts. Inget har ändrats.*
