## Hemsida

- **User-facing name:** Hemsida
- **Purpose:** Public marketing site + contact form + optional `.se` domain.
- **Routes:** `/hemsida` (editor), `/hemsida/doman`, public `/sajt` and `/sajt?preview=1`, customer policy `/integritetspolicy`. Domain status poll via server action `pollDomainAction` (`src/app/domain-actions.ts`; the old `GET /api/domains/[id]/status` route is removed).
- **How to get there:** Nav Hemsida (if feature on). Command *Skapa en hemsida*. Settings Funktioner **Aktivera Hemsida**. Disabled URL → `/installningar?flik=funktioner`.
- **Editor tabs:** Innehåll, Design, Inställningar (in `SiteEditorShell`).
- **Draft = safe workspace** ([PR #66](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/66)): on a **published** site every edit lands in the draft layer - section text/images/CTA/hours, ordering, visibility, add/remove and primary CTA (`draftSections`, `draftPrimaryCta`, via `mutableSections` / `mutablePrimaryCta`) plus theme/accent, footer and privacy (`draftDesign`, `draftFooter`, `draftPrivacyPolicy`). Builder `/hemsida` and `/sajt?preview=1` render the draft (`websiteDraftView`); public `/sajt` stays on the published version until Publicera. A never-published site is a draft in its entirety (no draft layer). Edits autosave; **no leave confirmation** when navigating away from `/hemsida` - the draft survives leave/return. Reverting an edit back to the published content clears that draft automatically (`clearMatchingSectionDrafts` in `touchSite`), so no false *Opublicerade ändringar*.
- **Main actions:** AI generate (*Vad gör ditt företag?*, `#hemsida-ai-beskrivning`), edit sections, **Publicera hemsidan** / **Publicera ändringar** (`PublishWebsiteButton`), **Återställ** (`RestoreWebsiteDraftButton`, only when live **and** `hasUnpublishedWebsiteDrafts`), Öppna i ny flik (`/sajt` when live, else `/sajt?preview=1`), Kopiera länk, domain search (`#doman-sok`). Mobile: `StickyMobileActions` holds Återställ + Publicera when dirty.
- **Publicera (snapshot publish):** the client sends its current editor snapshot (`WebsiteEditorSyncProvider.getSnapshot()`: revision, design, footer, privacy, section order/visibility, pending section updates, primary CTA) in `publishWebsiteAction` → `publishWebsite(input)`; the server persists + publishes in the same commit, so Publicera never waits on a pending autosave. Button disabled/deduped while in flight (`beginPublish`). Confirm modal *Publicera hemsidan* → **Publicera**; the success view *Hemsidan är publicerad* + **Öppna sajten** is shown only after the server confirms (revision published, `hasUnpublishedDrafts` false) - otherwise it refreshes or shows *Publiceringen hann inte med senaste ändringen. Försök igen.* A failed publish keeps the editor state. Newer client edits than the snapshot survive as drafts (`keepNewerDrafts`).
- **Återställ:** lightweight confirm - title *Återställ ändringar?*, body *Alla opublicerade ändringar tas bort och hemsidan återställs till den publicerade versionen.*, buttons **Avbryt** / **Återställ**. `restoreWebsiteDraftAction` → `restorePublishedWebsiteDraft()` deletes every draft field and bumps `draftRevision` + `publishedRevision`, then `sync.resetToServer` + `router.refresh()`. After restore draft = published and the badge is gone. Requires `status === "publicerad"`. Not the same thing as the banner *Din tidigare hemsida är återställd* (`?aterstalld=1` / paused site after feature re-activation).
- **Revision guard:** `draftRevision` / `publishedRevision` (`src/lib/website-publish.ts`: `isStaleWebsiteWrite`, `acceptWebsiteWrite`). Every autosave carries `clientRevision` (`enqueueWebsiteMutation`); a write with revision ≤ `publishedRevision` or < `draftRevision` is a no-op, so late or stale autosaves after Publicera / Återställ cannot recreate a draft. A publish snapshot older than `publishedRevision` fails with *Utkastet är för gammalt. Ladda om sidan och försök igen.*
- **Statuses:** Utkast, Publicerad, Pausad, Opublicerade ändringar (badge next to the preview URL; `hasUnpublishedWebsiteDrafts` = published and design, footer, privacy, sections **or** primary CTA differ from published). Header subtitle *Publicerad {datum} · opublicerade ändringar*. `/sajt?preview=1` shows the banner *Förhandsvisning av opublicerade ändringar …* when any of `draftDesignPending` / `draftFooterPending` / `draftPrivacyPending` / `draftContentPending` (sections + CTA) is set (`public-site.ts`).
- **Related:** Inställningar company identity (logo/address reused). Form creates **jobs** (`source: "web_form"`, message in `jobs.original_message`) that show up as **Ny förfrågan** on `/uppdrag`, as *Nytt uppdrag: {title}* on Hem and as the *Ny förfrågan från kunden* card on the job - see [Uppdrag](#uppdrag); owner mail via Notiser `forfragan`.
- **Components:** `site-widgets.tsx` (`SectionList`, `PublishWebsiteButton`, `RestoreWebsiteDraftButton`), `website-editor-sync.tsx` (`WebsiteEditorSyncProvider`, `useWebsiteEditorSync`, `enqueueWebsiteMutation`), `site-editor-shell.tsx`, `site-section-builder.tsx`, `site-design-widgets.tsx`, `site-footer-settings.tsx`, `domain-widgets.tsx`, `website-form-recipient.tsx`, `privacy-policy-settings.tsx`. Logic: `src/lib/services/website.ts` (`publishWebsite`, `publishedWebsiteSnapshot`, `restorePublishedWebsiteDraft`), `website-drafts.ts` (`websiteDraftView`, `draftWebsiteSections`, `hasUnpublishedWebsiteDrafts`), `website-publish.ts`, `public-site.ts`.
- **DB:** `websites` - published `sections`, `primary_cta`, `design`, `footer`, privacy columns + draft layer `draft_sections`, `draft_primary_cta` (new in PR #66), `draft_design`, `draft_footer`, `draft_privacy_policy`, revisions `draft_revision`, `published_revision` (migrations `20260831140000_23_website_revisions.sql`, `20260831143000_23_website_draft_workspace.sql`; mirrored in `apply-pending-schema.ts` + `mappers.ts`). `domains`. Flags: `meta.features.website`, `meta.websitePausedAt`.
- **Invariants:** Deactivate **pauses** public site; does not delete. Live public = feature on + `status === "publicerad"` + not paused. Public `/sajt` never renders draft data - only `?preview=1` and the builder do. A draft is never dropped by navigation; only Publicera (promotes) or Återställ (discards) ends it. `websiteNotificationEmail` ≠ company email unless set.
- **Selectors:** no `data-testid` on Publicera / Återställ or their modals - use button text (**Publicera ändringar**, **Återställ**), modal titles and the badge text.
- **Live:** header *Publicerad {datum}* is **relative** — the seed sets `publishedAt: d(90)` (90 days before today), so it read *3 juni 2026* on 2026-09-01 and *9 juni 2026, 12:00* on 2026-09-07; never assert a fixed date. URL `driva.site/sodermalms-snickeri`; no custom domain yet.
- **Verify (demo, seed site is published):**
  1. `/demo` → **Hemsida** → edit a section heading → badge **Opublicerade ändringar**, **Återställ** appears. `/sajt` unchanged; `/sajt?preview=1` shows the edit + the preview banner.
  2. Go to Hem and back to `/hemsida` → no leave modal, the edit is still there.
  3. **Återställ** → *Återställ ändringar?* → **Återställ** → badge gone, heading back to published.
  4. Edit again → **Publicera ändringar** → **Publicera** → *Hemsidan är publicerad*; `/sajt` updated, badge gone, no *Opublicerade ändringar* after reload.
  5. Form submit → new job, not inbox.
  Tests: `website-*.test.ts` - `website-publish-race.test.ts` (snapshot publish, late saves, draft workspace, Återställ, revision guard), `website-drafts.test.ts`, `website-schema.test.ts`. Script: `scripts/verify-privacy-browser.ts`.

---

## Inställningar

- **User-facing name:** Inställningar
- **Purpose:** Company identity, invoice/payment defaults, optional features, account.
- **Route:** `/installningar?flik=kom-igang|foretag|fakturering|notiser|funktioner|konto` (`src/lib/settings-routes.ts`); `flik=grossister` exists **only** while Grossistbeställningar is active (otherwise → `flik=funktioner`). Legacy `flik=standardval` → fakturering, `flik=notifieringar` → notiser. Deep-link `?falt=name|orgNumber|vatNumber|address|…`.
- **How to get there:** Sidebar/Mer **Inställningar**. Billing blockers and domain “complete company” deep-link here.
- **Tabs:** Kom igång · Företag · Fakturering & betalning · Notiser · Funktioner · (Grossister) · Konto. `SETTINGS_TABS` is the feature-off list; `settingsTabsFor(features)` inserts **Grossister** after Funktioner when active. **Kom igång** = the permanent setup center (profile, tasks, imports) — see [Kom igång](#kom-igång-setup-center).
- **Företag:** logo (`ImageDropzone` *Klicka eller släpp logotyp här · JPG, PNG eller WebP · Valfritt*), name, org.nr, VAT, address, contact, **Räkenskapsår** (below). Save *Spara ändringar*. `#installningar-saknas`. Address = shared `AddressFields` (label **Gatuadress**): `#installningar-address`, `#installningar-postalCode`, `#installningar-city`. Renaming the company does **not** change the inbound mail address — `inbound_mail_slug` is locked at create and not editable here (see [Underlag](#underlag-the-former-inbox)); unchanged by #124.
- **Vad Ferva stödjer för ditt företag** (card on Företag below Verifierade uppgifter, `scope-card.tsx`; spec §10): eligibility verdict for this business, status of consultant cases (*Godkänt av …* / *Väntar på konsultens godkännande*), editable scope answers saved via `updateScopeFlagsAction` → `updateScopeFlags`. See [Supportmatris & eligibility](#supportmatris--eligibility-spec-10).
- **Verifierade uppgifter** (card on Företag under the company fields, `company-claims-card.tsx`, saves immediately via `updateCompanyClaimsAction` → `updateCompanyClaims`; spec §8): Ferva never asserts F-skatt or insurance on its own. Two active confirmations: **Företaget är godkänt för F-skatt** (`[data-claim-fskatt-confirm]`, date ≤ today, optional source) and **Företaget har ansvarsförsäkring** (`[data-claim-insurance-confirm]`, insurer, *Giltig till och med* ≥ today, optional number/link; no file upload). Stored in `business_settings.claims` jsonb (migration 54; `CompanyClaims` in `types.ts`; pure helpers `src/lib/company-claims.ts`). Effects: quote terms system text gains *Vi är godkända för F-skatt.* / *Vi har ansvarsförsäkring hos X.* only while verified (`systemQuoteTerms`/`resolveQuoteTerms` in `standard-quote-terms.ts`); *Godkänd för F-skatt* in quote and invoice footers only when verified and frozen in `sellerSnapshot.fSkattConfirmedAt` at send/issue (older documents without the flag never show it); website generator “Om oss” appends *Vi har F-skatt och ansvarsförsäkring.* only when verified. Expired insurance → `[data-claim-insurance-expired]` notice and the claim disappears. Custom quote terms are never overwritten; the legacy system text (“Vi innehar F-skattsedel och full ansvarsförsäkring”) is treated as an untouched default (nulled by migration 54, never re-stored by the form). Hooks: `[data-company-claims]`, `[data-claims-save]`. Tests: `company-claims.test.ts`.
- **Räkenskapsår** (card below the company fields on Företag, own buttons - independent of **Spara ändringar**; [PR #124](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/124), landed on main with [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130); tests `accounting/fiscal.test.ts`): `FiscalYearSettings` (`src/components/fiscal-year-settings.tsx`), eyebrow **Räkenskapsår**, copy *Huvudbok, rapporter, moms och bokslut utgår från det här året. Default är 1 januari–31 december. Ett brutet år går bra – ange bara start och slut.* **Startdatum** `#installningar-rakenskapsar-start` / **Slutdatum** `#installningar-rakenskapsar-slut` are `DateField` triggers (`<button aria-haspopup="dialog">` whose text is the picked date, *1 januari 2026* / *31 december 2026*; click opens the portaled calendar `role="dialog"` `aria-label="Välj datum"` with **Föregående månad** / **Nästa månad** and day buttons - no typeable `<input type=date>`). The card edits the **current** year (the one containing today, else the last one) and shows *{label}: {start}–{slut}* under the fields (*2026: 1 januari 2026–31 december 2026*; *· stängt* when closed) or *Inget år är sparat ännu – 1 januari–31 december används tills du sparar.*; the list of all years appears only when there is more than one. **Spara räkenskapsår** (disabled until a date changed; *Sparar …* → ✓ *Sparat*) → `updateFiscalYearPeriodAction({ fiscalYearId?, startDate, endDate })` → `updateFiscalYearPeriod` (`src/lib/accounting/fiscal.ts`): open years only (*Ett stängt räkenskapsår kan inte ändras.* - when the current year is closed the button is replaced by *Stängda år ändras inte. Öppna året igen under Bokslut om datumen måste rättas.*), end must be after start (*Slutdatum måste vara efter startdatum.*), otherwise any length (nothing enforces 12 months and there is no 12- or 18-month limit in code - a broken year such as 1 May–30 April or a long first year is accepted; existing verifications never block the change), label re-derived by `fiscalYearLabel` (`2026` while start and end fall in the same calendar year, `2026/2027` when they do not), audit `rakenskapsar_andrat`, verifications keep their booking dates (period = filter). **Skapa nästa år** (*Skapar …* while pending) → `createNextFiscalYearAction` → `createNextFiscalYear`: starts the day after the base year's end and is as long as a calendar year (`fiscalYearFollowing`, 29 Feb → 28 Feb), base = the latest **ended** year, else the current one (`fiscalYearToExtend`); idempotent - the button is hidden once the successor exists and the domain returns the existing year; audit `rakenskapsar_skapat`. Errors `#installningar-rakenskapsar-fel` (`userFacingStorageError`, fallbacks *Kunde inte spara räkenskapsåret.* / *Kunde inte skapa nästa räkenskapsår.*). Both actions are plain `withBusiness` (owner surface; no consultant twin). Saving writes only the `fiscal_years` row + audit - no reminder, attention row or wizard comes from this card; the year already exists from company create (see [Onboarding](#onboarding)). After a save the bookkeeping views follow at once (Huvudbok subtitle *… 2026-01-01 till 2027-06-30 …*, export `ar=2026%2F2027`), and after **Skapa nästa år** the `FiscalYearPicker` chips appear on Huvudbok / Rapporter / Moms / Bokslut (see [Bokföring](#bokföring)).
- **Fakturering:** payment details (bankgiro/plusgiro/bank/IBAN), invoice defaults (`#installningar-paymentTermsDays`, `-lateInterestRate`, `-quoteValidityDays`, `-defaultQuoteTerms`, `-defaultHourlyRate`, `-defaultVatRate`), **billing readiness**, the connected bank card (*Hantera bankkoppling →* → `/bokforing/bank` since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), `connectedBankSummary`), the **Preliminärskatt (F-skatt) per månad** card (`FSkattSettingCard`, same component as in the Skatt / Skattekonto panel: amount + **Ändra** → **Belopp per månad** → `setFSkattPerMonthAction`, plus the opt-out checkbox **Bokför inte F-skatt automatiskt** since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) - note `settings-form.tsx` renders the card with `amount` only, so here the checkbox always starts unchecked even when the opt-out is stored (the Skatt / Skattekonto panel passes `autoBook`); see [Skattekonto](#skattekonto-bokforingskattekonto)) and the **Artikelregister** (below). *Komplettera för fakturering* is a plain form (moms + bankgiro always visible; address via `AddressFields` only if missing at open). Draft local state; persist only on **Spara** (`saveBillingCompletionAction`). **Stäng** discards the draft. **Använd förslaget** fills moms only. Modal visibility is user-controlled (`completeOpen`), not `readiness.ready` / send-blockers.
- **Artikelregister** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), commit `b0d1063`; `ArticleSettings` in `src/components/article-settings.tsx`, service `listArticles` / `upsertArticle` / `deleteArticle` / `articleToDocLine` / `articleFromLine` in `src/lib/services/articles.ts`, actions `listArticlesAction` / `upsertArticleAction` / `deleteArticleAction` / `articleFromLineAction`, tests `services/articles.test.ts`): card **Artikelregister** - *Samma arbete och samma pris, varje gång. Raderna dyker upp som förslag när du skriver offerter och fakturor.* - list rows *{beskrivning} · {Arbete|Material|Resor|Övrigt} · {kr}/{enhet}* with a trash button `aria-label="Ta bort {beskrivning}"`; empty *Inga artiklar ännu.*; **Ny artikel** opens one inline row: input placeholder **Beskrivning**, `<select>` Arbete / Material / Resor / Övrigt (unit auto-switches `tim` ↔ `st`), input **Enhet**, input **kr**, button **Spara** (saves immediately, VAT is fixed at **25 %**, there is no article number, no max count). Stored as `CatalogArticle[]` in `DB.meta.articles` = `businesses.meta` jsonb - **no table, no migration** (the task brief's “migration 47” does not cover it). Consumed by the line editor's **Från register** picker (see [Fakturor](#fakturor) → Line editor). No ids / `data-*` in the card. Demo: empty.
- **Notiser** (`owner-notice-settings.tsx`, saves immediately – no *Spara ändringar*): owner e-mail for events that happen **outside** the app. Recipient card: `#notiser-email` (empty = company e-mail from Företag; the value is stored only when it differs) + **Skicka testmejl** (`sendOwnerNoticeTestAction`; disabled in demo / without a valid address; honest error when mail is not configured). Händelser card: one `role="switch"` row per `OWNER_NOTICE_KINDS` (`[data-notice=<kind>]`): `offert_godkand` (customer accepted – `prepareQuoteAcceptedNotices` owner mail), `offert_avbojd` (customer declined – `prepareQuoteDeclinedNotice`, sent with `after()` from `declineQuoteByTokenAction`), `forfragan` (website lead – `deliverWebsiteJobNotification`; Hemsida → Webbformulär override still wins; when off the job gets `notification.status = "off"` and no mail), `inkorg` (mail-ingested faktura/kvitto: *är bokförd/bokfört* · *behöver kontrolleras* → `/inbox/[id]/kontrollera` · *Nytt dokument i inkorgen*; never for own uploads), `orderbekraftelse` (*stämmer* / *avviker* → `/uppdrag/[jobId]/bestallning/[orderId]`, *kunde inte kopplas* → `/inbox/[id]`). Both inbound webhooks (`/api/inbox/inbound`, `/api/inbox/inbound/resend`) build the notice inside the tenant run (`prepareInboxArrivalNotice`) and send it with `after()` (`sendOwnerNotices`). All kinds default **on**; the demo business never sends; `email_events.kind = notis:<kind>`. Rules: `src/lib/notices/owner-notices.ts`; service `src/lib/services/owner-notices.ts`; templates `src/lib/email/owner-notice-templates.ts`; storage `business_settings.notices` (jsonb, migration `43_owner_notices`); tests `owner-notices.test.ts`.
- **Funktioner:** Hemsida + Samarbeta + **Grossistbeställningar** toggles (`feature-settings.tsx`, one row per `OPTIONAL_FEATURE_IDS`) — Aktiv/Avstängd, Aktivera/Stäng av (confirm modal *Stänga av Grossistbeställningar?*). Hooks: `data-feature=<id>` + `data-feature-action=activate|deactivate|confirm-deactivate`, `aria-label` *Aktivera Grossistbeställningar* / *Stäng av Grossistbeställningar*. Activating Grossistbeställningar lands on `?flik=grossister`; in demo contexts (`isWholesalerDemoContext` = demo business or JSON store) it also seeds *Demo-grossisten* + a price list (see [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders)). The Kom igång task *articles_prices* activates the same way.
- **Grossister** (only while on): `wholesaler-settings.tsx` — **Lägg till grossist** → modal **Spara grossist**; one card per connection (`[data-wholesaler-connection=<id>]`) with **Ändra** / **Aktivera**·**Inaktivera** and the price-list card (**Ladda upp prisfil** / **Ersätt prisfilen**, `[data-price-file-input]`). Details in [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders).
- **Konto:** the **Abonnemang** card first (`abonnemang-card.tsx`, `data-abonnemang="<reason>"`): badge (Provperiod / Aktivt / Uppsagt / Betalning väntar / Skrivskyddat / Demo), one sentence from `billingAccess()` (`src/lib/billing/state.ts`; trial: *N dagar kvar av provperioden (till {datum})*), plan copy *Ferva kostar 199 kr per månad exklusive moms …*, then either *Abonnemangsbetalning är inte konfigurerad …* (`data-abonnemang-unconfigured`, no Stripe env), *Bara företagets ägare eller administratör …* (consultant), or the buttons **Fortsätt med Ferva** / **Teckna abonnemang** (`data-abonnemang-checkout` → `startCheckoutAction` → `redirect()` to Stripe Checkout) and **Hantera abonnemang** / **Uppdatera kort i kundportalen** (`data-abonnemang-portal` → `openCustomerPortalAction` → Customer Portal). Return URLs `/installningar?flik=konto&checkout=klart|avbrutet` render a `role="status"` line (*Tack! … aktiveras här så snart Stripe bekräftat det*); nothing is activated by the return itself - the webhook `POST /api/stripe/webhook` is the source of truth (see `docs/admin.md` → Abonnemang). Read-only / past-due businesses get the app-wide `SubscriptionBanner` (`data-subscription-banner`, *Skrivskyddat* / *Betalning väntar*, link **Teckna abonnemang** / **Hantera betalning**); every `withBusiness` write then fails with *Provperioden tog slut … Gå till Inställningar → Konto för att fortsätta.* Below it: real user email + logout via menu. Demo: *Ferva körs just nu utan inloggning…* no password.
- **Billing readiness testids:** `billing-readiness-banner` | `billing-readiness-ready` | `billing-readiness-success` | `billing-complete-modal` | `billing-complete-suggest-vat` | `billing-complete-{name|orgnr|vat|address|payment}`. Copy: *Redo att fakturera*. Persist only on Spara.
- **Related:** `/foretag` is a parent crumb to settings (legacy). Onboarding fields overlap.
- **Components:** `settings-form.tsx`, `fiscal-year-settings.tsx` (Företag → Räkenskapsår), `settings-billing-readiness.tsx`, `skattekonto-widgets.tsx` (`FSkattSettingCard` on Fakturering), `article-settings.tsx` (Artikelregister), `owner-notice-settings.tsx` (Notiser), `feature-settings.tsx`, `wholesaler-settings.tsx` (Grossister), `demo-reset-section.tsx`.
- **DB:** `business_settings` (incl. `notices` jsonb, `claims` jsonb, `f_skatt_per_month`), `fiscal_years` (Räkenskapsår), `businesses.meta` (`features`, `articles`), `business_onboarding` (Kom igång), `wholesaler_connections` / `wholesaler_price_imports` (Grossister).
- **Live:** *Redo att fakturera* (demo company complete). Demo reset section at bottom of Företag. Räkenskapsår card (2026-09-08): *2026: 1 januari 2026–31 december 2026*, **Spara räkenskapsår** + **Skapa nästa år**, no year list (one year).
- **Verify:** `/installningar?flik=fakturering` + testids. Script: `scripts/verify-billing-readiness-browser.ts`. Tests: `billing-readiness.test.ts`, `settings-*.test.ts`.

---

## Grossistbeställningar (wholesale orders)

- **User-facing name:** Grossistbeställningar (feature) · Grossister (settings tab) · Materialbeställningar (job section).
- **Purpose:** Electricians/plumbers connect their wholesalers (`WHOLESALER_KEYS`: Ahlsell, Dahl, Sonepar, Solar, Lundagrossisten, Rexel, *Annan grossist*), upload price lists / discount letters, search articles with **their own purchase prices**, build a cart per job, e-mail the order to the wholesaler and get the order confirmation matched back in the Ferva inbox. Optional feature — **invisible when off**: no nav item, the job material UI is unchanged.
- **Feature flag:** `wholesalers` in `src/lib/optional-features.ts` / `features.ts` (`wholesalersEnabled(db)`, `hasWholesalerUsage` backfills for existing data). Copy: *Sök material med dina priser och skicka beställningar till grossisten.* Deactivation never deletes connections, price files, articles or order history.
- **Routes:** settings `/installningar?flik=grossister` (redirects to `?flik=funktioner` while the feature is off); order detail `/uppdrag/[id]/bestallning/[orderId]` (crumbs *Uppdrag / {job} / Beställning FV-{n}*, nav label *Materialbeställning*, back **Uppdraget**; 404 unless the order belongs to that job); price-file upload `POST /api/grossist/prisfil` (multipart, max 8 MB, same-origin check, `manage_wholesalers`, `mode=preview|import`, CSV/TXT/XLSX/XML/ZIP only; a route handler because the files exceed what a server action body takes as base64; **not** a public prefix). Inbox items of type `orderbekraftelse` render `inbox-order-confirmation.tsx` (no invoice fields). No page of its own and no nav item.
- **Settings (Grossister):** `wholesaler-settings.tsx`. **Lägg till grossist** → modal (**Spara grossist**; field ids end in `-grossist`, `-kundnummer`, `-ordermejl`). Per connection: wholesaler, display name, customer number, order e-mail (user-entered — Ferva never guesses), CC self, default pickup/delivery + store/address, contact, phone, customer price rule (*Ange kundpris senare* | *Beräkna med påslag på inköpspriset* | *Använd utpris från prisfilen*), **Ändra**, **Aktivera** / **Inaktivera** (inactive connections drop out of the job search but keep their data). Price list card: *Dina priser uppdaterades {datum}* · *{n} artiklar importerades* · *Priserna kan vara gamla* / *Prisfilen kan behöva uppdateras* (> `PRICE_LIST_STALE_DAYS` = 90) · **Be om ny prislista** (mailto draft; sending does not mark the list updated) · *Vi hittade rabatter men saknar artikelregistret…* · last failed import with reason · **Ersätt prisfilen** / **Ladda upp prisfil** opens the OS file dialog immediately (the button is also a drop target; `[data-price-file-input]` lives on the card). No empty *Släpp prisfilen här* modal. After a file exists, modal *Prisfil för {grossist}* shows mapping/preview + *Den nuvarande prislistan påverkas inte förrän du bekräftar.* with **Stäng**, **Välj annan fil** (picker again) and **Importera prislistan**.
- **Import engine (`src/lib/wholesalers/`):** `file-detect.ts` (CSV/TXT/TSV/XLSX/XML/ZIP, BOM/UTF-8/Windows-1252), `csv.ts` (delimiter sniffing, Swedish decimals), `xlsx.ts` + `zip.ts` (own minimal readers; zip-bomb/ratio/entry limits, no nested archives, path traversal rejected), `xml.ts`/`xml-table.ts` (no DTD/entities), `table.ts` (formula-injection neutralised, row/column limits), `column-mapping.ts` (Swedish/English header synonyms + content heuristics; mapping remembered per connection), `import-engine.ts` (preview → validate → build). **Price rules:** explicit net price wins; otherwise list price × (1 − discount group %) from a discount letter stored on the connection; sales price from file only if that rule is chosen; else markup; never invents a price. Money = integer öre (`money.ts`); customer prices are whole kronor. **Atomic:** import row `processing` → products written under new `import_id` → activate + supersede old in a separate commit; failure leaves the previous list active (`status=failed`, `failed_reason`, row-level `errors[]` with line numbers).
- **Catalog storage:** `wholesaler_products` live **outside** the tenant aggregate: Supabase → SQL (`catalog-store-sql.ts`, indexes on article/E/RSK/GTIN keys + trigram on `search_text`); JSON/demo sessions → file/memory store (`catalog-store.ts`, `.data/wholesaler-catalog/`). Selection via `catalog.ts` from the **server-side tenant context only**.
- **Known wholesaler formats (Ahlsell first; migration `48_wholesaler_agreements`, `wholesaler-file-formats.test.ts`):** `parsePriceFile` runs a **format detection** (`src/lib/wholesalers/formats/registry.ts`, content only — never the filename) *before* the column mapping and lands in one of three outcomes: **known** (a registered `WholesalerFileParser` recognised the file → parsed with hard-coded field positions, no mapping dialog), **delimited** (today's column mapping), **fixed_width** (honest `PriceFileError` *Filen har fast kolumnbredd (rader på N tecken) men formatet är inte känt*, no mapping dialog); a known format that is too short (< 50 rows verified) is also an honest error. Parsers: `ahlsell-avtalsfil` (rabattbrev; 79-char header — TYP, KUNDNUMMER, ANLÄGGNINGSNUMMER, BENÄMNING, KEDJERABATT, KÖRNINGSDATUM — and 93-char rows with KEDJERABATTKOD, ARTIKELNUMMER *or* MATERIALKLASS, RABATT/SPECRABATT in tenths of a percent, NETTOPRIS in öre, SLUTDATUM YYMMDD; rows with both or neither of article/class, wrong length or letters in numeric fields are rejected with a row number) and `ahlsell-prisfil` (bruttoprislista, 112-char rows: Artikelnummer, Grundnettopris in öre — 0 = *pris på begäran*, Materialklass, Lagerenhet, Lagerförd, Benämning, Beställningsmultipel with two implied decimals, three J/N flags). Text is decoded strictly as UTF-8 or else **ISO-8859-1** (`formats/fixed-width.ts`, `Buffer.toString("latin1")` — not the WHATWG windows-1252 alias), CRLF/CR/LF normalised, never trimmed before slicing. **Storage:** the agreement header lives on `wholesaler_connections.discount_agreement` (jsonb: type, customer number, facility, name, chain-discount code, run/end dates, counts per row type, coverage snapshot), the terms in `wholesaler_agreement_terms` (kind `class|article`, `material_class`, `article_number`/`article_key`, `discount_tenths`, `net_price_ore`, `chain_discount_tenths`, `end_date`; RLS + same-business trigger; file store twin for JSON/demo); the price list reuses `wholesaler_products` (materialklass → `discount_group`, `stocked`, import `format`). Agreement and price list are stored separately and **joined at read time** in `agreement-pricing.ts` (`withAgreementPrices` in `services/wholesalers.ts` is the only place: article net price → article spec discount → material-class discount, exact class then longest prefix `CÄÄ011 → CÄÄ01` → list price; an explicit net price from a customer-specific price list is never discounted again). Every computed price carries a `priceExplanation` (list price, rule, class, agreement, end date) shown as **Varför?** on the product card (`[data-price-explanation]`); öre are kept until the quote/invoice line (README ADR-1 addendum). `KEDJERABATTKOD` is parsed and stored, rows with `J` are flagged, no chain-discount math. **UI:** for a known format the modal *Rabattavtal för {grossist}* / *Prislista för {grossist}* shows a **summary** instead of the mapping (`[data-known-format=<parserId>]`: Filtyp, Grossist, Kundnummer (+ anläggning), Avtal, Avtalstyp, Kedjerabatt, Körningsdatum, Gäller t.o.m., Teckenkodning, and `[data-known-format-counts]` — *Rabatt per materialklass*, *Artikelvillkor*, *– varav specrabatt*, *– varav nettopris*; price list: *Artiklar*, *– varav pris på begäran*, *– varav lagerförda*); expired dates, a customer number that differs from the connection and chain-discount rows are warnings, never blockers; an agreement uploaded without an active price list gets `[data-price-list-missing]` with the link **Ahlsell – prislistor och avtalsfil** (`AHLSELL_PRICE_FILE_HELP_URL`); confirm is **Läs in rabattavtalet** / **Importera prislistan**. The connection card shows `[data-wholesaler-agreement=<id>]` (*Rabattavtal {name}*, type · customer number, counts, *läst in {datum}*, *Gäller t.o.m. …* + *har gått ut* when expired) and `[data-agreement-coverage]` (*{n} av {m} artiklar i prislistan saknar rabatt i avtalet* — recomputed on every import of either file); with an agreement but no price list the price-list box reads *Rabattavtalet är inläst men prislistan saknas …* (`[data-agreement-without-price-list]`). The import center (`data-imports.ts`) refuses known wholesaler formats with a pointer to Inställningar → Grossister. Fixture: `src/lib/__fixtures__/wholesalers/ahlsell-avtalsfil.txt` (redacted excerpt, customer number `9999999`, ISO-8859-1 + CRLF); the full real file is asserted only when `AHLSELL_AVTALSFIL` (or `~/Downloads/Avtal_9999999%20(1).txt`) exists.
- **Job UI:** `job-work.tsx` → **Lägg till** (`[data-job-add-entry]`) opens `AddEntrySheet` (*Lägg till på uppdraget*). **Tid** and manual **Material** share `LinesEditor` `variant="single"`. Choose the **Material** segment (`[data-job-add-material]`) - that always opens `AddMaterialSheet` (*Lägg till material*; `[data-add-material-choice]`, value = Swedish label). **Sök och beställ** (only when the feature is on and ≥1 active connection) opens `wholesaler-material-sheet.tsx` (mobile bottom sheet / desktop modal): big search field `aria-label="Sök artikel, E-nummer, RSK-nummer eller EAN"` (the label gained *eller EAN* in [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)), wholesaler switcher (`aria-label="Välj grossist"`), results (`[data-wholesaler-result]`, `[data-in-cart]` on lines already in the cart: name · art.nr/E/RSK · brand · unit/pack · own price · customer price / *Kundpris saknas*), qty + **Lägg i varukorg**, sticky footer **Visa varukorg (n)**, discreet **Lägg till manuellt** (returns to the Material segment of `AddEntrySheet`). Cart: qty steppers (44 px), remove, customer price per line, free-text line, line comments, delivery (pickup/delivery, store/address, requested date), orderer name/e-mail/phone, CC self, message; totals for expected purchase cost and customer price (only when complete). One cart per (job, connection); several wholesalers → separate carts and separate orders. Cart lines are planned purchases — **not** `JobWorkEntry`.
- **Butik i Mowin-stil** ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), commit `7959f81`, migration `42_wholesaler_shop`; `wholesaler-shop.test.ts`): before you type, the sheet shows shelves **Beställt tidigare** (articles on earlier orders), **Favoriter** and **Kategorier**; filter chips (`aria-label="Filtrera artiklar"`) **Alla** · **Favoriter** · **Beställt tidigare** · one chip per catalog category (`category_key`, *N fler kategorier* / *Färre kategorier*). **Favorites**: heart button `aria-label="Spara {namn} som favorit"` / `"Ta bort {namn} från favoriter"` (`aria-pressed`), stored per connection keyed on **article number** (`wholesaler_connections.favorite_articles` jsonb ↔ `favoriteArticleNumbers`, max 200, `toggleWholesalerFavoriteAction`, `wholesalerShopContextAction`) - the article id changes with every import, the number does not. **Barcode**: button `aria-label="Skanna streckkod"` (`title="Skanna streckkod (EAN)"`, sheet title *Skanna streckkod*, help *Rikta kameran mot streckkoden på förpackningen. Artikeln söks fram så fort koden läses.*, video `aria-label="Kamerabild"`) uses the browser `BarcodeDetector` (ean_13 / ean_8 / upc_a / upc_e / code_128 / code_39 / itf) + `getUserMedia` rear camera and searches the GTIN - hidden where the API does not exist (desktop Chrome on Linux, Firefox, Safari). **Images**: `image_url` comes only from a price-file column mapped as **Bildlänk** (`sanitizeImageUrl`, absolute http(s) ≤ 500 chars); otherwise a category/name icon (`productIconKey`) - no image assets, and the demo price list has no image column. **Brand**: column **Varumärke** (synonyms varumärke / fabrikat / tillverkare / brand / manufacturer …) → `wholesaler_products.brand`. **Margin**: badge `{percent} %` (*Förlust {percent} %* when negative, `title="Marginal {kr} per {enhet} (kundpris − inköpspris)"`, `grossMargin()` in `wholesalers/pricing.ts`) next to *Inköp …* / *Kund …* - computed, never stored. No `data-*` on the heart, chips, scan button or margin badge (aria only). Nothing changed in the cart → review → **Skicka beställning** flow.
- **Send:** review view shows To / CC / Reply-To (company Ferva inbox) / subject / text + attachments (`bestallning-{ref}.pdf`, `.csv`) and blockers; explicit **Skicka beställning**. From = Ferva verified sender with display name *{Företag} via Ferva* (`mailFromWithDisplayName`), never the user's domain. Subject *Beställning FV-1001 – {Företag} – kundnr {n}* (reference `FV-{n}` from `meta.purchaseOrderSequence`, first order = FV-1001). Idempotent via `sendKey` + per-order lock + rate limit; provider error keeps status *Utkast* (*Beställningen kunde inte skickas. Inget har gått iväg till grossisten …*); demo business / mock provider → `sentSnapshot.transport = "simulated"` (honest copy *Inget mejl lämnade Ferva*); a **real** business without a mail provider gets *E-posttjänsten är inte konfigurerad, så beställningen har inte skickats …* and stays *Utkast* — never a fake “sent”. After send: **Skickad – inväntar bekräftelse**, immutable `sent_snapshot` (DB triggers `purchase_orders_guard` / `purchase_order_lines_guard`).
- **Statuses (`PURCHASE_ORDER_STATUS`):** draft *Utkast* · sent *Skickad – inväntar bekräftelse* · confirmed *Bekräftad* · partially_confirmed *Delvis bekräftad* · needs_review *Avvikelse kräver kontroll* · rejected *Avvisad* · cancelled *Avbruten*. Channel enum `email | edi | api | punchout` (only e-mail implemented).
- **Confirmations via inbox:** `ingestInboundMail` classifies `orderbekraftelse` (`looksLikeOrderConfirmation`, never when invoice hints exist; only when the business has sent orders). Matching (`matchOrderForMail`): 1) Ferva reference in subject/text/attachment, 2) known wholesaler order number, 3) customer number + job title + sender domain, else **candidates only** (`purchaseOrderCandidateIds`) — user picks *Det är den här*. Deterministic parsing first (`confirmation-parse.ts`: CSV/XML attachment → HTML table → text lines); AI fallback (`confirmation-ai.ts`, `enrichConfirmationWithAi` after the webhook ingest) only yields candidates with confidence < AUTO and never sends/books. Reconciliation (`reconcileConfirmation`): qty / price / backorder / substitute / missing / added / delivery_date / total. Exact article match with matching qty is auto-applied; qty/price/substitute/missing/added/total → `needs_review` with **Godkänn ändringarna**. Backorders and delivery dates are shown, not blocking. Idempotent per `inbox_item_id` and external message id; partial confirmations accumulate.
- **Material to job:** `syncJobWorkEntriesForOrder` creates/updates **one** `JobWorkEntry` per confirmed order line (`source: "wholesaler"`, `wholesaler` provenance: connection, order, line, confirmation, article, `unitCostOre`) only when a customer price exists (explicit > file > markup); *Kundpris saknas* lines stay confirmed but are **not** invoiceable at 0 kr (**Ange kundpris** on the order page). Invoiced entries are never changed automatically — deviations are listed (`lockedInvoicedLineIds`).
- **Permissions:** `manage_wholesalers` (owner/admin: connections + price files), `order_materials` (members too); consultants get neither. All reads/writes go through `withBusiness`; `business_id` never comes from the client.
- **Demo:** activating the feature in a demo context (`isWholesalerDemoContext` = `isDemoBusiness() || isJsonDemoStore()`) seeds *Demo-grossisten* (`DEMO_WHOLESALER_NAME`; wholesaler *other*, kundnr `DEMO-4711`, order e-mail `order@demo-grossisten.example`, pickup at *Demo-grossisten Årsta*, markup 25 %) + a 40-article el/VVS CSV price list through the real import engine (`wholesalers/demo.ts`), once per session (`meta.wholesalerDemoSeeded`). Sending simulates (no external mail) and immediately ingests a deterministic demo confirmation (`demoConfirmationPayload`: first line +5 % price, last line backordered 1 unit, so the reconciliation has a deviation and a backorder to show). Demo reset clears the tables (`app.reset_demo_business`) and the catalog file (`deleteCatalogFileFor`).
- **Attention:** no Hem/Bokföring rows for orders themselves; only an **unmatched** confirmation surfaces (*Välj beställning för orderbekräftelsen från …*, see [Underlag](#underlag-the-former-inbox)). Deviations live on the order page (**Godkänn ändringarna**) and as bullets in the job's *Materialbeställningar* list.
- **DB (migration `38_wholesalers`, shop columns in `42_wholesaler_shop`):** `wholesaler_connections` (+ `favorite_articles` jsonb), `wholesaler_price_imports`, `wholesaler_products` (+ `brand`, `image_url`, `category_key` with the partial index `wholesaler_products_import_category_idx`; `gtin` existed before), `purchase_orders`, `purchase_order_lines`, `purchase_order_confirmations` (+ `job_work_entries.wholesaler_provenance`, `source='wholesaler'`; `inbox_items.purchase_order_id/…_confirmation_id/…_candidates`, `document_type='orderbekraftelse'`). RLS on all; same-business triggers; unique `(business_id, reference)`. `ensureWholesalerSchema` in `apply-pending-schema.ts` applies the same DDL (incl. the shop columns) where the migrations are not pushed yet; migration 42 itself is a no-op when the 38 tables are absent (`to_regclass` guard, commit `2e51eae`).
- **Selectors (no `data-testid`; all `data-*`):** job `[data-job-add-entry]` (**Lägg till** on **Arbete och material**), `[data-job-add-material]` (the **Material** segment inside `AddEntrySheet`, also with the feature off), `[data-job-line-editor]` (Tid / Material-manuellt, same DocLine fields), `[data-add-material-choice]` (chooser; value = Swedish label), `[data-invoice-readiness]`, `[data-purchase-orders-section]`; sheet `[data-wholesaler-search]`, `[data-wholesaler-results]` → `[data-wholesaler-result]`, `[data-wholesaler-add]`, `[data-wholesaler-cart-button]`, `[data-wholesaler-cart-lines]` → `[data-wholesaler-cart-line]`, `[data-wholesaler-review-button]`, `[data-wholesaler-mail-preview]`, `[data-wholesaler-send]`, `[data-wholesaler-manual]`, `[data-comparable-unit-price]` (pack unit price when `packSize > 1`); order page `[data-purchase-order-lines]` → `[data-purchase-order-line]`, `[data-purchase-order-confirmation=<id>]`, `[data-approve-confirmation]` (**Godkänn ändringarna**), `[data-set-customer-price]` (the **Spara** of the kundpris field); inbox `[data-inbox-order-candidates]`, `[data-link-order=<orderId>]` (**Det är den här**), `[data-inbox-linked-order]`; Underlag Kontrollera `[data-document-line-review]`, `[data-split-jobs]`; settings `[data-wholesaler-connection=<id>]`, `[data-price-file-input]`; feature toggle `[data-feature="wholesalers"][data-feature-action="activate"|"deactivate"|"confirm-deactivate"]`. Aria: search input `aria-label="Sök artikel, E-nummer, RSK-nummer eller EAN"`, `"Välj grossist"`, `"Filtrera artiklar"` (chips), `"Spara {artikel} som favorit"` / `"Ta bort {artikel} från favoriter"`, `"Skanna streckkod"`, results `aria-label="Sökresultat"`, `"Lägg {artikel} i varukorgen"`, `"Minska antal"` / `"Öka antal"`, `"Grossistens ordernummer"`, `"Kundpris kr per {enhet}"`, job list rows `aria-label="Beställning FV-{n} till {grossist}"`, settings `aria-label="Ändra {grossist}"`.
- **Live (driva-alpha, 2026-09-06 after [PR #119](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/119)):** with the demo cookie, `/installningar?flik=funktioner` lists **Grossistbeställningar** (`[data-feature="wholesalers"]`, *Avstängd* for the seed company) next to Hemsida + Samarbeta, and `?flik=grossister` redirects to Funktioner until you activate. **Aktivera** lands on `?flik=grossister` with the new **Grossister** tab, **Lägg till grossist** and the seeded *Demo-grossisten* card (*Aktiv*, *Kundnummer DEMO-4711 · Order till order@demo-grossisten.example*, *Hämtning · Demo-grossisten Årsta · Inköpspris + 25 % påslag*, *Dina priser uppdaterades 6 september 2026*, *40 artiklar importerades · demo-prislista.csv*); afterwards `?flik=grossister` renders instead of redirecting, and `/uppdrag/job-kok` → **Lägg till** (`[data-job-add-entry]`) → **Material** (`[data-job-add-material]`) opened the wholesaler sheet (after [PR #151](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/151) the hop is **Sök och beställ** `[data-add-material-choice="Sök och beställ"]`) (*Sök artikel, E-nummer eller RSK-nummer* - since #130 *… eller EAN*, plus the Favoriter / Kategorier shelves, *Grossist: Demo-grossisten*) where `kabel` gives *5 träffar* (e.g. *Kabel EQLQ 5G2,5 halogenfri*, Art.nr DG-100110, 23,78 kr/m, Kundpris 30 kr). Cart → review → simulated send → confirmation were **not** clicked through on driva-alpha for this map - browser proof is `scripts/verify-wholesalers-browser.ts` against `npm run dev` on :3123 (`VERIFY_URL`, `CHROME`, `VERIFY_JOB_ID` default `job-kok`), which walks off → activate → Grossister seeded → second wholesaler + CSV upload → search → cart → review → simulated send → job section → order page → inbox (the script still navigates to `/inbox`, which now redirects to `/bokforing/underlag`; the orderbekräftelse row needs `ekonomiska=alla` since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) - not re-run for this map) → mobile 44 px / keyboard → deactivate keeps history → `?flik=grossister` redirect.
- **Verify:** `src/lib/wholesalers.test.ts` (feature gating, CSV/TXT/XLSX/XML/ZIP import, discount groups, net-price precedence, failed import keeps catalog, server-side search, carts, sending, idempotency, demo sink, confirmations, deviations, partial/idempotent, no duplicate work entries, missing customer price, cross-tenant); `scripts/db-validate.ts` (RLS, same-business, immutability, pending schema); `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` (SQL catalog + full flow through the adapter); browser: `scripts/verify-wholesalers-browser.ts`. Quick recipe in [Reproduction recipes](#reproduction-recipes-quick).

## Samarbeta / redovisningskonsult

- **User-facing name:** Samarbeta (owner). **Redovisning** (consultant workspace).
- **Purpose:** Invite an accountant/auditor into the company. Separate chrome — not the owner sidebar.
- **Owner routes:** `/samarbeta` — **gated**. Live demo redirects to Funktioner until activated.
- **How to get there (owner):** Settings → Funktioner → **Aktivera Samarbeta** → nav appears. Or command *Bjud in redovisningskonsult*. Demo menu **Visa redovisningsvyn** jumps to accountant UI without enabling the nav item.
- **Owner UI:** *Låt din redovisningskonsult hjälpa till direkt i Ferva.* CTA **Bjud in redovisningskonsult**. `#invite-form`, `#invite-email`. Statuses: Inbjudan skickad, Ansluten, Åtkomst borttagen, Inbjudan har gått ut.
- **Invite accept:** `/inbjudan/[token]` → Acceptera → `/redovisning`. Roles shown: Redovisningskonsult, Revisor, Ägare, Administratör, Medlem.
- **Consultant routes:**
  | Route | Label |
  |-------|-------|
  | `/redovisning` | portfolio |
  | `/redovisning/k/[businessId]` | Arbeta (queue + filters; footer links *Periodstängning och periodlås*, *Ingående balans och övertagande från annat program*) |
  | `.../verifikationer`, `/bank`, `/moms`, `/rapporter`, `/bokslut` | client tabs (`CLIENT_TABS` in `accountant-workspace.tsx` - unchanged, no Skattekonto/Lön tab) |
  | `.../verifikationer/nytt` | Nytt verifikat (header button on Verifikationer when `write_accounting`) |
  | `.../bokslut/bilagor`, `.../bokslut/avstamning` | Bokslutsbilagor, Avstämning |
  | `.../bokslut/arsredovisning/[fiscalYearId]` (+ `/pdf`) | Årsredovisning (+ A4) |
  | `.../bokslut/ink2/[fiscalYearId]` | INK2 |
  | `.../periodstangning`, `.../ingaende-balans` | Periodstängning, Ingående balans (tab **Arbeta** stays active) |
  | `/redovisning/installningar` | logout |
- `/redovisning/klienter` and `/att-gora` redirect into portfolio/client.
- **Shared views** ([PR #109](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/109)): the bokslut/bilagor/avstämning/årsredovisning/INK2/periodstängning/ingående-balans/manuellt-verifikat pages render the **same components** as the owner surface (`BokslutView`, `BokslutsbilagorView`, `AvstamningView`, `ArsredovisningView`, `Ink2View`, `PeriodstangningView`, `IngaendeBalansView`, `ManualVerificationForm`, `MomsPeriods`) with three extra props: `businessId` (sent to every server action so the client - not the cookie - decides whose books are written; `withBusiness` re-checks membership), `readOnly` (`!can(role, capability)` - auditor), and `hrefFor` / `base` (`accountantHref` in `src/lib/collaboration/hrefs.ts` translates owner hrefs like `/bokforing/moms` → `/redovisning/k/<id>/moms`; bokslut sub-paths keep their suffix; anything without a twin - lön, ekonomi, inställningar - lands on the client's Arbeta view; the consultant never gets a link into the owner layout). Page data is read in the client's tenant context (`loadStateSnapshot` + `runInTenantContext`).
- **Not on the consultant surface:** Skattekonto, Lön (no `/redovisning/k/[id]/lon`), the momsperiodicitet picker on Moms. Queue kinds `PERIOD_CLOSE` (*Periodstängning*) and `YEAR_END_REVIEW` (*Bokslut*) come from action ids `period-close-*` / `year-end-*` and fall under the **Granskning** filter.
- **Filters:** Alla, Brådskande, Moms, Bank, Underlag, Granskning, Väntar. Empty: *✓ Allt klart — Inget behöver din hjälp just nu.*
- **Permissions** (`src/lib/collaboration/permissions.ts`): `accounting_consultant` = read/write accounting, categorize, match payments, correct vouchers, `vat`, `reconcile`, `period_close`, `year_end`, export, prepare supplier payments, **`prepare_filing`** (generate the deklaration file) and client information requests - but **not** `submit_filing` (sign + lämna in), `submit_bank_payment`, send quotes/invoices, website, invites, `manage_customers`, `reveal_personnummer`. `auditor` = `read_accounting` + `export_accounting` only (every shared view `readOnly`, *Revisorer har endast läsbehörighet.*). Owner/admin have everything; `member` = owner minus invite/revoke. Read routes never deny accounting roles (see [Auth gates](#auth-gates)).
- **Related:** owner Inställningar Funktioner; demo accountant Anna Svensson.
- **Components:** `samarbeta-view.tsx`, `redovisning-shell.tsx`, `accountant-workspace.tsx`, `accountant-verifikationer.tsx` + the shared Bokföring views listed above.
- **DB:** `business_memberships` (extended roles), `collaboration_invitations`, `client_information_requests`.
- **Verify:** activate feature → `/samarbeta` shows invite form. Demo: company menu → *Visa redovisningsvyn* → `/redovisning` → open the client → **Bokslut** tab shows the same checklist as `/bokforing/bokslut` with links **Bokslutsbilagor** / **Avstämning per balanskonto**; Verifikationer has **Nytt verifikat** (consultant) - none of these consultant routes were exercised on the live deploy for this map (code-verified only). Tests: `collaboration.test.ts` (capability matrix incl. `prepare_filing` vs `submit_filing`, read vs write gate).

## Supportmatris & eligibility (spec §10)

One versioned source of truth for what Ferva supports: `src/lib/support/matrix.ts` (`SUPPORT_MATRIX_VERSION`, `SUPPORT_MATRIX` — every entry has `id`, `area`, `label`, `level` supported|consultant|unsupported, `summary`, `enforcement`, primary `source`, `effectiveFrom`, `owner`, `tests`). It is not copy: onboarding, help, the consultant view and server validation all read it.

- **Levels.** *supported* = expert-verified rules in the engine (AB, K2, Sweden/SEK, 0/6/12/25 % VAT invoicing, ROT/RUT, fixed monthly payroll, bookkeeping/moms/AGI/INK2, K2 year-end schedules, manual filing). *consultant* = allowed only after an accounting consultant with access approves it for the business (**enskild firma**, **omvänd byggmoms on outgoing invoices**). *unsupported* = blocked server-side (other company forms, koncern, K3, foreign currency, EU/export/import, VMB, inventory/manufacturing, complex equity events, complex payroll, year-end posts without a schedule, e-invoice/Peppol, API filing).
- **Eligibility** (`src/lib/support/eligibility.ts`, pure/client-safe): `SCOPE_QUESTIONS`, `assessEligibility({companyForm, flags})` → `{verdict, blocking, consultant, matrixVersion}`; `entryStatusFor(settings, id)` → supported|approved|consultant|unsupported; `businessEligibility`, `pendingConsultantEntries`, `normalizeBusinessScope`, `newBusinessScope`.
- **Server guards** (`src/lib/support/guard.ts`): `assertCompanyFormSupported` (onboarding + `updateBusinessProfile`), `assertEligibleToCreate` (`createCompanyAction`), `assertScopeAllowed(entryId)` (tenant context), `ScopeBlockedError`. Invoice issuing: `collectScopeBlockers` in `src/lib/invoices/validate.ts` adds blocker `scope_reverse_charge` (href `/samarbeta`) for any reverse-charge invoice until the entry is approved — `issueInvoice` throws `InvoiceNotReadyError` (*… är ett konsultfall i Ferva …*). Already-existing enforcement is referenced per entry (`line_vat`, `zero_total`, periodiseringsfond/INK2 checks for enskild, fixed-salary employee model).
- **Consultant approval** (`src/lib/services/scope.ts`, actions in `src/app/omfattning-actions.ts`): capability **`approve_scope`** — accounting_consultant only (owner/admin/member/auditor cannot; owner cannot self-approve). `approveScopeEntry(entryId, note?)` (idempotent, replaces) / `revokeScopeApproval` write `settings.scope.approvals[]` `{entryId, approvedAt, approvedBy{userId,name,email}, matrixVersion, note?}` and audit `omfattning_godkand` / `omfattning_aterkallad`; `updateScopeFlags` (owner) audits `omfattning_andrad` and never touches approvals.
- **Surfaces.** Public help page **`/omfattning`** (*Vad Ferva stödjer*, `(juridik)` layout, `[data-support-matrix-page]`, `[data-support-entry=<id>][data-support-level]`, `SupportMatrixView`/`SupportMatrixLegend` in `support-matrix-view.tsx`; linked from Villkor §1–2 and from `/support` `[data-support-scope-link]`). Owner: Inställningar → Företag → card **Vad Ferva stödjer för ditt företag** (`scope-card.tsx`, `[data-scope-card]`, verdict + `[data-scope-approvals]` + editable `ScopeQuestions` → `updateScopeFlagsAction`). Consultant: `/redovisning/k/[businessId]/omfattning` (verdict, client answers, full matrix with `ScopeApprovalPanel` per consultant entry: `[data-scope-approve]` with optional note, `[data-scope-revoke]`); the client overview shows `[data-scope-pending]` while consultant cases await approval. Customer forms mention the consultant gating next to the *Omvänd byggmoms* checkbox.
- **Terms.** `LEGAL_DOCUMENTS.villkor` bumped to 2.1 (minor, no re-acceptance): §1 points to `/omfattning`, §2 lists consultant cases.
- **Rule hygiene.** No entry without `source`, `effectiveFrom`, `owner` and at least one existing test file — `src/lib/support/support.test.ts` asserts it (and the §10 level assignments). Changing a level ⇒ bump `SUPPORT_MATRIX_VERSION`.
- **Verify (JSON mode).** `/omfattning` renders 23 entries in five areas. Onboarding preview `/dev/onboarding?steg=1`: choose Aktiebolag → green verdict; tick *Har lager…* → warn verdict and Fortsätt disabled. Reverse charge: Kunder → företag → *Omvänd byggmoms* → invoice → checklist shows the `scope_reverse_charge` blocker until `/redovisning/k/<id>/omfattning` **Godkänn för det här bolaget** (consultant session). Tests: `src/lib/support/support.test.ts`, `src/lib/invoices/reverse-charge.test.ts` (*spärras tills bolagets redovisningskonsult godkänt*), `scripts/adapter-validate.ts` (*Omvänd byggmoms genom adaptern* asserts the gate, the `scope` column round-trip and the onboarding insert).

## Hjälp & support (adjacent)

- **Hjälp & support** `/support` — *Beskriv vad du behöver hjälp med.* Optional attachment *Bifoga bild eller PDF (valfritt)* = the shared `FileDropzone` inline zone **Släpp en bild eller PDF här** ([PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125); native `input[type=file][name="attachment"]` submitted with the form, PNG/JPG/WebP/GIF/PDF, > 5 MB → *Bilagan är för stor (max 5 MB).* under the zone). Submit **Skicka**. Creates `support_tickets`.
- **Ferva Admin** `/admin` (separate dark shell): Översikt, Support, Företag, Användare, Förslag, System, Säkerhet (`/admin/mfa`), Admins. Not in customer nav. See `docs/admin.md`.
  - **MFA (TOTP, Supabase Auth):** required for every platform admin in Supabase mode (cannot be disabled in production; `PLATFORM_ADMIN_REQUIRE_MFA=0` only outside production). `(panel)/layout.tsx` redirects an `aal1` session to `/admin/mfa` (outside the panel layout - no admin data rendered), which shows enrollment (QR + one-time *Reservnyckel*, `data-mfa-enroll-start` / `data-mfa-enroll-confirm`) when no verified factor exists, the challenge (`data-mfa-challenge`, 6-digit code, `?next=/admin/...`) when one does, and factor management (`data-mfa-manage`: add device, remove - needs `aal2`) when the session is already `aal2`. Server actions in `src/app/admin/mfa-actions.ts` (`requirePlatformAdminPreMfa`); `src/lib/platform/mfa.ts` wraps `supabase.auth.mfa.*`. Super admins reset another admin's factors from `/admin/admins` (**Återställ MFA**, reason required, audit `admin_mfa_reset`); the MFA column there reads `auth.admin.mfa.listFactors` via service role (*Okänd* without it). JSON mode: *MFA hanteras av Supabase Auth …* - no simulation.
  - **System** (`/admin/system`): cards *Databas & lagring*, *Mejl (Resend)* (failures, last outbound test, inbound webhook state, **Skicka testmejl till {own email}** → `sendTestEmailAction`), *Abonnemang (Stripe)*, *Version & migrationer* (release vs `EXPECTED_MIGRATION_VERSION`, `src/lib/storage/schema-version.ts`), *Felövervakning (Sentry)*, *Cron, webhooks & integrationer* (last cron run, queued/failed webhooks, Tink, filing provider), *Backup & återställning* (PITR attestation, last documented restore drill, RPO/RTO, ansvarig - **Ej verifierat** until a super admin registers one via `recordRestoreDrillAction`), *AI*, *Deployment & säkerhet*, then recent failures, mail log, admin audit. Ops rows live in `platform_ops_records` (`src/lib/platform/ops.ts`).
  - **Observability:** `@sentry/nextjs` (server/edge/client init files + `src/instrumentation.ts`, only when `SENTRY_DSN`/`NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` set; `next.config.ts` wraps `withSentryConfig` conditionally). `src/lib/observability/scrub.ts` = `beforeSend`/`beforeBreadcrumb` scrubbing (personnummer, tokens, keys, e-mail, IBAN, blobs, request bodies/headers/cookies, tag allowlist). `reportSafeError()` returns a `correlationId` (also in the webhook 500 body / server log). `GET /api/health` adds `ops` + `warnings[]`. Runbooks: `docs/runbooks/`. Restore drill script: `npm run restore:drill -- --target staging --staging-ref <ref>` (refuses production).

The repo has **almost no** `data-testid`. No `data-cy` / `data-qa` / `getByTestId`.

| testid | File | Use |
|--------|------|-----|
| `discard-draft-trigger` | `src/components/discard-draft-button.tsx` | Quote/invoice **button** discard (not the icon-in-register variant) |
| `billing-readiness-banner` | `settings-billing-readiness.tsx` | Missing billing fields |
| `billing-readiness-ready` | same | Silent ready |
| `billing-readiness-success` | same | Just completed |
| `billing-complete-modal` | same | Komplettera-modal body (plain form) |
