### Other stable hooks (prefer these today)

**Auth / onboarding ids:** `auth-email`, `auth-password`, `signup-email`, `signup-phone`, `signup-password`, `reset-email`, `new-password`, `confirm-password`, `ob-*`.

**Document forms:** `offert-saknas`, `offert-kund`, `offert-rubrik`, `offert-rot-rut`, `offert-fastighet`, `offert-fastighet-val`, `offert-fastighet-adress` (RUT), `offert-betalplan`, `quote-send-blockers`, `offert-skicka-epost-{documentId}`, `offert-skicka-telefon-{documentId}` (errors `-fel-`), `faktura-saknas`, `faktura-kund`, `faktura-rot-rut`, `faktura-fastighet`, `faktura-fastighet-val`, `faktura-fastighet-adress` (RUT), `invoice-send-blockers`, `prisrader`.

**Customers / jobs:** `ny-kund-namn`, `ny-kund-epost`, `ny-kund-telefon`, `ny-kund-orgnr`, `ny-kund-kontaktperson`, `kund-namn`, `kund-epost`, `kund-personnummer` (ROT/RUT section only — `#ny-kund-personnummer` is gone), `nytt-uppdrag-titel`, `uppdrag-titel`.

**ROT/RUT bostad ([PR #141](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/141)):** `SearchableCombobox` trigger `#offert-fastighet-val` / `#faktura-fastighet-val` is `role="combobox"` `aria-haspopup="listbox"` `aria-expanded` (same chrome as `customer-picker.tsx`; menu portaled, z-index 55). Options are `role="option"` with no `data-*`. Search input appears only when there are ≥ 5 options (`SEARCH_FROM_COUNT`) and has no id. Footer **+ Ny fastighet** is text. `WorkLocationForm` designation: `#fastighetsbeteckning` (new on kundkort), `#fastighet-{id}` (edit), `#offert-fastighet-ny` / `#faktura-fastighet-ny` (from the document footer). Bostadstyp chips, BRF org.nr, Lägenhetsnummer, **Lägg till** / **Ta bort** are text only.

**Customer activity list ([PR #142](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/142), [PR #159](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/159)):** `[data-activity-tabs]` (buttons are Swedish text **Alla** / **Offerter** / **Fakturor** / **Uppdrag** / **Betalningar** only - no per-tab `data-*`); panel `[data-activity-list][data-activity-min-height][data-activity-visible][data-activity-total]` (min-height value is 0 when visible rows > 0, else empty floor px); sort `button[data-activity-sort=datum|handelse|belopp|status]` (`th` has `aria-sort="ascending"|"descending"|"none"`); **Visa fler** `button[data-activity-visa-fler]`. Not `data-testid`.

**Customer Skapa faktura** (main `683593bd` / `723ab705`): `[data-invoice-create=standalone|picker|from-job|from-job-list|standalone-choice]`; picker menu `[data-invoice-picker]` (`role="menu"`). Not `data-testid`.

**Settings:** `installningar-saknas`, `installningar-address`, `installningar-postalCode`, `installningar-city`, `installningar-bankgiro`, `komplettera-address`, `komplettera-postalCode`, `komplettera-city`, `installningar-rakenskapsar-start`, `installningar-rakenskapsar-slut` (Räkenskapsår date triggers, see below), `installningar-rakenskapsar-fel`.

**Räkenskapsår ([PR #124](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/124)):** the two date ids are `<button type="button" aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-expanded>` triggers of `DateField` - click, then pick in `[role=dialog][aria-label="Välj datum"]` (`button[aria-label="Föregående månad"|"Nästa månad"]`, day buttons by text; adjacent-month days carry `text-muted/70`); there is no text input to type into. Buttons by text only: **Spara räkenskapsår** (disabled until dirty), **Skapa nästa år** (absent once the successor exists); success text *Sparat*; the current-year line *{label}: {start}–{slut}* is plain text. `FiscalYearPicker` chips (Huvudbok / Saldobalans / Resultat / Balans / Moms / Bokslut and the consultant twins) have **no** `data-testid`: match on the link text = the year label (`2026`, `2026/2027`, `2025 (stängt)`) or on `a[href*="ar="]` **excluding** the Huvudbok account chips (`href*="konto="`, which also carry `ar=`); the active chip has `bg-ink text-white`. Period text to assert on: the Huvudbok / Saldobalans / Resultat subtitles (`YYYY-MM-DD till YYYY-MM-DD`), Balans *per {datum}*, Bokslut *Räkenskapsåret {label} …* and **Slutför bokslut {label}**.

**Address autocomplete (all surfaces):** street input `role="combobox"` (`aria-expanded`, `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-busy` while a search is in flight — wait for it to clear before asserting the list); suggestion menu `data-address-suggestions` (portaled — query from `document`, not the form); options `data-address-option={i}`; demo suggestions show a **Demo** tag.

**Other:** `nekat-belopp`, `invite-email`, `invite-form`, `hemsida-ai-beskrivning`, `doman-sok`, `webbformular-mottagare`, `data-nav="back"`, `data-driva-demo="1"`.

**Bokföring (PR #109) ids:** Moms `momsperiod` (select). Lön `lon-namn`, `lon-pnr`, `lon-roll`, `lon-belopp`, `lon-epost`, `lon-start`, `lon-tabell`, `lon-avdrag`, `lon-procent`, `lon-slut`, `ater-{runId}`. Bokslut `reopen-reason-{fiscalYearId}`. Bilagor `saved-days`, `fund-allocation`, `accrual-description`, `accrual-total`, `accrual-account`, `accrual-from`, `accrual-to`. Årsredovisning `ar-verksamhet`, `ar-handelser`, `ar-utdelning`, `sig-name-{i}`, `sig-role-{i}`, `cert-date`, `cert-name`, `cert-role`, `cert-decision`. Aria: `aria-label="Debet"` / `"Kredit"` / `"Ta bort raden"` (manual voucher rows), `"Har underlag"` (paperclip), `"Ta bort underskrift"`, `"Återför från {år}"`; `AccountCombobox` input `aria-haspopup="listbox"` + `role="listbox"` / `role="option"`. Stable hrefs: `a[href^="/api/verifikat/"]` (underlag), `a[href^="/api/bokforing/deklaration?typ="]` (eSKD / AGI / SRU / iXBRL), `a[href*="typ=arkiv"]` (**Ladda ner arkivet**), `a[href^="/bokforing/lon/"]` (lönespecifikation). Tabs: `aria-current="page"` on the active Bokföring tab link, the row wrapper `div[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"|"avancerat"]` and the toggle `button[data-bokforing-mode]` ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)). No `data-testid` anywhere in the new surfaces - including the #137 pages Underlag / Bank / Skatt / Kontoregister, which have **no** new `data-*` either (match on `<title>` / heading text: *Underlag*, *Bank*, *Skatt*, *Kontoregister*, the toggle labels **Visa redovisningsvy** / **Visa enkel vy**, the line *Tillbaka till Att göra*, the checkbox *Bara ekonomiska dokument*, **Lägg till konto**, **Skicka till redovisningskonsult**, *Bokför inte F-skatt automatiskt*).

**Receipt file:** link text **Visa kvitto** with `href^="/api/kvitto/"` in `ExpenseRegister` (only when a file is available on the receipt row or via the inbox attachment - a receipt booked from the Inbox carries its file since [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130), commit `8e7a594`). Upload controls are drop zones since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) (landed on main with [PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)) - there is no `<label>` + hidden input pattern any more and no testid.

**File drop zones (`FileDropzone`, [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125)) and the receipt queue (`ReceiptUpload`, `90d43e3`):** zone = `div[role="button"]` (`tabindex="0"`, `-1` while locked; no role when the zone carries children: import center, support form) with `aria-label` = the visible title (*Släpp kvitton och fakturor här* on `/bokforing/underlag` and the `/bokforing` overview, *Släpp kvitton här* on Utgifter, *Lägg till kvitto* on attention items / register rows, *Släpp kvittot här* on **Ny utgift** (`manual-expense-form.tsx`), *Släpp underlaget här*, *Släpp SIE-filen här* / *Släpp en annan SIE-fil här*, *Dra filer hit eller välj från enheten*, *Släpp prisfilen här*, *Släpp en bild eller PDF här*; the two `ReceiptUpload` landing zones read *Läser av {n} av {total} …* while the queue runs), `aria-disabled` when locked, `aria-busy` while `busy` (the attention item's compact zone while its upload is pending - title *Läser in …* meanwhile; the SIE zone while previewing; the prisfil zone while reading. The compact `ReceiptUpload` on register / Bank rows swaps the zone for the text *Läser av …* instead, the landing receipt zones stay `busy={false}` and signal through their rows, and the import, underlag and support zones never set it), `data-dropzone="import"` + `data-import-dropzone` on the import zone only. The real `input[type=file]` is `sr-only`, a sibling **before** the zone inside the same wrapper (set files on it directly; `data-import-file-input` / `data-price-file-input` where they existed before, `name="attachment"` on the support form, otherwise unmarked - scope it through the wrapper or the zone's `aria-label`). Camera: `button[data-dropzone-camera]` **Fota kvitto** (landing/inline) or `button[aria-label="Fota kvitto"]` (compact), both only visible on coarse pointers (`pointer-coarse:`), driving a separate `input[type=file][accept="image/*"][capture="environment"]`. Paste: on the zone, plus document-wide on `/bokforing/underlag`, the `/bokforing` overview and Utgifter (`pasteAnywhere`). Queue: `[data-receipt-upload]` wrapper (landing/inline only) → `li[data-receipt-upload-row][data-status="vantar"|"laser"|"klar"|"fel"]`, row texts *Väntar · {size}* / *Läser av …* / *Bokfört* / *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna* / *Kvitto sparat* / *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* / server error, `button[aria-label="Ta bort ur listan"]`, footer **Rensa**; the list is `aria-live="polite"`. Picked-file clear: **Ta bort** text (inline/landing) or `button[aria-label="Ta bort fil"]` (compact). Size and server errors are plain text under the zone (attention items: the row's error span) except the verifikat underlag, which uses the form's `role="alert"`.

**PR #130 `data-*` hooks (no testids):** Bokföring `button[data-bokforing-mode="enkelt"|"avancerat"]` (**Visa redovisningsvy** / **Visa enkel vy** since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137); was *Visa avancerat* / *Visa enkelt*) inside `div[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"|"avancerat"]`; calendar `section[data-skv-calendar]`, `a[data-skv-event][data-skv-status=forfallen|snart|kommande|klar]`, ICS `a[href="/api/bokforing/kalender"]`; Moms `#period-{key}` + `data-vat-period` / `data-vat-state` / `data-vat-step`, rail `li[data-vat-rail-step][data-vat-rail-status]` inside `ol[aria-label="Momsens tre steg"]`, `[data-vat-step-body]`, checklist `li[data-vat-check][data-vat-check-ok]`, `button[data-vat-declare]` → `[data-vat-declare-confirm]`, `[data-vat-ocr-field]`, `aria-label="Ändra OCR-nummer"`, `aria-label="Kopiera ruta {nn}"` / `"Kopiera belopp"` / `"Kopiera bankgiro"` / `"Kopiera ocr-nummer"`; Hem `section[data-home-money]`; Uppdrag `[data-testid=job-incoming-message]`, `[data-rot-deadline]`, `aria-label="Ta bort foto"`, `[data-job-avtalat]` / `[data-job-kvar]` (Ekonomi headline Avtalat / Kvar values, no testid); send modals `[data-rot-share]`; quote overflow `[data-quote-owner-actions]`, `[data-quote-owner-fakturera]`, `[data-quote-withdraw]`; line editor `[data-line-editor]`, `[data-line-id]`, `#rad-{id}-rabatt` etc., `aria-label="Rabatt i procent"` / `"Rubrik"` / `"Kopiera rad"` / `"Flytta upp"` / `"Flytta ner"`; credit dialog `[role=radiogroup][aria-label="Vad ska krediteras"]` + `input[name="kredit-lage"]`; Inställningar `#installningar-rakenskapsar-start` / `-slut` / `-fel`; Ny utgift `[role=radiogroup][aria-label="Typ av utgift"|"Vem betalade"|"Sorts representation"]`; Ekonomi rows `#utgift-{id}[data-highlighted="1"]` / `#tx-{id}` after `?atgard=`; modals `button[data-modal-close]` (**Stäng**) with `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"`; toasts `[data-toast-viewport]` / `[data-toast][data-toast-tone]`; empty states `[data-clear-filters]`; shop `aria-label="Filtrera artiklar"`, `"Spara {artikel} som favorit"`, `"Skanna streckkod"`, `[data-in-cart]`.

**Grossistbeställningar (PR #111) hooks:** `data-*` attributes, no ids or testids — `data-job-add-entry` (**Lägg till**), `data-job-add-material` (**Material** segment), `data-job-line-editor` (manual Tid/Material DocLine fields), `data-add-material-choice` (chooser; value = Swedish label), `data-invoice-readiness`, `data-document-line-review`, `data-split-jobs`, `data-comparable-unit-price`, `data-purchase-orders-section`, `data-wholesaler-search` / `-results` / `-result` / `-add` / `-cart-button` / `-cart-lines` / `-cart-line` / `-review-button` / `-mail-preview` / `-send` / `-manual`, `data-purchase-order-lines` / `-line` / `-confirmation`, `data-approve-confirmation`, `data-set-customer-price`, `data-inbox-order-candidates`, `data-link-order`, `data-inbox-linked-order`, `data-wholesaler-connection=<id>`, `data-price-file-input`. Feature toggles (all three features): `data-feature=<id>` + `data-feature-action=activate|deactivate|confirm-deactivate`. Connection-form inputs: `id$="-grossist"`, `id$="-kundnummer"`, `id$="-ordermejl"` (prefix is a React `useId`). Aria labels are listed in [Grossistbeställningar](#grossistbeställningar-wholesale-orders). Browser script: `scripts/verify-wholesalers-browser.ts`.

**Aria / roles used by existing browser scripts:**

- Nav links: `aria-label` = Swedish section name (plus badge text).
- Create: `aria-label="Ny offert"|"Ny faktura"|"Ny kund"`.
- Discard icon: `aria-label="Kasta offertutkast"|"Kasta fakturautkast"`.
- Command bar: `role="listbox"` `aria-label="Förslag"`.
- Mer sheet: `role="dialog"` `aria-label="Mer"`. Mobile bottom bar: `nav[aria-label="Huvudnavigation"]`. Desktop Mer group: `[data-nav-group="mer"]`. Active nav link: `aria-current="page"`.
- Demo menu: `aria-haspopup="menu"`.
- List rows: `aria-label={customer.name}` / `{job.title}`.
- Attention overflow: `aria-label` prefix *Fler alternativ för*.
- Command-bar voice: `button[aria-label="Använd röst"|"Stoppa inspelning"|"Avbryt röstinmatning"]` (`scripts/verify-voice-browser.ts`).

**Demo fixtures (stable ids/tokens):** see [Demo mode](#demo-mode). Prefer these over brittle row indexes.

**Existing automated coverage:** `src/lib/*.test.ts` (domain, no DOM). Browser scripts in `scripts/verify-*.ts` expect **dev server :3123** and mostly use text/aria, not testids. **CI** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, every PR + push to main, no external services): `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test`, `npm run test:db` (PGlite), `npm run test:adapter` (PGlite), `npm run build`. Browser scripts are **not** in CI.

---

## Missing verification hooks

Important UI with **no** `data-testid` (agents must use text, href, or fragile CSS):

- Sidebar / bottom nav / Mer sheet items and badges
- Command bar input, suggestion rows, confirm cards
- Address suggestion list / pick flow (use `[data-address-suggestions]`, `[data-address-option]`, the `role="combobox"` input, or the **Demo** tag text instead)
- Hem attention rows, watching rows, reminder rows + Klar/Snooza
- Ekonomi / Underlag / Bokföring **tabs** and Kunder / Uppdrag **filter chips** (the Bokföring row has `[data-bokforing-tabs]` on its wrapper since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137), the links themselves are text + `aria-current`; the Underlag checkbox **Bara ekonomiska dokument** is an `input[type=checkbox]` wrapped in a `<label>` with that text - no id or `data-*`)
- Customer activity filter tab **buttons** ([PR #142](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/142)): wrapper `[data-activity-tabs]` exists, but each tab is Swedish text only (Alla / Offerter / Fakturor / Uppdrag / Betalningar) with no per-key `data-*`
- Customer **Skapa faktura** picker: wrapper `[data-invoice-create=picker|standalone]` and `[data-invoice-picker]` exist, but menu items are Swedish text / `role="menuitem"` (**Från offert**, **Från uppdrag**, **Fristående**, nested `Offert #N` / job titles). Nested Från offert / Från uppdrag lists have no per-id `data-*` beyond the wrappers (`from-job` / `from-job-list`). There is **no** separate **Fristående faktura** customer CTA. Do not add product test IDs.
- Register rows (quote/invoice/customer/job/inbox) as clickable units (invoice rows only mark the **title** with `invoice-list-title`; the link is sr-only text)
- Quote/invoice detail primary actions: Skicka, Redigera / Redigera faktura, Visa kundvy (invoice only — the quote page has no *Öppna kundvyn*), Kopiera kundlänk (discard button is the exception)
- Public offer page: **Godkänn offert** / **Avböj offerten** are the exception (`public-quote-accept` below the card, `public-quote-accept-bar` + `public-quote-accept-name-bar` in the mobile bar, `public-quote-decline`); the desktop name field is only `#godkann-namn` (bar: `#godkann-namn-bar`, section `#godkann-offert`), and the intyg / print links, accepted banner and receipt are text or `data-*` only
- Owner live refresh (`LiveRefresh`, [PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127)) renders nothing, so there is nothing to hook and no testid is proposed - assert on the refreshed content (row badge text, `[data-quote-accepted-banner]`) or on the RSC re-fetch after Back/Forward / refocus / the `driva-live-refresh` message
- Job detail header / **…** menu: see the Uppdrag bullet below. `Markera som klart` is **Avsluta uppdrag** in **…** (never a page-level button); the delete item is **Ta bort** (modal title *Ta bort uppdraget?*). `[data-testid=job-primary-invoice]` covers the invoice primary when that is the header action.
- Underlag (`/bokforing/underlag/[id]`, the old Inbox) detail workflow + Skapa bankfil
- Receipt upload ([PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125) + `90d43e3`): no `data-testid` anywhere, but the drop zones **do** have stable hooks - use them (see *File drop zones* under [Stable selectors](#stable-selectors--test-ids)): the zone `div[role="button"][aria-label="<title>"]` (*Lägg till kvitto* on attention items / register rows, *Släpp kvitton här* on Utgifter, *Släpp kvitton och fakturor här* on `/bokforing/underlag` and the Bokföring overview), the `sr-only` `input[type=file]` sibling before it (unmarked - scope through the zone's wrapper), `[data-receipt-upload]` + `li[data-receipt-upload-row][data-status=…]` for the queue, `[data-dropzone-camera]` for **Fota kvitto**. Gaps: the compact zones have no `data-*` at all (aria-label only); the hidden inputs of the receipt, underlag and SIE zones carry no attribute (only import/price do); *Kvitto sparat* / *Kvitto sparat och matchat*, the row notes *Bokfört* / *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna* / *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.*, the thumbnails, *Läser av …* / *Läser in …* and every server error are text only; **Visa kvitto** is only `href^="/api/kvitto/"`; the verifikat underlag size error *Underlaget är för stort (max 8 MB).* is the form's `role="alert"`
- **Ny utgift** (`/ekonomi/utgifter/ny`): since [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170), kind radios have `#expense-kind-{kop|utlagg|milersattning|traktamente|representation}`, submit has `#expense-submit`, and Traktamente reseräkning fields have the `#traktamente-*` ids (`#traktamente-land`, `#traktamente-landnamn`, `#traktamente-resmal`, `#traktamente-avresetid`, `#traktamente-hemkomsttid`, `#traktamente-anledning`, `#traktamente-frikost`, `#traktamente-fri-logi`, `#traktamente-50km`) + `[data-traktamente-vem|dagar|belopp]`. Still missing stable hooks for: supplier/description, amount, moms, category, job, date, milersättning km/vehicle, representation sorts/person count, Vem betalade radios, and the generic Så bokförs det / Bokför … controls for non-traktamente kinds
- Uppdrag: the **…** menu items (*Avsluta uppdrag*, *Återöppna uppdrag*, *Redigera uppdrag*, *Foton*, *Ta bort*), the **Lägg till foto** button and its hidden `input[capture]`, the photo grid, the remaining *Ekonomi* numbers (Fakturerat, Inköp, Täckning) and the **Ny förfrågan** chip / *Väntar på offert* cell in the list - text only (`[data-testid=job-incoming-message]`, `[data-testid=job-primary-invoice]`, `[data-testid=job-photos]`, `aria-label="Ta bort foto"`, `[data-job-add-entry]`, `[data-job-add-material]`, `[data-job-line-editor]`, `[data-invoice-readiness]`, `[data-job-avtalat]` and `[data-job-kvar]` are the exceptions)
- Materialkedjan ([PR #151](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/151), [PR #153](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/153)): chooser exists via `[data-add-material-choice]` (value = Swedish label **Sök och beställ** / **Fota kvitto** / **Lägg till manuellt**). The job page has no Inköpsreferens card, no **Kopiera referens** / **Fota materialköp**, and opening the page must not mint `FV-`. Invoice-readiness copy (*Redo att fakturera* / *Du kan fakturera det som är klart. Resten ligger kvar på uppdraget.* / *Inget klart att fakturera ännu.*) is text only. Underlag Kontrollera: wrapper `[data-document-line-review]` and split `[data-split-jobs]`; per-line Till kunden / Företagskostnad / Privat / **Bekräfta rader** and the other review actions are text/aria only - no per-line `data-*`. Do not add product test IDs.
- Quote draft send contact ([PR #165](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/165), [PR #167](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/167)): **E-post** / **Telefon** live in the Skicka modal (`#offert-skicka-epost-{documentId}` / `#offert-skicka-telefon-{documentId}`, errors `-fel-`) but **no** `data-testid`. Soft-gap banners under the draft title are a bare `<p>` (match the Swedish in `quoteContactGapCopy`). Channel radios: `role="radiogroup"` `aria-label="Hur ska offerten skickas"`, each option `role="radio"` titled **E-post** / **SMS** / **E-post och SMS** — no `data-testid` / `data-*`. Do not add product test IDs.
- Quote/invoice share + copy: **Kopiera offert**, **Kopiera kundlänk**, **Dela**, **SMS** (`a[href^="sms:"]` is the only handle and its href is rewritten on click), **Personligt meddelande** (no id; quote modal only, hidden on SMS-only), *Förhandsgranska och dela*; the *Skicka offert* / *Skicka faktura* send modals have no `data-*`
- Line editor: **Rubrik**, **Från register** and the *Artikelregister* picker rows (quote/invoice lists only), **Flytta upp/ner** / **Kopiera rad** (aria only), *Raden borttagen* toast + **Ångra**; no per-row *Spara i registret*; no `data-testid` (ids are `rad-{lineId}-{fält}`, container `[data-line-editor]`; job single-line `[data-job-line-editor]`)
- Kreditera dialog: the two radios have `name="kredit-lage"` but no ids, the amount input is `useId()`, **Kreditera hela fakturan** / **Kreditera {belopp}** / **Avbryt** are text only
- Artikelregister (Inställningar → Fakturering): **Ny artikel** row (placeholders *Beskrivning* / *Enhet* / *kr*, unlabelled `<select>`), **Spara**, delete only via `aria-label="Ta bort {beskrivning}"`
- Räkenskapsår ([PR #124](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/124)): the `FiscalYearPicker` year chips on Huvudbok / Rapporter / Moms / Bokslut (link text = year label, `href*="ar="`; no id / `data-*`, the picker itself has no wrapper hook and is absent with one year) and the Företag card buttons **Spara räkenskapsår** / **Skapa nästa år** + *Sparat* and the year list (text only; the two date triggers and the error line do have ids)
- Skatteverket calendar: rows have `data-skv-event` / `data-skv-status`, but **Lägg i kalendern** is only `a[href="/api/bokforing/kalender"]`
- Moms steps: rich `data-vat-*` hooks (see above), but **Hämta deklarationsfil**, **Underlag (CSV)**, **Öppna Lämna momsdeklaration**, **Öppna OCR-beräkningen**, **Ja, den är inlämnad** / **Inte ännu** (inside `[data-vat-declare-confirm]`) and **För {period} till skattekontot** are text/href only; the OCR input is `useId()`
- F-skatt card (Skattekonto / Fakturering): **Ändra**, **Belopp per månad** (`useId()`), the amount - text only
- ROT ([PR #141](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/141)): kundkort year-cap widget is **gone** (no *Ferva i {år}*). Bostad combobox trigger ids exist (`#offert-fastighet-val` / `#faktura-fastighet-val`) but options, **+ Ny fastighet**, and the search field (when ≥ 5 properties) have no `data-*`. `WorkLocationForm` BRF / lägenhet / bostadstyp chips and **Lägg till fastighet** / **Ta bort** are text. Preliminärt-avdrag edit input is `aria-label` *ROT-avdrag att använda* / *RUT-avdrag att använda* only (no id). The old *ROT använt {år}* aria input is **gone**. Beslut import `input[type=file][accept="application/json,.json"]` + sr-only **Importera**, `[data-rot-deadline]` / `[data-rot-share]` exist but their buttons/copy are text
- Material shop: favorite hearts, category chips, **Skanna streckkod**, the margin badge and the shelves (*Beställt tidigare* / *Favoriter* / *Kategorier*) - aria/text only
- Hem money strip: `section[data-home-money]` only - no per-tile hooks
- Error pages: *Sidan kunde inte visas* / *Sidan finns inte*, **Försök igen**, **Till Hem** - text only (the digest is a bare `<p>`)
- Hemsida: **Publicera hemsidan** / **Publicera ändringar**, **Återställ** and their confirm modals (*Publicera hemsidan*, *Återställ ändringar?*) - button text + modal title only; the **Opublicerade ändringar** badge is text only
- Bokföring overview question cards and VAT mark-declared
- Bokföring **tab strip** (Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Skatt in enkelt läge, 10 tabs in avancerat) and the Rapporter chips incl. **Konton** - link text + `aria-current` only; the exceptions are the wrapper `[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"|"avancerat"]` and the mode toggle `button[data-bokforing-mode]` (text **Visa redovisningsvy** / **Visa enkel vy**); the deep-link line *{Sida}. Tillbaka till Att göra* and the first-switch toast are text only
- Underlag / Bank / Skatt / Kontoregister pages ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)): no `data-testid` or new `data-*` - QA matches on `<title>` (*Underlag · Ferva*, *Bank · Ferva*, *Skatt · Ferva*, *Konton · Ferva*), headings (*Underlag*, *Bank*, *Skatt*, *Kontoregister*) and Swedish button text (**Hantera bankhändelser** on Ekonomi, **Lägg till konto** / **Byt namn** / **Arkivera** / **Återställ**, *Sök konto*, **Stämmer** for the suggested OCR, **Skicka till redovisningskonsult** + the sr-only *Mejladress till redovisningskonsulten*, **Stäng alla klara månader** / **Stäng N månader**, *Bokför inte F-skatt automatiskt* (`useId()` checkbox), Verifikationer search (sr-only *Sök verifikationer* / *Från datum* / *Till datum*, placeholder *Sök på text, belopp eller konto*), the Mall chips **Omföring** … **Rättelse** and **Händelsen inträffade ett annat datum** on Nytt verifikat)
- Manuellt verifikat: **Bokföringsdatum** / **Handelsdatum** / **Vad har hänt?** inputs have no ids (labels wrap them); account picker rows; **Lägg till rad**, the underlag zone (`[role="button"][aria-label="Släpp underlaget här"]` + unmarked `sr-only` `input[type=file]`, no **Välj fil** button since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125)), **Bokför verifikatet**, success **Öppna M{n}** / **Bokför ett till**
- Skattekonto / Skatt: **För … till skattekontot**, **Bokför F-skatt {YYYY-MM}** (only rendered after the auto-booking opt-out), **Bokför som inbetalning (…)**, the reconcile `textarea` (placeholder only), **Stäm av** / **Rensa**, result texts
- Lön: **Lägg upp lönen** / **Spara ändringen** / **Ändra lönen**, **Bokför lön {månad}**, **Återför** / **Återför lönen**, **Ta fram deklaration för {månad}**, **Markera {månad} som lämnad**, **Avsluta anställningen**, **Hämta deklarationsfil (XML)**, personnummer **Visa** toggle
- Periodstängning: **Stäng {månad}** → **Ja, stäng perioden**; checklist rows and the month table badges
- Ingående balans: the SIE zone (`[role="button"][aria-label="Släpp SIE-filen här"]` + unmarked `sr-only` `input[type=file][accept=".se,…"]`, no **Välj SIE-fil** button since [PR #125](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/125)), preview table, **Sätt som ingående balans {år}**
- Bokslut: checklist rows, **Slutför bokslut {år}** → **Ja, slutför bokslutet**, **Bokför bokslutsposterna**, **Generera årsredovisning**, **Öppna året igen** → **Ja, öppna {år}** (only the reason textarea has an id), **Ladda ner arkivet** (href only)
- Bilagor: doubtful-invoice checkboxes (label text = *Faktura #n – kund*), **Bokför bilagan**, **Bokför periodiseringen**
- Avstämning: rows, **Stämmer** / **För hand** badges, difference cells
- Årsredovisning: **Markera som granskad / signerad / inlämnad**, **Spara texten** / **Spara underskrifterna** / **Lägg till person**, **Hämta iXBRL-filen**, **Visa som A4**, status badge; A4 view `PdfPrintBar`
- INK2: adjustment rows (ruta numbers are plain text), **Hämta BLANKETTER.SRU** / **Hämta INFO.SRU**
- Inlämning panel: **Förbered inlämning**, **Generera om**, **Signera**, **Lämna in till Skatteverket/Bolagsverket**, **Hämta kvittens**, **Gör ett nytt försök**, status badge text, file/SHA list
- Kund **Omvänd byggmoms** checkbox (only Ny kund has `name=reverseChargeConstruction`; the edit form checkbox has neither id nor name) and the invoice form note / document section for reverse charge
- Verifikationer **Nytt verifikat** button (href only), paperclip icon, underlag link
- Demo menu items (Återställ / Avsluta / Visa redovisningsvyn / Skapa konto)
- Settings tab strip (link text + `aria-current` only; the feature toggles **do** have `data-feature` / `data-feature-action`)
- Grossistbeställningar: **Lägg till grossist** / **Spara grossist** and the connection form (only `-grossist` / `-kundnummer` / `-ordermejl` ids; the rest is label-wrapped), **Ändra** / **Aktivera** / **Inaktivera** per connection (`aria-label="Ändra {grossist}"` only), the price-file modal steps (**Importera prislistan** / **Spara rabatter** / **Klar**, the column selects under *Vilken kolumn är vad?*), cart qty inputs and comments (aria-label only), **Granska beställning** / **Skicka beställning** have `data-*` but the review's blocker list does not, **Avbryt beställningen** → **Ja, avbryt**, the **Ange kundpris** opener (text button; the field is `aria-label="Kundpris kr per {enhet}"` and only its **Spara** carries `[data-set-customer-price]`), inbox candidate rows (only the **Det är den här** button has `[data-link-order=<orderId>]`)
- Samarbeta invite submit (has `#invite-email` only)
- Support form submit
- Auth submit buttons (fields have ids; buttons do not)
- Fältläge `/falt` (`field-mode.tsx`) and the connectivity pill (`ConnectivityStatus`): **no** `data-testid` / `data-field-*` / `data-connectivity-*`. Use Swedish button text (**Starta tid**, **Stoppa**, **Spara anteckning**, **Ta foto**, **Synka nu**, **Försök igen**, **Ta bort**), stable ids where present (`#falt-kvitto`, `#falt-kund`, `#falt-titel`, `#h-<jobId>`, etc.), and queue badge text (Väntar / Synkar / Konflikt / …)

---

## Highest-value missing verification hooks

Add **only** these. Enough to make the main Swedish flows automatable without a dump of one id per widget.

| Priority | Proposed `data-testid` | Where | Why |
|----------|------------------------|-------|-----|
| 1 | `nav-item-{hem,uppdrag,kunder,ekonomi,bokforing,hemsida,samarbeta,installningar}` | `nav.tsx` | Reach any area without depending on visible label/CSS. Include mobile Mer. (No `inbox` since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137).) |
| 2 | `command-bar-input` | `command-bar.tsx` | Hem’s primary control; every “do X from Hem” repro starts here. |
| 3 | `quote-row-{id}` / `invoice-row-{id}` | `economy-register.tsx` | Open #116 / #1042 without matching Swedish status text. |
| 4 | `quote-send` / `invoice-send` | draft send components | Pair with existing `discard-draft-trigger` + checklists. |
| 5 | `attention-item-{actionId}` | `attention-list.tsx` | Hem/Bokföring share ids; needed for “Inte aktuell”, remind, bankfil. |
| 6 | `public-quote-accept` / `public-quote-decline` (**added**) | `quote-accept.tsx` / `quote-public-actions.tsx` | Customer accept is the product’s signature moment. |
| 7 | `demo-menu` + `demo-reset` + `demo-end` | `demo-menu.tsx` | Every live/QA session enters and resets here. |
| 8 | `job-row-{id}` + `job-create-invoice` | uppdrag list + `job-controls.tsx` | Job → invoice is the core money path. |
| 9 | `underlag-row-{id}` + `underlag-create-payment-file` | `inbox-list.tsx` / Underlag detail (`/bokforing/underlag`) | Badge/open/pay path; today only address text is asserted. |
| 10 | `ekonomi-tab-{offerter,fakturor,utgifter}` | tab strips | Agents constantly miss that Offerter/Fakturor are **tabs** under Ekonomi (Uppdrag is its own nav item; Bank is a Bokföring tab since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)). |
| 11 | `bokforing-tab-{oversikt,underlag,bank,verifikationer,huvudbok,rapporter,skatt,moms,skattekonto,lon,bokslut}` + `inlamning-{generate,sign,submit,receipt}` + `bokslut-close` / `bokslut-reopen` / `period-close` / `period-close-all` | `bokforing-advanced-nav.tsx`, `inlamning.tsx`, `bokforing-widgets.tsx`, `periodstangning-widgets.tsx`, `close-all-ready-months-button.tsx` | The accounting product (#109, reshaped by #137) has four tabs in enkelt and ten in avancerat plus one shared filing panel on four pages; today every step is matched on Swedish button text that includes a period or year. `[data-bokforing-tabs]` / `[data-bokforing-mode]` exist for the row and the toggle only. |
| 12 | `expense-kind-{kop,utlagg,milersattning,traktamente,representation}` + `expense-submit` (**added as `id`s on `manual-expense-form.tsx` in [PR #170](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/170)**, together with the `#traktamente-*` reseräkning fields); `job-add-hour` / `job-timer` (removed from the job page 2026-09-13 — field timer lives under Fältläge `/falt`; do not add these IDs on `job-work.tsx`) / `job-photo-input`; `credit-mode-{hel,del}` + `credit-amount` + `credit-submit`; `share-sms` / `share-copy` / `quote-copy`; `article-picker-open` + `article-row-{id}`; `fiscal-year-{save,next}` | `manual-expense-form.tsx`, `job-photos.tsx`, `field-mode.tsx` (timer, if hooking later), `money-widgets.tsx`, `share-customer-link.tsx`, `quote-chain-actions.tsx`, `lines-editor.tsx`, `fiscal-year-settings.tsx` | The Wave 1–3 surfaces ([PR #130](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/130)) ship with `data-*` on containers (`data-receipt-upload`, `data-vat-*`, `data-home-money`) but their **actions** are Swedish button text with amounts or times in them (*Bokför 1 250 kr*, *Stoppa 03:12* on `/falt`, *Kreditera 5 000 kr*). |

Do **not** add testids to every settings field or design token — those already have `id=` hooks (`ob-*`, `offert-*`, `installningar-*`).

## Reproduction recipes (quick)

**Always start from a clean demo:** `/demo` or Återställ demo. Seed ids above are valid after reset.

| Task | Steps |
|------|-------|
| Open Offerter | `/demo` → **Ekonomi** → tab Offerter (default) |
| Delete a quote | Only #116 → *Kasta utkast* → confirm. Sent quotes: Hem *Inte aktuell* or public *Avböj*. |
| Accept a quote | `/offert/demo-bertil-fasad` → accept chrome **below** the document → name → Godkänn offert. An owner tab left on Ekonomi → Offerter in the same browser flips #115 to **Godkänd** by itself; Back/Forward or refocus after ≥ 1 s re-fetches too ([PR #127](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/127)) |
| Overdue invoice | `/ekonomi/fakturor/inv-1042` |
| Underlag badge item (the old Inbox item) | `/bokforing/underlag/inbox-mail-byggmax` (`/inbox/inbox-mail-byggmax` still 307s there); it is the “1” inside the **Bokföring** badge, not a badge of its own |
| Upload a receipt file | `/bokforing` → *Kvitto saknas – Clas Ohlson, 349 kr* → compact zone `[role="button"][aria-label="Lägg till kvitto"]` (drop a file, or set the `sr-only` `input[type=file]` next to it; file < 1,5 MB) → *Kvitto sparat och matchat* → Ekonomi → Utgifter → **Visa kvitto** |
| Upload to Underlag (no purchase) | Ekonomi → Utgifter → zone **Släpp kvitton här**, or `/bokforing/underlag` / the `/bokforing` overview → zone **Släpp kvitton och fakturor här** (`[data-receipt-upload]`; drop, click, or Ctrl/Cmd+V anywhere on the page) → a ~1,2 MB PNG/PDF works (old data-URL cap was ~730 KB) → `[data-receipt-upload-row][data-status="klar"]` with *Bokfört* / *I inboxen – kontrollera uppgifterna*; > 8 MB → `data-status="fel"` *För stor ({size}) – max 8 MB.* with zero requests ([PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137); 5 MB before); several files queue and upload one at a time. Verifikat underlag: `/bokforing/verifikationer/nytt` → zone **Släpp underlaget här** > 8 MB → *Underlaget är för stort (max 8 MB).* at selection |
| Ny utgift (milersättning) | Ekonomi → **Ny utgift** (or `/ekonomi/utgifter/ny?typ=milersattning`) → radiogroup *Typ av utgift* = **Milersättning** → km + **Egen bil** → banner *Bokförs som skuld till dig (2893) …* → **Bokför {belopp}** → lands on `/ekonomi?flik=utgifter&atgard=utgift-<id>` with the highlighted row *Milersättning · Bokfört · betalt privat* and the banner *Bolaget är skyldigt dig {belopp}* |
| Momsen i tre steg | `/bokforing/moms` (or the enkelt tab **Skatt** `/bokforing/skatt`, same cards) → card `#period-2026-K1` (`data-vat-step="deklarera"`) → **Jag har deklarerat** → **Ja, den är inlämnad** → card flips to `data-vat-step="betala"` with *Bankgiro · Skatteverket 5050-1055* + the OCR field → paste a valid OCR → **Spara** → **För januari–mars 2026 till skattekontot** |
| Enkelt ↔ avancerat | `/bokforing` → tab row *Att göra · Underlag · Bank · Skatt* in `[data-bokforing-tabs="enkelt"]` → `button[data-bokforing-mode="enkelt"]` **Visa redovisningsvy** → 10 tabs (*… Verifikationer · Huvudbok · Rapporter · Moms · Skattekonto · Lön · Bokslut*, no Skatt), toast *Redovisningsvyn visar verifikationer, huvudbok och rapporter. …* on the first switch, cookie `driva_bokforing_lage=avancerat` → **Visa enkel vy** back; the mode survives a reload (cookie) and, without a cookie, follows `meta.bookkeepingModeByUser` (`persistBookkeepingModeAction`); a deep link to `/bokforing/huvudbok` renders in both modes - in enkelt with *Huvudbok. Tillbaka till Att göra* under the row |
| Skatteverket-kalender | `/bokforing` → section `[data-skv-calendar]` *Kommande till Skatteverket* → row `a[data-skv-event="moms-2026-K2"][data-skv-status="forfallen"]` → `/bokforing/moms?fokus=2026-K2`; **Lägg i kalendern** → `GET /api/bokforing/kalender` → `skatteverket.ics` with one `VEVENT` per row |
| Delkredit | `/ekonomi/fakturor/inv-1042` → **Fler åtgärder** → **Kreditera** → **En del av beloppet** → *Belopp att kreditera, inkl. moms* `5000` → **Kreditera 5 000 kr** → new *Kreditfaktura* row; #1042 keeps `status: "skickad"` (still overdue in the list) and `invoiceOutstanding` drops to 18 000 kr; in a real tenant the customer gets *Kreditfaktura från …* |
| Kopiera offert / dela | `/ekonomi/offerter/quote-fasad` → **Fler åtgärder** → **Kopiera offert** → new utkast *Kopia av Fasadarbete och nya fönsterfoder*; **SMS** → `a[href^="sms:"]` whose body becomes *Offert från oss* + the public link on click |
| Lead → offert | `/uppdrag` → row **Ny förfrågan** *Byte av köksluckor och bänkskiva* (Sara Nilsson, *Via webbformulär*) → `[data-testid=job-incoming-message]` *Ny förfrågan från kunden* → **Skapa offert** → the message is the offer description; back on the job the card reads *Kundens förfrågan* and the list chip is gone |
| Uppdrag: tid + foto | `/uppdrag/job-kok` → **Lägg till** → *Tid* → `LinesEditor` `variant="single"` (`[data-job-line-editor]`) → `1` timme → a new labour row (description + kr/tim from the quoted labour line when the job has one, else *Arbete* at the default hourly rate); **Lägg till** → *Material* → `AddMaterialSheet` chooser → **Lägg till manuellt** → the same single-line editor; **…** → *Foton* → image → toast **Foto tillagt** + thumbnail in the dialog (inline data-URL, max 24) |
| Räkenskapsår (Skapa nästa år) | `/installningar?flik=foretag` → **Räkenskapsår** (`#installningar-rakenskapsar-start` / `-slut` hold *1 januari 2026* / *31 december 2026*) → **Skapa nästa år** → a second row *2027: …* → `/bokforing/huvudbok` now shows the `FiscalYearPicker` pills (*2026* / *2027*) and `?ar=2027` opens the empty year. Changing the dates of the seeded year is possible (**Spara räkenskapsår** → *Sparat*) but leaves verifications outside the year - do it on an empty JSON dev company instead |
| Artikelregister | `/installningar?flik=fakturering` → **Artikelregister** → **Ny artikel** → *Beskrivning* `Montering kök`, Arbete, `tim`, `650` → **Spara** → Ekonomi → **Ny offert** → line editor **Från register** → pick it → row filled |
| Address autocomplete (demo) | `/demo` → Kunder → **Ny kund** → type `Väd` in **Adress** → `[data-address-suggestions]` with **Demo** tag → pick → adress + postnummer + ort filled |
| Manuellt verifikat | `/bokforing/verifikationer` → **Nytt verifikat** → *Vad har hänt?* + two rows (e.g. 6991 debet 100 / 1930 kredit 100) → **Bokför verifikatet** → *M1 är bokförd på 100 kr* → **Öppna M1** |
| Skattekonto F-skatt | Since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137) F-skatt is booked for you: open any `/bokforing/*` page → `ensureAutoFSkattBookings` has posted *F-skatt {YYYY-MM}* (2518/1630, `createdBy: "auto"`) for every past charge date - on the demo the seed already carries 2026-01 … 2026-09, so `/bokforing/skatt` → *Rörelser på kontot* lists them and there is **no** *Bokför F-skatt* card. To see the manual path: `/bokforing/skatt` (or `/bokforing/skattekonto`) → **Inställning** → tick **Bokför inte F-skatt automatiskt** → an *F-skatt {YYYY-MM}* card with **Bokför F-skatt {YYYY-MM}** appears under *Att bokföra* for every unbooked month **before** the current one (`fSkattMonthsAwaitingBooking` skips the running month, so a month's card only shows from the 1st of the month after it - on the demo, where 2026-01 … 2026-09 are already booked, nothing before 2026-11-01); untick and reload → `ensureAutoFSkattBookings` books the same months on load |
| Lön (owner) | `/bokforing/lon` → **Lägg upp lönen** (`#lon-namn`, `#lon-pnr` valid 12-digit pnr, `#lon-belopp`) → **Bokför lön {månad}** → row in *Lönekörningar* → open month → lönespecifikation → **Hämta deklarationsfil (XML)** on the AGI card |
| Stäng en månad | `/bokforing/periodstangning` → *Nästa period att stänga* → **Stäng januari 2026** → **Ja, stäng perioden** → badge *Till och med 2026-01-31*; `/bokforing/verifikationer/nytt` now has `min` = 2026-02-01 |
| Omvänd byggmoms | `/kunder/cust-nord` → tick **Omvänd byggmoms** → Ekonomi → **Ny faktura** for Nord Studio AB → lines forced to 0 %, note *Omvänd byggmoms.* → document totals read *Moms – omvänd byggmoms* + section **Omvänd byggmoms** |
| Deklarationsfil / arkiv | `/bokforing/moms` → open *Visa underlag* (`<details>`) on a non-ongoing period → **Hämta deklarationsfil (eSKD)** (`momsdeklaration-2026-K2.xml`); `/api/bokforing/export?typ=arkiv&ar=2026` → zip with `LÄSMIG.txt` + `underlag/register.csv` |
| Räkenskapsår (set / view) | `/installningar?flik=foretag` → **Räkenskapsår** card → click `#installningar-rakenskapsar-slut` → *Nästa månad* ×6 → **30** (2027-06-30; keeps the seed's 2026 verifications inside the year) → **Spara räkenskapsår** → *Sparat*, line *2026/2027: 1 januari 2026–30 juni 2027* → `/bokforing/huvudbok` subtitle *… 2026-01-01 till 2027-06-30 …*, **Exportera CSV** `ar=2026%2F2027`, still no chips → back on the card **Skapa nästa år** → list *2026/2027* + *2027/2028 · 1 juli 2027–30 juni 2028*, button gone → Huvudbok / Resultat / Saldobalans / Balans / Moms / Bokslut show the two chips (`?ar=2026%2F2027`, `?ar=2027%2F2028`); `/bokforing/bokslut?ar=2027%2F2028` → *Bokslut 2027/2028*; `?ar=1999` falls back to the current year. Clicked through on JSON dev 2026-09-08; Återställ demo undoes it |
| Enable Samarbeta | `/installningar?flik=funktioner` → Aktivera Samarbeta |
| Grossistbeställning (`/demo` on driva-alpha or JSON dev) | `/installningar?flik=funktioner` → **Aktivera** Grossistbeställningar → lands on **Grossister** with *Demo-grossisten* (*Dina priser uppdaterades …*) → `/uppdrag/job-kok` → **Lägg till** (`[data-job-add-entry]`) → **Material** (`[data-job-add-material]`) → **Sök och beställ** (`[data-add-material-choice="Sök och beställ"]`) → type `kabel` in `[data-wholesaler-search]` → **Lägg i varukorg** → **Visa varukorg (1)** → **Granska beställning** (`[data-wholesaler-mail-preview]` shows `order@demo-grossisten.example`, `DEMO-4711`, `bestallning-FV-1001.pdf`) → **Skicka beställning** → *Inget mejl lämnade Ferva* → **Klar** → job section *Materialbeställningar* lists `FV-1001 · Demo-grossisten` → `/bokforing/underlag?visning=alla&ekonomiska=alla` (untick **Bara ekonomiska dokument**; orderbekräftelser are hidden from the default list since [PR #137](https://github.com/elialm94/driva/pull/137)) has *Orderbekräftelse Demo-grossisten · FV-1001* linked to the order (`[data-inbox-linked-order]`). **Stäng av** afterwards: the section stays, the search is gone |
